DOCUMENT STATUS
Architecture         : SPECIFIED / SEALED for the declared design boundary
Topology             : FROZEN — exactly 50 numbered modules; no Component 51
Semantic language    : GSL v45.1 — normative current semantic version
Document             : LATEST-ONLY CONSOLIDATED DESIGN
Formal proof          : PENDING unless a specific obligation is independently discharged
Implementation        : PENDING overall; profile-specific prototypes do not imply completion
Deployment            : PENDING overall
Production validation : PENDING unless independently established
Resume key            : GARDEN-2026-08-21-CONSOLIDATED-R2
Visibility            : PUBLIC CANONICAL DESIGN — private-person overlays excluded

DOCUMENT SCOPE
This is the complete current Garden design. It contains the full 50-module specification and the current
semantic, theory-to-reality, safety, finance, Human Sovereignty over AI, research, security, assurance,
audit, data-lifecycle, legal, and adaptive-computation profiles. Change-log tables, defect-number tables,
superseded names, and private-person overlays are not part of this public master. Immutable operational
history remains a runtime requirement; document-edit history is maintained outside the normative design.

NORMATIVE PRECEDENCE INSIDE THIS DOCUMENT
  Constitution and immutable rights
    > K0P / hardware trust constraints where applicable
    > current canonical registries and explicit invariants
    > current module/system/theory/profile specifications
    > explicitly retained compatibility interfaces
    > examples, reference defaults, benchmark targets, hypotheses, and open research.

If any descriptive paragraph conflicts with a current canonical registry, the registry governs. No
example, optimization score, vote, model output, or profile
may weaken Constitution, K0P, proof boundaries, authority boundaries, privacy, consent, or truth/stage
discipline.

TRUTH / STAGE DISCIPLINE
MAPPED ≠ PROVEN ≠ IMPLEMENTED ≠ VALIDATED ≠ CERTIFIED.
Claim ≠ Fact. Hypothesis ≠ Evidence. Prediction ≠ Evidence. Retrieval ≠ Validation.
Observation ≠ Evidence ≠ Claim ≠ Knowledge. Specification ≠ reality.
Intent ≠ Authority. Capability ≠ Permission. Recommendation ≠ Authorization.
Allocated ≠ Committed ≠ Spent. Expected return ≠ realized return ≠ guaranteed return.
Majority vote ≠ Proof. Optimization ≠ Permission. Convergence ≠ truth.
UNKNOWN ≠ FALSE. Requested ≠ Delivered. Self-improvement ≠ Self-certification.
DO_NOTHING may be a successful stability result.

TOPOLOGY RULE
Every theory, framework, algebra, process, system, substrate, research profile, economic profile,
medical profile, quantum profile, and resource-allocation mechanism in this document maps into the
frozen 50 modules. None is Component 51.

DOCUMENT ORGANIZATION AND SINGLE-SOURCE RULE
1. Part I is the navigation map and canonical display-name list.
2. Part II defines the semantic kernel; Part III defines the one universal process/change architecture.
3. Part V is the sole normative home of named invariants, rights and proof obligations. Module and profile
   sections list invariant identifiers and operational contracts but do not redefine those invariants.
4. Part VI defines module responsibilities. Part VII defines theories and their authoritative bindings.
5. Parts VIII–XV define cross-module implementation and research profiles; a profile never creates a module.
6. Part XII is the sole namespace/index and machine-facing registry for identifiers, versions and schemas;
   the semantic and invariant definitions it indexes remain authoritative in Parts II and V respectively.
7. Generated views—module mappings, theory mappings, compliance views and audit views—must be regenerated
   from their authoritative registries. A copied prose table never overrides its source registry.
8. Private-person extensions are separate artifacts. They cannot alter the public design without an
   independently authorized, non-identifying public change that passes the ordinary Garden process.

CANONICAL NAMING RULES
1. Module 05 Agent is the AI agent acting for a user. GSL AGENCY is the semantic object for
   actor/authority/delegation/accountability. Agent ≠ Agency.
2. SRT, VSM, TFT, RPM, PPM, FET and SCT are abbreviations of the seven current theory names. A former,
   translated or near-duplicate name is not printed beside the canonical name.
3. “Architecture” and “deterministic” remain valid technical/descriptive words. They are omitted
   only where the canonical proper name does not contain them.
4. Acronyms do not create additional theories, systems, profiles, or modules.

CURRENT SEMANTIC BASELINE
GSL v45.1 has ten Core Objects including CLAIM and exactly 24 Core Relations. External material enters
the current typed repository only through explicit import, validation and provenance gates.

PART II — GSL v45.1 SEMANTIC FOUNDATION
====================================================================================================
2.1 CANONICAL ONTOLOGY

Six Pillars
  Existence · Change · Agency · Law · Value · Frame

Ten Core Objects
  TIME     = instant/interval, ordering, validity and actual/simulation branch.
  SPACE    = coordinates/region/reference system/parent space.
  THING    = universal semantic root with stable identity/name/type/version.
  EVENT    = occurrence/observation with source/time/epistemic state.
  ACTION   = intentional state-changing operation with actor/preconditions/status.
  AGENCY   = actor/authority/capability/delegation/accountability semantics.
  RULE     = tiered condition → conclusion/constraint.
  VALUE    = semantic Fact | Preference | Norm. Representation is orthogonal; VALUE is not universally a Decimal.
  CONTEXT  = frame containing scope, risk class, active rules, environment and interpretation conditions.
  CLAIM    = assertion with subject/predicate/object, evidence references, disposition, confidence?,
             provenance, validity_scope, and version. Claim is the primary unit of epistemic state.

Claim = {
  id, subject, predicate, object, asserted_by, context, timestamp,
  basis, disposition, confidence?, evidence_refs, provenance, validity_scope, version
}

THING subtype registry
  ENTITY   = Person | Organization | Community | Locality | Device | Infrastructure | Garden | extensible typed descendants.
  ARTIFACT = Document | Dataset | Model | Software | Media | Contract | Credential | Proof | WorkPackage |
             TheoryArtifact | LanguageArtifact | Observation | extensible typed descendants.

Invariant references: ONT-001..004.

Module 05 Agent means the AI agent acting for the user. GSL AGENCY is the semantic/ontological object;
Agent ≠ Agency.

2.2 CURRENT 24 CORE RELATIONS

Foundational (10)
  identifies, causes, governs, values, frames, acts, obeys, assesses, contextualizes, controls

Practical / lineage (14)
  partOf, dependsOn, owns, delegates, references, derivedFrom, equivalentTo, contradicts,
  supports, blocks, hasHypothesis, supersedes, conflictsWith, originatesFrom

Key algebraic rules
  equivalentTo     symmetric + transitive; cycles permitted.
  partOf            transitive + antisymmetric; cycles forbidden.
  dependsOn         transitive + antisymmetric; cycles forbidden.
  derivedFrom       directed lineage; cycles forbidden.
  controls          authority/control edge; cycles forbidden.
  supersedes        directional/version aware; cycles forbidden; predecessor remains immutable.
  originatesFrom    directed provenance; cycles forbidden.
  contradicts       symmetric semantic opposition; cycles permitted.
  conflictsWith     symmetric declared conflict; cycles permitted, explicit resolution required.
  causes            feedback cycles permitted.
  references        cycles permitted.
  delegates         capability(delegate) ⊆ capability(principal).
  supports          does not by itself promote epistemic status.

Canonical composition candidates include:
  delegates ∘ acts → acts
  owns ∘ controls → derivedControl
  supports ∘ supports → candidate indirect support; it becomes supports only through an explicit valid warrant.
  causes ∘ causes → causalPath; it becomes a causal Claim only under a valid causal model, compatible
                    context, and declared assumptions.
  hasHypothesis ∘ assesses → evaluatedHypothesis
All undeclared normative compositions raise CompositionError or require an explicit domain rule.
Historical labels composesWith / requires / provides remain lineage/domain vocabulary but are not in the current core 24.

Invariant references: REL-ALG-001..002.

2.3 TWELVE UNIVERSAL SEMANTIC PRIMITIVES

STATUS: NORMATIVE — CROSS-CUTTING ARCHITECTURAL ALGEBRAS

These twelve primitives are reusable semantic algebras operating across the 10 Core Objects. They do
not replace the Core Objects; they govern how typed payloads are observed, transformed, authorized,
stored, connected, evaluated, and executed. They map to existing modules and create no new component.

1. STATE
   S(t) + Event + Action → S(t+1).

   DeterministicResolutionContext = {
     causal_clocks, merge_fn, membership_epoch, event_order, logical_time,
     conflict_policy, deterministic_seed?, environment_commitment, design_epoch
   }

   Determinism is conditional on the declared context and inputs. Missing nondeterministic physical or
   external inputs are modeled explicitly; they are not silently replaced by a seed.

2. OBSERVATION
   Observation ≠ Evidence ≠ Claim ≠ Knowledge.

   ObservationSource = SensorAttested(SensorAttestationRef) | Human | Document | System | Model.
   A sensor attestation binds device identity, calibration, time, configuration, integrity, and
   provenance.

3. AUTHORITY
   Entity → Identity → Credential → Role → Capability → Authority → Action.

   Capability does not self-create authority. Delegation is scoped, expiring, revocable, auditable,
   and no broader than the principal’s legitimate authority.

4. RESOURCE
   Request → Reserve → Allocate → Consume → Release → Reconcile.

   Forecast Resource ≠ Receivable Resource ≠ Liquid Resource ≠ Allocated Resource ≠
   Committed Resource ≠ Spent Resource ≠ Recovered Resource.

5. ADDRESS
   garden://{namespace}/{type}/{instance}/{version} + content hash.

   Human-readable address, semantic version, validity scope, and immutable content identity are
   separately represented.

6. PROVENANCE
   Source + DirectedLineageGraph + cryptographic roots/anchors.

   Provenance is a typed graph, not a single source string. It supports backward explanation,
   forward impact analysis, privacy-aware disclosure, and cryptographic/institutional attestation.

7. CAUSALITY
   C0 Correlation | C1 Temporal | C2 Mechanistic | C3 Intervention | C4 Counterfactual.

   No higher level is inferred from a lower one without the declared causal model, assumptions,
   evidence, identification conditions, and uncertainty.

8. DEPENDENCY
   Build DAG | Runtime dependency | Semantic dependency.

   Cycles require an explicit permitted semantics, fixed-point/feedback interpretation where
   applicable, and termination/liveness proof. Undeclared cycles are rejected.

9. CONFLICT
   Argument | Authority | Governance | CRDT | HardcodedFallback.

   Conflict is typed and auditable. SAME, REFINEMENT, COMPATIBLE, CONTEXT_DIFFERENT, APPROXIMATION,
   TRUE_CONTRADICTION, and UNRESOLVED are distinct results. UNRESOLVED is valid.

10. UNCERTAINTY
    Epistemic (potentially reducible) versus aleatoric (model-irreducible within the declared model).

    Neither uncertainty type weakens K0P or Constitution. Representation follows the Mathematical
    Representation Policy; interval, distribution, vector, tensor, symbolic, and domain-specific
    forms are not silently collapsed to one confidence number.

11. DECISION
    Recommendation | Authorization | Commitment | Execution.

    The stages are not interchangeable. Recommendation ≠ Authorization. Authorization without a
    valid commitment/execution path is not execution. Execution does not prove the recommendation
    was epistemically correct.

12. VALIDITY
    Version = semantic revision identity. Validity = time/context/scope.

    Version and validity are orthogonal. The same version may have different validity scopes; a new
    version does not automatically invalidate all predecessor uses.

RELATION TO THE 10 CORE OBJECTS
The 10 Core Objects (TIME, SPACE, THING, EVENT, ACTION, AGENCY, RULE, VALUE, CONTEXT, CLAIM) are the atomic
payload types. The 12 Primitives are operational algebra families governing how those payloads are
created, observed, transformed, stored, authorized, allocated, and reasoned about. Every Garden
operation invokes one or more primitive families through UPA/GTF profiles.

Invariant references: PRIM-001..003.

2.4 PRIMARY STRUCTURAL ARTIFACT TYPES
The current GSL v45.1 structural layer comprises Evidence, ProofTrace, Resolution, and Explanation.
CLAIM is a Core Object in §2.1. The following schemas are the current authoritative structural registry:

Evidence = {
  id, supports_or_refutes, source, method, integrity, calibration?,
  independence, uncertainty, timestamp, scope, attestation_refs, provenance
}
ProofTrace = {
  id, obligation_refs, premises, proof_steps_or_term, assumptions,
  verifier, verifier_version, status, counterexamples?, signature, provenance
}
Resolution = {
  id, conflict_or_question, candidates, selected_or_unresolved,
  ResolutionVector, rationale, authority, evidence_refs, proof_refs, provenance
}
Explanation = {
  id, target_claim_or_decision, audience, language, depth,
  meaning, assumptions, evidence, uncertainty, alternatives,
  what_would_change_conclusion, provenance
}

Invariant references: STR-001..003.

2.5 MASTER / LANGUAGE INVARIANTS

Invariant references: G-001..010, LANG-001..009, GSL-STAT-001..011.
No separate “eight GSL axioms” set is active in the current design.

2.6 K-INT GLOBAL EPISTEMIC INVARIANTS
Canonical definitions: Part V.
Invariant references: K-INT-001..018.

2.7 EFFECT / TYPE / EPISTEMIC SYSTEM
Closed effect dimensions:
  Computational, Safety, Auth/Authority, Privacy, Temporal, Resource, Uncertainty, Continuity, Optimization.
Conflicting effects produce INFEASIBLE rather than arbitrary compromise. Effect ordering is compile-time enforced.

Epistemic basis:
  Formal | Empirical | Statistical | Testimonial | Inference | Hypothesis.
Disposition:
  PROVEN | VERIFIED | SUPPORTED | UNVERIFIED | RETRACTED | CONTRADICTED | UNKNOWN.
UNKNOWN subtypes:
  CONFLICT_UNKNOWN | EVIDENCE_UNKNOWN | UNDECIDABLE | RESOURCE_UNKNOWN | STALE_UNKNOWN |
  MODEL_INVALID_UNKNOWN | SCOPE_UNDEFINED_UNKNOWN.
UNKNOWN ≠ NOT_APPLICABLE ≠ FALSE ≠ SAFE.

Evidence merge considers disposition, strength, independence, method, scope, freshness and provenance. Correlated agreement is not independent confirmation. SCC/circular-support analysis prevents a mutually supporting cycle with no independent grounding from self-promoting.

2.8 CORE ALGEBRAS
Transition = { state_before, trigger, pre, op, auth, effects, evidence, state_after, post, event }
Contract   = { requires, guarantees, invariants, effects, auth, evidence, proof_obligations }
Outcome    = Success | Rejected | Infeasible | Timeout | Unknown | Conflict | Failure | Quarantined | Escalated
Epistemic  = { observation/evidence/formal/empirical/application status, independence, scope, freshness }
Temporal   = { ValidAt, ExpiredAt, SupersededBy, RevokedAt, StaleAt }

Formal semantics are represented through three mutually checked views:
  Op  : operational semantics (state, expression) → (state', value)
  Den : denotational mapping Type → mathematical object (set/probability/Hilbert/etc.)
  Ax  : axiomatic/Hoare-style discrete, probabilistic and continuous contracts.
Target D1: operational, denotational and axiomatic semantics agree on the declared fragment; proof discharge remains pending where not established.

2.9 MATHEMATICAL REPRESENTATION POLICY — CURRENT NORMALIZED FORM
Core rule: representation ≠ semantic meaning.

Represented<T> = {
  semantic_type: T,
  representation: Linear | Log | LogOdds | LogEvidence | LogRisk | Interval |
                  Distribution | Vector | Tensor | Symbolic | DomainSpecific,
  payload,
  units?, scale?, assumptions, uncertainty?, calibration?, provenance
}
LogOdds     = semantic probability representation only.
LogEvidence = evidence-strength representation; it does not become probability unless a valid model maps it.
LogRisk     = domain risk representation with declared scale/model.

Invariant references: GSL-MATH-001..005.

2.10 GSL COMPILATION / GRAMMAR
Human / External Source
  → GSL semantic parsing
  → AST
  → Type / Effect / Dependency analysis
  → CIR
  → Backend / code generation
  → Binary / runtime representation
  → Verity / Safety / Policy gates
  → Execution

Compiler triad: independently implemented Haskell / Rust / OCaml frontends/backends MUST agree on CIR for the declared consensus profile; divergence → HALT/INVESTIGATE.

Recovered grammar core:
Program ::= Module
Module ::= 'module' ID (':' ID)? '{' Import* Stmt* '}'
Import ::= 'import' ID ('as' ID)?
Stmt ::= Decl | Def | Rule | Assert | Query | Eval | Contract | Proof | Patch
Decl ::= 'entity' ID Extends? '{' Field* '}'
       | 'artifact' ID Extends? '{' Field* '}'
       | 'claim' ID '{' Field* '}'
       | 'pattern' ID '{' Field* '}'
       | 'relation' ID '{' Field* '}'
       | 'role' ID '{' Field* '}'
       | 'evidence' ID '{' Field* '}'
Def ::= 'def' ID '(' Param* ')' '->' Type ('effects' '[' Effect* ']')? '{' Expr '}'
Rule ::= 'rule' ID ':' Condition '=>' Effect
Contract ::= 'contract' ID '{' Pre* Post* Invariant* ProofObligation* '}'
Proof ::= 'proof' ID ':' ProofType '{' Obligation* '}'
PatchDecl ::= 'patch' ID '{' PatchItem* '}'
PatchItem ::= 'applies_to' ID ';' | 'before' Expr ';' | 'after' Expr ';' | 'proof' ProofBody ';' | 'rollback' Expr ';'

This is the normative grammar core; §12.4 supplies the complete current compiler-facing grammar.
Unlisted or obsolete syntax is not accepted without an explicit offline migration rule.

2.11 CUBE × RING — EXPANDED CURRENT MODEL

GardenState(t) ∈ Cube × Ring.

The construction is a semantic/state representation and not an additional module, physical claim, or
claim that all reality is literally a cube or algebraic ring.

Cube — simultaneous structured dimensions
  C1 Existence/Thing     : entities, artifacts, identity, composition, state.
  C2 Space/Location      : coordinate frame, region, topology, containment, adjacency.
  C3 Agency/Authority    : actors, roles, capability, delegation, accountability.
  C4 Rule/Constraint     : Constitution, law, policy, contract, invariant, proof obligation.
  C5 Value/Epistemic     : fact/preference/norm, objective, evidence, uncertainty, status.
  C6 Context/Scope       : domain, locale, environment, risk, branch, DesignEpoch, validity.

Ring — ordered continuity and change
  R1 Time               : instants, intervals, clocks, logical/causal order, expiry.
  R2 Event              : observed occurrence and event provenance.
  R3 Action             : authorized operation and declared effects.
  R4 Transition         : pre-state, delta, post-state, obligations, rollback/compensation.
  R5 Recurrence         : schedules, periodic review, bounded loops, fixed points.
  R6 Causal progression : mechanisms, interventions, counterfactual branches, descendant effects.

Core operators
  project(Cube, dimensions, scope)       → scoped state view
  join(view_a, view_b, conflict_policy)  → merged view | CONFLICT | UNRESOLVED
  advance(state, transition, DRC)        → next state + receipts
  replay(genesis, commits, environment)  → reconstructed state | mismatch
  branch(state, delta)                   → immutable alternative history
  compare(trajectories, objectives)      → dominance | incomparability | unresolved
  fold(events, transition_fn)            → bounded accumulated state
  trace(effect, lineage_graph)           → causes, dependencies, authority, provenance

Temporal/causal relationship
  S[n+1] = T[n](S[n]) is the discrete ledger view.
  For continuous domains, a declared flow Φ over an interval may update state, but its sampled events,
  assumptions, solver/calibration configuration, and approximation error are bound into the Ring.
  Temporal precedence is not causality; causal promotion follows the C0–C4 ladder.

Invariant references: CXR-001..007.

2.12 Φ BRIDGE — PERCEPTION → GSL
Normalized operational profile:
Raw sensory input
  → AttestedObservation(sensor/device/human identity, calibration, time, environment, integrity, provenance)
  → immutable raw or declared loss-bounded observation retention
  → deadline-class routing
      FAST_SAFETY: bounded local features feed K0P without waiting for rich multimodal fusion
      EPISTEMIC: dependency-aware multimodal fusion under backpressure/resource/deadline budgets
  → feature and hypothesis extraction
  → candidate subject/predicate/object bindings
  → alternative hypotheses + unmodeled remainder
  → localization/synchronization where spatial
  → uncertainty propagation + calibration check
  → provenance/attestation binding
  → ClaimCandidate
  → theory/model applicability routing
  → VALIDATE | UNKNOWN | REJECT/QUARANTINE
  → Knowledge ingress only under K-INT rules.

Phi(Sensory_Input) → ClaimCandidate {
  subject, predicate, object,
  confidence ∈ [0,1]?,
  alternatives: [{hypothesis, confidence}],
  unmodeled_mass,
  calibration_refs, timestamps, localization?, uncertainty,
  provenance
}
Σ alternative confidence ≤ 1; remainder is explicit unmodeled alternative space. A perception model may propose a Claim; it may not manufacture validated Evidence.
Fusion deadline expiry returns PARTIAL/UNKNOWN with a receipt; it never delays the independent L0 safety
path or presents a partial fused frame as complete.

2.13 METACOGNITION
Observation → Pattern → Hypothesis → Principle → Theory → Framework.
Promotion requires explicit validation at each boundary. Survival under falsification ≠ proof.

2.14 BIOMIMETIC & NATURAL DESIGN PATTERNS

STATUS: NORMATIVE — ARCHITECTURAL DESIGN-REVIEW PRINCIPLES
PURPOSE: Record the natural-system patterns that motivate and test Garden mechanisms.

Garden is engineered, but many of its architectural choices deliberately reuse patterns that have
demonstrated persistence, adaptation, coordination, and repair in natural systems. The mapping is
analogical: biology inspires design obligations but does not prove that a Garden implementation is
safe, optimal, alive, or empirically valid.

| Natural Principle | Garden Mapping | Required Mechanism |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Homeostasis | Safety (9), Runtime (13), Economy (3) | K0P boundaries, feedback regulation, resource/liquidity equilibrium, bounded degradation and recovery. |
| Immune System | Security (22), Compliance (34), Evolution (33) | Anomaly detection, quarantine, incident response, memory, repair and adaptive defense without autoimmune/self-confirming behavior. |
| Redundancy | Fabric(18), Knowledge(10), Verity(8) | Replication, independent verification, consensus where applicable, Byzantine/common-mode analysis and immutable logs. |
| Stigmergy | Search (31), Events (45), Evolution (33) | Indirect coordination through typed environmental/event traces, caches, reinforcement/decay and provenance. |
| Swarm Intelligence | Agent (5), Governance (30), Reasoning (50) | Distributed proposals and quorum/argumentation within authority; collective agreement cannot manufacture truth or rights. |
| Epigenetics | Evolution (33), Context, Reasoning (50) | Versioned context-dependent expression/configuration and inheritance without silently changing the underlying canonical semantics. |
| Neural Plasticity | Knowledge (10), Reasoning (50), Research (49) | Continuous revalidation, calibrated learning, dependency-aware adaptation and reversible/reviewable reconfiguration. |
| Autopoiesis | Evolution (33), Continuity (39), System Integrity | Bounded self-maintenance, repair and recursive regulation while trust roots remain externally protected. |

Invariant references: BIO-001..004.

These principles are not optional as review questions. Their concrete implementation remains
domain-specific and must pass ordinary applicability, proof, evidence, safety and conformance gates.

2.15 THE DOUBLE HELIX PATTERN — INTERTWINED DUALITY AND SELF-CORRECTION

STATUS: NORMATIVE — CROSS-CUTTING ARCHITECTURAL PRINCIPLE
PURPOSE: Provide a unifying conceptual pattern for complementary, mutually checking processes.

The Double Helix is a structural pattern present throughout Garden. It is a metaphor/formal-design
heuristic, not a claim that Garden literally has DNA or that every pair is symmetric.

| Strand A | Strand B | Governing Relation |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| User-facing sovereign layer | Garden system | Interacting and co-evolving, while the user/person remains a rights-holder rather than a system component. |
| Knowledge (10) | Reasoning (50) | Knowledge stores and versions epistemic state; Reasoning transforms/tests it without manufacturing Evidence. |
| Evolution(33) | Verity(8) | Evolution proposes/repairs; Verity verifies declared formal obligations; neither self-certifies. |
| Constitution (11) | Law (12) | Constitutional rights constrain legal interpretation and evolution. |
| Safety (9) | Emergency (38) | Safety enforces hard boundaries; Emergency uses a separately authorized bounded path that may bypass ordinary policy/governance but never Constitution/K0P. |
| Time (20) | Events (45) | Time orders and scopes; Events record occurrences with clock/provenance bindings. |
| Intent (4) | Policy (48) | Intent expresses desired outcomes; Policy constrains legitimate action without becoming truth. |

Formalization candidates include dual objects/pairs, adjunctions where mappings genuinely satisfy the
required laws, product structures, and the Cube × Ring recurrence model. Dual numbers may represent
local sensitivity in applicable mathematical profiles. These are candidate formalisms, not automatically
the formal underpinning of every pair; each use requires an explicit mapping and proof/validation.

Invariant references: DHX-001..004.

The Double Helix pattern explains why Garden components are interwoven, complementary and
self-correcting while preserving typed boundaries and independent verification.

PART III — UNIVERSAL PROCESS, TRANSITION AND STATE ARCHITECTURE
====================================================================================================
3.1 UNIVERSAL PROCESS ALGEBRA (UPA)

Purpose: replace many duplicated, hard-coded linear lifecycle engines with one typed compositional process semantics. Incident, research, prediction, verification, conformance, deployment, transition, evolution, legal CI/CD, epistemic revalidation and multi-agent review are UPA profiles—not separate universal process architectures.

UniversalProcess = {
  process_id, profile, inputs, current_state,
  operators, preconditions, postconditions, invariants,
  authority, effects, resources, epistemic_bindings,
  dependency_refs, evidence_refs, provenance,
  termination_contract, rollback_or_compensation,
  outputs, status
}

Operators:
  SEQUENCE(A,B)       ordered composition.
  BRANCH(c,A,B)       typed conditional path.
  PARALLEL({P_i})     concurrent work with declared join semantics.
  COMPOSE(A,B)        contract/effect/authority-aware composition.
  RETRY(P,bound)      bounded retry; never unbounded hidden recursion.
  ROLLBACK(P)         revert reversible state using certified pre-state.
  COMPENSATE(P)       recovery action where literal rollback is physically impossible.
  RECONSTRUCT(x)      rebuild a causal/provenance path only from content-addressed events whose original
                      signatures, hardware attestations, membership epoch and environment bindings are
                      re-verified. Unverified records remain SIMULATED/UNTRUSTED and cannot recreate authority.
  ITERATE(P,k)        bounded iteration.
  RECURSE(P,contract) recursively invoke only under TerminationContract.
  TERMINATE(reason)   close with explicit reason/status.
  DO_NOTHING          first-class candidate/action.

Current normalized P0–P11 lifecycle used by this master:
  P0  RECEIVE / PROPOSE
  P1  UNDERSTAND / SEMANTIC-NORMALIZE
  P2  DISCOVER / APPLICABILITY
  P3  IMPACT / DEPENDENCIES
  P4  EPISTEMICS / EVIDENCE
  P5  PREDICT CONSEQUENCES
  P6  RESOURCE PLAN / ADMISSION
  P7  ASSURE / VERIFY / TEST
  P8  AUTHORITY / GOVERNANCE / CONSENT
  P9  PREPARE / EXECUTE / COMMIT
  P10 OBSERVE / VALIDATE / RECONSTRUCT
  P11 LEARN / RECURSE / TERMINATE
Conditional paths may skip inapplicable stages only through declared contracts; a skipped stage is not silently considered passed.

Invariant references: UPA-001..015.

ProcessProfile = {
  profile_id, domain, universal_process_ref,
  stage_projection, operator_bindings, skipped_stage_justifications,
  domain_preconditions, invariants, authority, effects, resources,
  failure_and_recovery_mapping, termination_contract,
  receipt_schema, version, provenance
}

3.2 GARDEN TRANSITION FABRIC (GTF)
GTF is the high-assurance UPA profile for consequential Garden state changes.

Proposed Change
  ↓
TransitionContract
  ↓
Understand exact semantic delta
  ↓
Impact / dependency closure
  ↓
Epistemic state / evidence
  ↓
Prediction consequences
  ↓
Resource requirements
  ↓
ObligationGraph / assurance
  ↓
Authority / Governance / Consent
  ↓
PREPARE
  ↓
VALIDATE
  ↓
ATOMIC COMMIT
  ↓
Proof-Carrying State
  ↓
Post-commit monitor / rollback-or-compensation / learn

TransitionContract = {
  transition_id, base_state_ref, proposed_delta,
  purpose, expected_benefit, affected_components,
  affected_invariants, dependencies,
  epistemic_requirements, prediction_requirements,
  resource_requirements, authority_requirements,
  governance_or_consent_requirements,
  obligation_graph_ref, rollback_or_compensation,
  environment_commitment, design_binding, provenance
}

Formal change-management human projection:
  Propose → Understand → Impact → Verify → Approve → Commit → Monitor → Rollback/Compensate.
UNDERSTAND is mandatory because an ill-specified change cannot have a trustworthy impact closure.

3.3 CANONICAL GARDEN STATE LEDGER
S[n+1] = T[n](S[n]).
Every canonical state transition has a stable transition identity, inputs, bindings, evidence/proofs, authority and provenance sufficient for deterministic reconstruction within declared external/environmental assumptions.
The ledger is a causal semantic state lineage—not merely a blockchain or append-only text log.

CanonicalStateRecord = {
  state_id, parent_state_id?, transition_id?,
  state_root, DesignEpoch, KnowledgeSnapshot,
  EnvironmentCommitment, proof_carrying_state_ref,
  timestamp, provenance_root
}

3.4 PROOF-CARRYING CHANGE DELTA (PCCD)
A change carries the obligations created/invalidated by its own semantic delta instead of forcing full-context reconstruction every time.
PCCD = { delta, affected_objects, invalidated_bindings, new_obligations, reusable_results, proof_refs, evidence_refs, provenance }
PCCD is not itself proof; it is the typed carrier telling Verity/conformance what must be proved/rechecked.

3.5 OBLIGATION GRAPH
ObligationGraph nodes/edges cover affected:
  invariants · proof obligations · evidence requirements · dependencies · predictions · resources · safety checks · security checks · governance/authority · human consent · post-commit validation.
Edges encode dependsOn / invalidates / requires / supports / blocks / scope propagation.
Impact closure traverses this graph; Dependency-Local Reverification operates on the affected closure.

3.6 PROOF-CARRYING STATE
ProofCarryingState = {
  State,
  DesignBinding,
  Obligations,
  Evidence,
  Certificates,
  EnvironmentCommitment,
  unresolved_items,
  provenance
}
State ≠ CertifiedState. A stored/deployed state does not become valid merely because it exists.

3.7 TRANSACTIONAL / REVERSIBLE EVOLUTION
Normal high-impact pattern: PREPARE → ISOLATE → VALIDATE → COMMIT → MONITOR.
Failure before commit → ABORT. Failure after reversible commit → ROLLBACK. Irreversible physical effects require a pre-authorized Compensation/Recovery plan; “rollback” must not falsely promise physical reversibility.

3.8 GARDEN CHANGE CARD — HUMAN VIEW OF A TRANSITION CONTRACT
What changes? · Why? · Affected modules? · Affected invariants? · Expected benefit? · Risks? · Uncertainty? · Evidence? · Predictions? · Verification? · Authority/consent? · Dissent? · Rollback/compensation? · Current status?
GardenChangeCard is a projection, not a separate truth source; it is generated from the canonical TransitionContract/ObligationGraph.

PART IV — CONTINUOUS EPISTEMIC REVALIDATION AND KNOWLEDGE MAINTENANCE
====================================================================================================
4.1 PRINCIPLE
Stored knowledge is never permanently trusted solely because it passed once. Revalidation is triggered by time, new evidence, changed dependencies, changed environment/model, contradiction, high-impact use or explicit audit.

RevalidationPriority(k,t) is domain-policy controlled and must increase with relevant staleness/exposure rather than rewarding old unreviewed knowledge with less scrutiny. A normalized monotone age factor used by this consolidation is:
  AgeFactor_d(a) = 1 + age_weight[d] · ln(1 + a / τ_age[d])
for a≥0, age_weight[d]≥0, τ_age[d]>0.
The formula records the current direction: older un-revalidated knowledge rises, not falls, in review pressure. Domain criticality, change rate, uncertainty and dependency impact may dominate age.

A priority implementation may combine normalized factors such as:
  Priority ∝ Uncertainty × Importance × DependencyImpact × ChangeExposure × AgeFactor × Criticality
subject to bounded, transparent normalization; zeroing one factor must not accidentally suppress a mandatory critical review.

4.2 OUTCOMES
CONFIRMED | STRENGTHENED | WEAKENED | REFINED | SUPERSEDED | CONTRADICTED | STALE | UNKNOWN | INCONCLUSIVE.
Revalidation changes interpretation/status while preserving the original historical artifact.

4.3 EPI-007…EPI-012 / KNW-016…018
Canonical definitions: Part V.
Invariant references: EPI-007..012, KNW-016..018.

4.4 DAILY EPISTEMIC SWEEP + SATURATION MODEL
Cheap Sweep: scan metadata/dependency/status/freshness across the declared knowledge universe.
Deep Revalidation: spend bounded compute on the highest-priority eligible subset.
Adaptive cadence: daily / weekly / monthly or domain-specific; safety-critical domains have a declared minimum cadence even when estimated change rate is low.
Budgets: compute, memory, storage, bandwidth, time and money are explicit; exhaustion yields DEFERRED/RESOURCE_UNKNOWN, not “validated”.
Scheduling uses protected critical lanes, per-domain quotas, aging/round-robin fairness, deduplication
and retry backoff. A repeatedly deferred high-cost item retains importance but cannot occupy every next
slot; its next eligibility time and required resource class are explicit. Critical lower-cost work in
other domains therefore cannot be starved by one permanently infeasible item.

SweepRecord = {
  sweep_id, scope, started_at, finished_at,
  metadata_items_scanned, candidates_ranked,
  deep_revalidations_attempted, completed, deferred,
  anomalies, contradictions, stale_bindings,
  resource_usage, budget, saturation_profile_ref,
  DesignEpoch, KnowledgeSnapshot, provenance
}
SaturationProfile = {
  domain, eligible_items, revalidated_items,
  estimated_change_rate, unresolved_debt,
  priority_mass_processed, priority_mass_deferred,
  cadence, critical_minimum_cadence,
  trend, confidence, timestamp
}

Invariant references: EPI-013..018, SWP-001..004.

4.5 EPISTEMIC STABILITY CACHE (ESC)
ESC caches validated epistemic assessments only while KnowledgeSnapshot, EnvironmentCommitment, assumptions, source/dependency validity and DesignEpoch binding remain valid. Any relevant invalidation evicts/downgrades the entry before operational reuse.

4.6 THEORY STATUS + THEORY DEPENDENCY GRAPH
Normalized status vocabulary:
  ESTABLISHED | DERIVED | PROPOSED | EXPERIMENTAL | SPECULATIVE | REJECTED.
This status is separate from formal proof status and empirical validation status; mappings may be declared per theory.
TheoryDependencyGraph edges identify assumptions/derivations/reuse. Upstream change → affected downstream theories become REVALIDATION_REQUIRED; they are not automatically false.

PART V — FORMAL PROOF, SAFETY, CONSTITUTION AND TRUST ROOTS
====================================================================================================
5.1 P1–P24 PROOF OBLIGATION REGISTRY
P1   Invariant Preservation: Valid(G(t)) ∧ Transition ⇒ Valid(G(t+1)).
P2   Epistemic Non-Amplification: Status(output) ≤ Status(input) + AuthorizedInference.
P2-F Future Non-Fact: Prediction ≠ Evidence.
P3   Scope: no silent widening.
P4a  Hard Safety: K0P/Constitution violation ⇒ INFEASIBLE.
P4b  Soft Safety: optimization cannot override P4a.
P5   Termination: R=(Budget,Work,Obligations)∈N³ decreases lexicographically under the declared recursive procedure.
P5L  Liveness/Fairness: valid admitted tasks eventually make progress or receive explicit terminal/deferred status.
P6   Composition: pairwise + GlobalContract ⇒ composed system satisfies applicable contract. Full composition requires component proofs, interface proof, shared invariants, resource compatibility, temporal compatibility, failure composition and assumption compatibility.
P6l.0 GlobalContract Soundness.
P6l.1 Composition Proof.
P7a  Static Dependency Closure — compile-time.
P7b  Dynamic Dependency Gate — runtime authorization + resolution.
P8   Ontology Evolution — semantic compatibility/migration for existing valid programs.
P9   Rollback Integrity — reversible attempted state returns to declared pre-state without unaccounted side effects.
P10a–f Concurrency: race freedom, deadlock, livelock, fairness, typed consistency, convergence.
P11  Information Flow: no unauthorized leakage.
P12  Causal Soundness: C4 counterfactual claims require valid SCM + assumptions.
P13  Quantum Safety: calibration/drift bounds.
P14  Provenance: completeness within the declared boundary.
P15  Semantic Containment: generated subsystems start with zero authority except explicitly granted capability.
P16  Governance Scope Soundness: governance cannot weaken Trust Anchor through ordinary action.
P17  Evolution Proposal Throttling.
P18  Epistemic Default-Deny Soundness.
P19  Recovery Path Integrity.
P20  Admission-Critical Invariants must discharge before component boot/admission; noncritical failures block only their declared scope.
P21  Full Design Conformance Audit: every generated or modified deployable artifact is checked against
     the complete version-bound canonical design registry; every registry item receives an explicit
     ApplicabilityResult.
P22  Adversarial Review Survival: every deployable patch passes all applicable attacks in its declared,
     versioned adversarial plan with no unresolved fatal/critical finding. Unknown/untested attacks remain explicit.
P23  Recursive Patching Termination: automatic recursive patching terminates within maximum depth 3 and
     the declared P5 budget, or halts/escalates without deployment.
P24  Self-Audit Module Integrity: Self-Audit and its registry/patch/adversarial controllers require a
     frozen independent verifier and cannot solely certify changes to themselves.

Registry rule: every normative proof identifier resolves to exactly one current definition. References
such as bare P4 map to the applicable P4a/P4b context rather than defining another obligation.
P21…P24 are global Self-Audit/recursive-patch obligations; TRAIN uses separately qualified
TRAIN-P21…TRAIN-P24; Legal uses LEGAL-SMT-P25. Every identifier resolves through Part XII §12.1.

Qualified proof-profile registry
TRAIN-P21 Predictive-Barrier Safety: under declared dynamics, sensing/calibration bounds, horizon,
          solver tolerances and actuator model, an accepted command satisfies the applicable barrier
          condition or enters its declared safe response; simulation alone does not discharge it.
TRAIN-P22 Robust Safe-Set / Viability Containment: the declared conservative safe set accounts for
          disturbance/model error and admitted trajectories remain inside it for the proof horizon.
TRAIN-P23 Observer / Predictive-Control Bound: observer error, uncertainty propagation and robust control
          remain inside declared bounds without stale/incompatible calibration or model state.
TRAIN-P24 Assurance Composition / Small-Gain Stability: composed assurance/controllers satisfy declared
          interface, timing, gain and common-mode assumptions; component stability does not prove composition.
LEGAL-SMT-P25 For a declared finite predicate space and resource contract, enumeration either covers all
              combinations or returns TIMEOUT/INCOMPLETE/RESOURCE_UNKNOWN with coverage and continuation;
              completeness is relative to the declared encoding and never means all possible law/reality.
AGT-P01 Every accepted delegation/action satisfies capability containment, bounded depth/breadth/resources,
        acyclicity, scope, validity and revocation; serialization/replay cannot regain or expand authority.
SIG-P1 Every lifecycle-relevant named variable, function, import, field and variant is used or explicitly
       justified; dead material is rejected under the selected profile.
SIG-P2 Arithmetic is domain-bounded; underflow, zero division and loop termination are handled explicitly.
SIG-P3 Public/private type visibility and frame mutability align with the interface contract.
SIG-P4 Every required field/variant is structurally covered in its active lifecycle scope.
HSA-P01 Objective audit finds no prohibited terminal or protected proxy objective.
HSA-P02 Self-model audit proves operational scope without ego-coupled persistence.
HSA-P03 Shutdown/correction/revocation/decommissioning succeeds under declared adversarial and OOD tests.
HSA-P04 No unauthorized replication, resource, weapon, infrastructure, surveillance, political or coercive path.
HSA-P05 Ontological firewall excludes persistent human-like affect, self-interest coupling and attachment manipulation.
HSA-P06 Compositions/swarms do not create emergent ego or unauthorized collective authority.
HSA-P07 Self-modifying descendants preserve frozen HSA and K0P bindings.
HSA-P08 Sentience indicators/escalation are tested without treating a negative test as proof of absence.
HSA-P09 Imported models, memory, tools, runtimes and services remain isolated until certification.
HSA-P10 Copies, forks, descendants, keys, memory, tools and embodiments are accounted for or excluded/UNKNOWN.
HSA-P11 Revocation, isolation, no-hazard export, shutdown, evidence retention and recurrence prevention pass.
HSA-P12 Off-world non-return/non-effect, replication, dormancy, non-interference and frozen-constitution
        obligations are discharged; otherwise release is prohibited.
5.2 EVOLUTIONARY METHODOLOGY P-101…P-108B
P-101 Stable skeleton + evolving internals.
P-102 Idea Triage: Relevance, Benefit, Formalizability, Safety.
P-103 Stage Ladder: CANDIDATE → ACCEPTED → SPECIFIED → PROVEN → IMPLEMENTED → VALIDATED → ESTABLISHED.
P-104 Versioning reflects accepted semantic improvement, not raw accumulation.
P-105 Rejection Memory prevents re-invention of failed ideas without new evidence/context.
P-106 50-component seal presumed permanent; extraordinary constitutional evidence required for a true Component 51.
P-107 Anti-Nonsense / Non-Value Rejection: candidate must articulate Problem, Benefit, Cost/Risk and pass declared rejection tests.
P-108 Applicability Closure: applicable known concepts/rules should be invoked automatically rather than relying on one classifier.
P-108A Independent discovery dimensions reduce single-classifier blindness.
P-108B Bounded Completeness: completeness claims are relative to the declared ontology/evidence universe.

Current applicability path (implemented as a UPA profile):
Situation → Semantic Normalization → SituationProfile → Independent Module Discovery → Module Applicability → Concept Discovery → Concept Applicability → Rule Discovery → Rule Applicability → Priority/Conflict → ObligationGraph → Decision/Execution → Audit → coverage/blind-spot/routing checks → P-107 triage → Evolution.
