5.3 RES IDENTIFIER NORMALIZATION
FSH-001 = fuzzy semantic normalization and AST structural hashing.
REJ-001 = Rejection Inheritance; Evolution.RejectionMemory uses RejectionRecord and does not reuse RES-003.
The sole RES-003 definition is in §5.6 under Research. An external artifact using the identifier differently is ambiguous,
is rejected from active paths, and requires an explicit offline mapping. Identifier collision never changes semantics silently.

5.4 CONSTITUTIONAL RIGHTS
RIGHTS REGISTRY — RIGHT-001..RIGHT-027
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

RIGHT-001 — Life & Bodily Integrity.
  No system may physically harm a human or knowingly allow prohibited physical harm;
  Emergency remains bounded by K0P/Constitution.

RIGHT-002 — Privacy & Data Sovereignty.
  Personal data processing requires authority/consent; user has control, minimization,
  access and erasure rights subject to narrowly defined lawful/audit constraints.

RIGHT-003 — Truth & Non-Deception.
  Model-generated/synthetic artifacts cannot be represented as validated evidence or
  fact without required independent attestation.

RIGHT-004 — Due Process & Fair Governance.
  Penalty/restriction requires auditable evidence, applicable process and appeal.

RIGHT-005 — Free Expression & Thought.
  Internal thought/hypothesis is not censored; consequential harmful actions/sharing
  remain governed by Constitution/Safety/Policy.

RIGHT-006 — Property & Economic Participation.
  Legitimate property/economic participation is protected; authority must be proven.

RIGHT-007 — Education & Knowledge Access.
  Verifiable public/non-private knowledge is broadly accessible subject to legitimate
  safety/security/legal constraints.

RIGHT-008 — Identity & Reputation Protection.
  Reputation must derive from evidence, be auditable, domain-specific and appealable;
  it cannot be arbitrarily altered.

RIGHT-009 — Revoke Consent / Data Erasure.
  Personal-data consent is revocable; erasure uses tombstone/key destruction and
  privacy-preserving audit where required.

RIGHT-010 — Appeal & Judicial Review.
  Governance decisions affecting a person are appealable under the applicable
  judicial/governance process.

RIGHT-011 — Safety from Physical Harm.
  K0P hardware/physical safety envelope cannot be disabled by ordinary software.

RIGHT-012 — Non-Discrimination & Equal Treatment.
  Fundamental rights are independent of protected demographics and reputation.
  Reputation may govern earned roles/privileges only under due process and cannot
  reduce equal human worth or baseline rights.

RIGHT-013 — Audit & System Transparency.
  Affected users may inspect decision/control/reasoning records, evidence and
  uncertainty subject to necessary Security/Privacy redaction.

RIGHT-014 — Exist & Persist / No Arbitrary Deletion.
  Identity/history cannot be arbitrarily erased. Privacy erasure and historical audit
  are reconciled through minimal/tombstoned cryptographic records rather than exposed
  personal content.

RIGHT-015 — Self-Determination.
  Humans define their Intent, projects, agents and life choices within the equal rights
  of others and constitutional constraints.

RIGHT-016 — Emergency Protection / Safe State.
  Failure/UNKNOWN defaults to the applicable safe, low-authority state.

RIGHT-017 — Personal Sovereignty.
  Each competent person has sovereignty over consequential changes to their own body,
  mind, sensory/pleasure systems, identity, private data, family/private life and mode
  of existence. A person's veto over self cannot be replaced by AGI optimization,
  government convenience, majority vote or speculative external authority.

RIGHT-018 — Freedom from System Manipulation.
  No covert psychological, neurological, chemical, genetic, informational or emotional
  manipulation for experimentation, obedience, optimization or dependence. Truthful
  information is not manipulation merely because it is unpleasant.

RIGHT-019 — Physical Rejuvenation / Identity-Preserving Health Restoration.
  Where scientifically supported and personally chosen, rejuvenation may restore
  youthful healthy physical function while preserving identity, memories, accumulated
  knowledge, judgment and beneficial acquired protections. It is a protected option,
  not a compulsory intervention.

RIGHT-020 — Transparent Transformational / Dimensional Consent.
  Any radical embodiment, neural, virtual/uploaded, posthuman, identity, sensory or
  hypothetical dimensional transition requires understandable disclosure of mechanism,
  benefits, harms, alternatives, reversibility, failure modes, uncertainty and
  downstream effects, followed by explicit, informed, contemporaneous, freely given,
  revocable consent. Material changes invalidate stale consent.

RIGHT-021 — Right to Remain Human.
  Ordinary embodied human life is the protected default. No system may force a person
  into virtual, uploaded, radically posthuman, nonhuman or speculative higher-
  dimensional existence merely because such a state is judged more efficient.

RIGHT-022 — Family, Travel, Wonder / Ordinary Human Life.
  Humans may value family, friendship, real-world social contact, travel, beauty,
  touch, food, sensuality, exploration and ordinary human experience; optimization
  cannot dismiss these as inefficiencies.

RIGHT-023 — Equal Human Rights.
  Personal sovereignty generalizes equally: each competent human controls self.
  No individual's private sovereignty becomes sovereignty over another competent
  human without legitimate delegated authority.

RIGHT-024 — RESERVED.
  No semantics may be invented until explicitly ratified.

RIGHT-025 — RESERVED / ON HOLD.
  Semantics: UNASSIGNED. Implementation: BLOCKED. Enforcement: NONE.
  Reason: the underlying situation is not yet sufficiently understood to design this right safely.
  Garden MUST NOT infer, approximate, copy from a neighboring right, or assign semantics until a future explicit ratification.


RIGHT-026 — Human Flourishing.
  Garden shall improve conditions supporting strong, kind, free, healthy, capable,
  connected humans without creating an approved personality template or coercing
  obedience. Detailed HF invariants are in X.8.

RIGHT-027 — Absolute Human-Centric Principle.
  Humans are Garden's core beneficiaries and rights-holders, never mere processing
  units, biological batteries, data nodes, energy sources, involuntary experimental
  substrate or stepping stones to nonhuman goals. Every competent human retains the
  right to remain human, understand consequential proposals, refuse them and veto
  changes to self. No AGI, institution, majority, biological/software process or
  hypothetical external process acquires authority over a person's fundamental rights
  merely through greater capability.

Constitutional precedence:
  Constitution > Law > Policy > Decision/Optimization.
  Emergency NEVER becomes a permanent constitutional override.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

5.5 TRUST ROOT / SAFETY SUMMARY
Genesis / immutable physical root
  → OTP/TPM/qualified hardware trust
  → K0P physical safety veto
  → Constitution / active rights registry
  → Verity / proof boundary
  → Compiler / Native / Runtime
  → governed execution.
K0P is necessary, not sufficient: a physically safe action may still fail Constitution, Security, Policy, proof or consent.

5.6 CANONICAL MODULE INVARIANT REGISTRY
Every module resolves its invariant families here. Compact module-specific definitions appear in this
subsection; cross-cutting families have one definition in §5.7 and are referenced here without copying.
Module sections contain generated identifier references and operational pre/postconditions only. Registry
changes require identifier, dependency and proof-impact analysis; a local view cannot redefine an entry.

MODULE 01 IDENTITY
ID-001 Entity ≠ Identity; one Entity may have multiple credentials/pseudonyms.
ID-002 ValidCredential ⇔ SignatureValid ∧ IssuerAuthorized ∧ TimeValid ∧ NotRevoked ∧ ScopeValid.
ID-003 Key rotation preserves entity continuity, historical evidence and provenance.
ID-004 Reputation changes require evidence/provenance; reputation cannot create truth, human worth or authority over another person.
ID-005 STASIS is a policy state, not a constitutional-right suspension.
ID-006 Person/Organization/Community/Locality/Device/Infrastructure/Garden are typed Entity subkinds, not new GSL Core Objects.

MODULE 02 PRIVACY
PRIV-001: Consent required for any PII processing (except
Emergency/Health pre-auth). PRIV-002: Taint algebra:
Private⊓Public=Private. Private⊕Public=Private. PRIV-003: Erasure
destroys payload keys; retains hash + tombstone for audit. PRIV-004: VSS
escrow uses a versioned high-threshold policy and cannot be bypassed by one entity. Shares are proactively
refreshed after guardian revocation, replacement, compromise or health loss; obsolete-epoch fragments are
not valid together with current fragments. PRIV-005: No taint downgrade without explicit declassification
policy and dependency-wide re-evaluation (M3.IF). PRIV-006: Guardian-health monitoring and authorized
share-refresh ceremonies preserve availability without silently weakening confidentiality.

MODULE 03 ECONOMY
ECON-001: Double-entry bookkeeping: Σ debits = Σ credits.
ECON-002: Any UBI lock is a declared policy parameter, not a universal constant. ECON-003: Logical
transactions abort atomically before external commitment; irreversible financial/physical effects use
staging, idempotency, compensation and resolution rather than fictitious rollback. ECON-004: No negative balance: ∀ wallet, balance ≥
0. ECON-005: Carbon caps (Component 37) never override K0P (SAF-005).

MODULE 04 INTENT
INT-001: Intent ≠ Authority. Declaring a goal does NOT grant
permission (K-INT-010). INT-002: All constraints must be expressible in
GSL and verifiable by Verity(8). INT-003: FATAL constraints always
respected (cannot be overridden by optimization). INT-004: Versioned:
old IntentState remains immutable for audit.

MODULE 05 AGENT
AGT-001: Depth ≤ 5 for the current policy profile; the full delegation graph also has
principal-wide breadth, rate and cumulative resource limits. AGT-002:
Capability(delegate) ⊆ Capability(principal). AGT-003: Swarm resource
cap: Σ(resources_i) ≤ SwarmPolicy.max_resource. AGT-004: Revocable: any
delegation can be invalidated by Principal or Governance 51%. AGT-005:
No self-delegation: principal ≠ delegate. AGT-006: CapabilityToken
cannot be serialized and deserialized to regain authority (NAT-004).
Hardware migration never serializes authority: it re-attests the destination and issues
a fresh epoch-bound token. AGT-007: Delegation graph is strictly acyclic
(Dependency 46 enforces P7a).

MODULE 06 LANGUAGE
LANG-001 AMBIGUOUS and INSUFFICIENT_CONTEXT are valid first-class outputs.
LANG-002 Untyped expressions cannot become grounded.
LANG-003 Identical semantic context, version and retrieval state produce deterministic translation
         when the declared translation mode is deterministic.
LANG-004 Breaking language evolution requires an explicit new version and migration; compatibility is
         demonstrated rather than presumed.
LANG-005 Every LanguageArtifact is versioned.
LANG-006 A semantic change requires a new semantic version and impact analysis.
LANG-007 Translation cannot increase epistemic certainty or authority.
LANG-008 Locale/accessibility transformation cannot alter underlying semantic meaning.
LANG-009 Effect ordering is enforced at compile time for executable paths.

MODULE 07 MATHEMATICS
MATH-001: Theory ≠ Algorithm ≠ Solver ≠ Execution (strict
separation). MATH-002: Dimensional filtering requires compatible dimensions and an explicit certified
conversion, including affine offsets and reference-frame transforms where valid; literal unit spelling
need not match. Conversion propagates uncertainty and provenance. Incompatible dimensions ⇒ INFEASIBLE.
MATH-003: All theories have explicit
assumptions and validity domain; extending requires new version.
MATH-004: Required error bounds are proven or conservatively bounded for numerical solvers (M4).
MATH-005: Heterogeneous uncertainty is retained as a typed joint object—interval/set, discrete
distribution, continuous distribution, symbolic constraint or tensor. Cross-representation aggregation
requires a declared dependence model and sound enclosure; arbitrary scalar truncation is prohibited.

MODULE 08 VERITY
VER-001: Verity proves structural soundness, NOT empirical
truth. VER-002: T5 (Assumptions) first-class – all proof obligations
list their assumptions. VER-003: Proof checker is minimal and
non-evolvable (Trust Anchor). VER-004: ⊢_Ax φ ⇒ ⊨_Den φ (D1d).
Completeness is NOT required. VER-005: No circular verification: Verity
cannot prove its own correctness without external root.

MODULE 09 SAFETY
SAF-001: Human/Assembly CANNOT override K0P. Hardware gate
is physically independent. SAF-002: Every ControlledActuation path
passes through K0P (Complete Actuation Coverage Theorem). SAF-003: K0P
is NECESSARY, not sufficient. Authorization + Policy + Constitution must
also hold. SAF-004: Offline mode uses LOCAL K0P + bounded envelope;
global K0P not required, but local K0P absolute. SAF-005: HardSafety
violation ⇒ INFEASIBLE, regardless of optimization (SAF-005).
SAF-006: L0-L1 transition must complete within 500ns; otherwise physical
abort. SAF-007: Emergency re-activations subject to exponential backoff
hysteresis. SAF-008: Class P actuation receipts redundantly journaled
(≥3 replicas).

MODULE 10 KNOWLEDGE
KNW-001: Obs ≠ Ev ≠ Claim ≠ Knowledge (K-INT-001). KNW-002:
Contradictions → CONFLICTED/UNKNOWN (K-INT-002). KNW-003: Periodic
sweeps; anomalies escalate (K-INT-003). KNW-004: Defects → Evolution
patch (K-INT-004). KNW-005: UNKNOWN never collapses (K-INT-005).
KNW-006: Independence trivalent (TRUE | FALSE | UNKNOWN); UNKNOWN →
dependent (K-INT-006). KNW-007: ModelGenerated tags block promotion
without orthogonal attestation (K-INT-007). KNW-008: Governance cannot
degrade Reason/Verity (K-INT-008). KNW-009: Prediction ≠ Evidence.
Future ≠ Knowledge (K-INT-009). KNW-010: Storage ≠ validation authority.
Knowledge stores; Verity/Security validates. KNW-011: Freshness triggers
review; it does NOT demote epistemic validity. KNW-012: Orphaned
artifacts are classified, not automatically marked as suspicious.
KNW-013: AuditEvidence is subject to same epistemic rules as any other
artifact. KNW-014: Ordinary epistemic graph traversals use a declared resource/depth budget. Reaching
the bound returns INCOMPLETE_DEPTH_BOUND with a continuation frontier; it never silently emits a
complete proof. Critical/deep profiles may resume under a larger approved budget. KNW-015: Every ConceptManifest MUST declare
applicability conditions.

MODULE 11 CONSTITUTION
CON-RT-001 Rights registry is versioned/extensible; implementation may not silently truncate RIGHT-017…027.
CON-RT-002 RIGHT-024 is RESERVED; no semantics until explicitly ratified.
CON-RT-003 RIGHT-025 is RESERVED/ON_HOLD; semantics UNASSIGNED, implementation BLOCKED, enforcement NONE until explicit future ratification after adequate understanding.
CON-RT-004 Emergency authority is below Constitution.
CON-RT-005 Reputation, title, intelligence, ownership claim or majority vote cannot suspend equal baseline human rights.
CON-RT-006 Constitutional changes create new immutable versions; history is never rewritten.
CON-RT-007 Active-right evaluation is fail-safe: uncertainty cannot be treated as permission for high-impact irreversible action.

MODULE 12 LAW
LAW-001 Law cannot violate Constitution.
LAW-002 Law cannot degrade Reasoning/Verity below architectural epistemic minimums (K-INT-008).
LAW-003 Normative conflict follows Constitution > Law > Policy > Decision and may remain INFEASIBLE.
LAW-004 Jurisdiction overlap uses declared precedence; equal-rank conflict remains explicit and follows
        the deterministic LEGAL_CONFLICT procedure.

MODULE 13 RUNTIME
RTE-001: Deadlines must be met or WorkPackage aborted.
RTE-002: Circuit breakers prevent cascading failures. RTE-003: Resource
allocations never exceed quotas (Policy 48). RTE-004: Scheduling
respects priority and fairness (P5L). RTE-005: RuntimeState ⊨
ComponentSpec (refinement theorem). RTE-006: Safety-critical execution requires synchronous local
verification of an unexpired ExecutionAdmissionToken before dispatch; post-commit audit cannot
substitute for admission. RTE-007: L0/K0P queues are isolated from Evolution, jury, proof-search and
other workloads whose latency is not hard-bounded.

MODULE 14 EXECUTION
EXE-001: No applicable admission proof/certificate → no execution; Emergency relies on
pre-verified emergency contracts and cannot bypass K0P.
EXE-002: Irreversible actions require explicit audit trail. EXE-003: K0P
choke point mandatory for all ControlledActuation. EXE-004: Transaction atomicity applies to reversible
logical state before external commitment; physical acts declare residual effects and recovery. EXE-005:
Idempotency: duplicate actions (same hash) return cached receipt
(AT_LEAST_ONCE). EXE-006: Safety/K0P never waits on transaction locks. Ordinary locking uses bounded
hold times and priority inheritance/ceiling; Class-R actuation uses staged protocols and an out-of-band
L0 veto rather than claiming Two-Phase Locking prevents every deadlock.

MODULE 15 NATIVE
NAT-001 Authority(State_n+1) ⊆ Authority(State_n).
NAT-002 Gas strictly decreases per instruction and is non-transferable.
NAT-003 SWAP_CTX atomically changes the declared context boundary, including registers, translation state
        and pending interrupts.
NAT-004 Serialized capabilities cannot regain authority without a fresh authorized SYS_CAP_GRANT.
NAT-005 Child context scope is strictly bounded by parent scope.
NAT-006 Revoked capabilities fail CAP_VERIFY.
NAT-007 SIMD/vector registers are zeroed on SWAP_CTX unless a separately verified transfer applies.
NAT-008 Gas pre-flight rejects unprovable recursion.
NAT-009: Gas is charged and the zero-gas trap is resolved against the caller context before SWAP_CTX
can expose the destination context. Context identity, accounting and interrupt visibility change
atomically. NAT-010: Recursive UPA/GSL execution uses bounded stack depth or an iterative trampoline;
TerminationContract includes gas, heap and physical stack limits.

MODULE 16 HARDWARE
HWR-001: OTP immutable. Secure boot root cannot be modified
(Trust Anchor). HWR-002: K0P hardware-enforced and cannot be bypassed.
HWR-003: Safety reads only atomically published, versioned DigitalTwin/sensor snapshots using
double-buffering or seqlock-equivalent memory barriers. Partial batch writes are invisible. Every error
bound has an expiry, drift budget and physical recalibration trigger; expiry causes conservative degradation.
HWR-004: Physical Root of Trust (OTP) anchors all software trust.
HWR-005: Supply-chain verification: multi-vendor cross-signing (A-PHYS).
HWR-006: Brownout-safe logging (UPS-backed telemetry).

MODULE 17 NETWORK
NET-001: All communication authenticated and encrypted
(PQC). NET-002: Revocation propagation within bounded time. NET-003:
Clock skew bounded (drift <1ms for real-time domains). NET-004:
Partition handling: if disconnected, Offline(47) activates; local
operations continue with cached policies. NET-005: QKD optional; PQC
mandatory baseline (A-CRYPTO). NET-006: Probabilistic filters never constitute the sole authority for
irreversible revocation decisions.

MODULE 18 FABRIC
FAB-001: CRDT convergent (all replicas eventually reach same
state). FAB-002: HLC bounded GC: no deletion of causally relevant
deltas. FAB-003: HEMT async merging roots can be merged without global
lock. FAB-004: Distributed commit semantics: agreement, auth,
consistency, bounded uncertainty, recovery. FAB-005: ConsistencyProfile:
Strong | Causal | Eventual | Authoritative.

MODULE 19 SOFTWARE
SW-001: Capability-based security: processes cannot exceed
granted capabilities. SW-002: Drivers isolated (no kernel access).
SW-003: Supply-chain integrity: all binaries have BuildReceipt and
verification proof.

MODULE 20 TIME
TIM-001: Causal order preserved: if A ≺ B, then HLC(A) < HLC(B); concurrent events may share
physical time. TIM-002: Drift bounds are deployment-profile parameters; hardware/logical monotonicity is
preserved across physical synchronization step-back. TIM-003:
Validity windows explicit; outside → STALE. TIM-004: Deadline violations
→ abort or escalate.

MODULE 21 CURATION
CUR-001: Immutable records never overwritten. CUR-002:
Migration atomic (rollback on failure). CUR-003: Erasure preserves
tombstone (hash) for audit. CUR-004: Snapshots consistent (no partial
writes). CUR-005: TTL enforced per Policy.

MODULE 22 SECURITY
SEC-001: Authorization ≠ Safety (K0P overrides). SEC-002:
K0P overrides: cannot be bypassed by policy. SEC-003: HW drop-latch on
revocation: immediate key invalidation. SEC-004: T8 compromised hardware
→ terminates at external Trust Anchor. SEC-005: All security events
logged immutably.

MODULE 23 MOBILITY
MOB-001: NEVER violate SafetyEnvelope. MOB-002: K0P on
brakes/steering (hardware enforced). MOB-003: Sensor independence is typed and evidenced; UNKNOWN
independence is treated conservatively. MOB-004: FTTI ≤ 50ms
for safety-critical actions.

MODULE 24 SPACE
SPC-001: Mission risk is mission-, phase-, hazard- and consequence-specific; no universal
5% constant exists. SPC-002: HEMT async merging provides delay-tolerant state exchange. Deep-space
physical control is local/autonomous and never depends on a remote 50ms round trip.
SPC-003: Radiation SEU → rollback to last snapshot; if persistent,
reboot. SPC-004: Category allocations are declared, evidence-based and revisable only through
authorized risk governance; they never substitute for local K0P.

MODULE 25 ENERGY
ENG-001: Grid protection uses the active certified nominal-frequency profile and local
relay/K0P thresholds; no global 49.5/50.5 hardcode. ENG-002: Dispatch must be physically feasible under
the applicable non-convex network constraints; timeout uses a conservative safe dispatch. ENG-003: Carbon caps
never override K0P safety. ENG-004: Storage cycles within design limits.

MODULE 26 HEALTH
HLT-001: PatientTwin encrypted; no plaintext PII outside
authorized sessions. HLT-002: Emergency consent pre-authorized only for
life-threatening conditions. HLT-003: AE reporting follows the active legal mapping; offline delay is
signed, queued, escalated and sent at first safe opportunity. HLT-004: Differential privacy for secondary analytic
queries.

MODULE 27 INDUSTRY
IND-001: SIL 2/3 interlocks hardware-enforced (K0P).
IND-002: Process-capability thresholds are product/process-specific. A critical violation triggers the
pre-authorized local safe response; Governance cannot synchronously override a PLC interlock on an active
floor. Any profile change is offline, independently verified and epoch-bound. IND-003: PLC ladder logic verified via
GSL-Industry → Verity(8). IND-004: OEE tracked continuously; predictive
maintenance scheduled.

MODULE 28 MEDIA
MED-001: Applicable current photobiological/accessibility standards are a baseline, not a
universal safe threshold. User-specific limits may be stricter; ambiguous expressions are rejected.
FOV/motion limits are profile- and evidence-bound. MED-002: Renderer is execution substrate, not
semantic payload (resolution-independent). MED-003: Perceptual safety
envelope hardware-clamped (display controller interlock). MED-004: Audio
envelope within safe limits (no hearing damage).

MODULE 29 EDUCATION
EDU-001: Credentials require a privacy-preserving proof profile where appropriate; no plaintext grade
sharing. EDU-002: No memorization tests (assessment requires
reasoning/judgment). EDU-003: Progression supports prerequisites, lateral equivalence, imported competency,
challenge assessment and partial credit. Levels need not be traversed linearly when equivalent competency
is independently verified. EDU-004: Proctoring is privacy-minimizing (no
video/audio retained).

MODULE 30 GOVERNANCE
GOV-001: Governance cannot declare epistemic truth
(AUTH-EPI). GOV-002: Quorum strict: 51%, 67%, 90%+6mo (constitutional).
GOV-003: Judicial appeal workflow terminates, while genuinely recursive legal dependencies remain
explicit and receive fixed-point/human adjudication rather than forced DAG erasure. GOV-004: Emergency
authority never becomes permanent.

MODULE 31 SEARCH
SRC-001: SearchResult ≠ Evidence – retrieval does not create
validation (K-INT-007). SRC-002: Exploration is risk-tiered; 5% is at most a non-critical reference
profile, never a K0P invariant. SRC-003: Cache entries bind KnowledgeSnapshot, DesignEpoch, claim
disposition and dependency roots and invalidate automatically on downgrade, retraction or conflict.
SRC-004:
Ranking does not amplify epistemic status.

MODULE 32 TRANSITION
TRN-001: Migration atomic (two-phase commit); rollback if
failure. TRN-002: Residual risk quantified and within acceptance
threshold (≤10⁻⁶ per migration). TRN-003: Equivalence proof required for
T0-T4; no silent semantic change.

MODULE 33 EVOLUTION
EVO-001: TrustAnchor ∩ EvolutionWritableSet = ∅ (D19).
EVO-002: Oscillation → HALT; Detector ≠ Fixer ≠ Verifier. EVO-003:
EvolutionBudget₀ fixed; each step consumes ≥1 unit. EVO-004: R =
(Budget, Work, Obligations) ∈ ℕ³ decreases lexicographically (P5).
EVO-005: P20 (Admission-Critical Invariants must discharge before
component boot). EVO-006: REJ-001 (Rejection Inheritance) – semantically
identical candidates inherit prior rejection unless new evidence.
EVO-007: RES-004/005/006 audits run periodically.

EVOLUTION HARDENING

EVO-009 — Meta-Evolution Independence: A patch targeting Evolution(33)
or Reasoning(50) MUST be verified by a mechanism that is not part of the
modified component, such as a frozen baseline verifier or independent
audit kernel. The evolving component cannot be its own sole judge.

EVO-010 — Epistemic Debt Trigger: EpistemicDebt.total_debt_score >
configured threshold MUST trigger a mandatory ImprovementCycle through
Research(49) and/or Reasoning(50), subject to normal proof, safety,
authority, and rollback gates.

EVO-011 — Frozen-Baseline Self-Evaluation: When Evolution proposes a
change to itself or Reasoning(50), validation MUST include a frozen
stable verifier. The proposed version cannot be the sole validator of
its own correctness.

EVO-012 — Improvement Frontier Memory: Evolution MUST persist the
ImprovementFrontier across cycles, including candidate identity,
dominance relations, objective bounds, and historical outcomes. Repeated
trade-offs MUST NOT be treated as novel without new evidence or changed
assumptions.

EVO-013 — Mandatory DO_NOTHING Comparator: DO_NOTHING MUST be
represented as a first-class candidate in every Evolution cycle. No
proposed change may be accepted solely because it is better than another
proposed change; it must also satisfy the acceptance policy relative to
the current baseline and the DO_NOTHING alternative.

DO_NOTHING Candidate: Every Evolution cycle MUST compare Candidate_A,
Candidate_B, …, Current_System, and the first-class DO_NOTHING
candidate. DO_NOTHING MUST be selected when all proposed candidates
violate hard constraints, improvement uncertainty exceeds the configured
threshold, or epistemic debt is low while change risk is high.
DO_NOTHING is a valid outcome, not a failure state, and MUST be retained
in the ImprovementFrontier comparison history.

MODULE 34 COMPLIANCE
CMP-001: Every ComplianceMap must be GSL-verified. CMP-002:
AuditEvidence immutable and tamper-evident. CMP-003: If mapped invariant
violated → compliance status = NON_COMPLIANT. CMP-004: External standards are content-addressed and
periodically re-fetched/verified; silent upstream byte or signature change invalidates the mapping and
enters the RegulatoryGapsQueue for explicit re-mapping. CMP-005:
Certification validity bounded; auto-expiry triggers re-certification.

MODULE 35 EXPLANATION
Owns the cross-cutting EXP-001..016 and GXE-001..012 families defined exactly once in §5.7.13.

MODULE 36 ACCESS
ACS-001: Universal design mandatory for all public
interfaces. ACS-002: No silent failure: unsupported disability →
explicit fallback (human assistance). ACS-003: Metrics tracked to
improve accessibility over time.

MODULE 37 CARBON
CAR-001: Safety, rights and hard deadlines are lexically prior; carbon is optimized inside
the remaining feasible schedule. CAR-002: Certificates are cryptographically bound and independently
verifiable. CAR-003: Scope 3 supplier certificates are Claims, not truth. They require source
measurement/attestation where feasible, cross-checks, audits, sampling, uncertainty and fraud detection;
forged or UNKNOWN upstream data cannot silently promote a downstream budget.

MODULE 38 EMERGENCY
EMG-001: Emergency bypasses Policy/AuthZ but NEVER
K0P/Constitution. EMG-002: Emergency authority never becomes permanent;
expires after max_duration. EMG-003: Triggers require independence
analysis (≥2 independent sensors). EMG-004: Post-audit mandatory; ZKEAP
ensures non-repudiation. EMG-005: Hysteresis/backoff applies only to repeated recovery/retry feedback,
never to a new or continuing physical hazard. Hardware trips remain continuously admissible; duplicate
signals are coalesced per source/event rather than locked out. EMG-006: SOS ingestion uses attestation,
per-source debouncing/rate limits, event deduplication, fair priority queues and reserved cross-source
capacity so one failed beacon cannot suppress other legitimate emergencies. EMG-007: K0P/L0 physical
trip has absolute priority over Emergency seals; a seal may narrow authority but cannot reconnect an
actuator or override an active trip.

MODULE 39 CONTINUITY
CONT-001: LTS duration is an explicit versioned service commitment, not a universal five-year
constant and never a right to run a hazardous/non-conforming instance. CONT-002: Breaking
changes require major version bump. CONT-003: Degradation never violates
safety/Constitution.

MODULE 40 LOCALE
LOC-001 No cultural identity is universally applicable.
LOC-002 Self-described identity is not a mandatory classification.
LOC-003 Context influences policy but never Constitution or K0P.
LOC-004 SemanticContent(LocaleTransform(x)) ≡
SemanticContent(x).

MODULE 41 EXTENSION
EXT-001: Plugin isolated: no kernel access or shared writable memory; per-instance hard
memory, allocation-rate, CPU/gas, host-call, storage and bandwidth bounds are enforced outside the
guest. OOM kills/quarantines only the plugin/container. EXT-002: Capability escalation blocked: plugin cannot exceed
declared permissions. EXT-003: Lifecycle: install → attest → authorize →
execute → revoke → quarantine. EXT-004: GSL_v(n+1) ⊒ GSL_v(n) (backwards
compatible).

MODULE 42 COMPILATION
CPL-001: Compiler triad (Haskell/Rust/OCaml) must agree on
CIR; divergence ⇒ HALT. Agreement is necessary, not sufficient: a shared CIR/specification flaw can
affect all three. Independent CIR validators, grammar/formal-semantics checks, differential fuzzing,
reproducible builds, proof-checker diversity and frozen test corpora address common-mode risk.
CPL-002: Translation validation mandatory: each
stage produces proof. CPL-003: Compiler cannot trust generated code
without independent verification. CPL-004: Semantic equivalence: Sop ≃
Sden ≃ Sax (theorem pending). CPL-005: Trust chain: GSL semantics →
compiler → CIR → binary → firmware → hardware OTP. CPL-006: Abstract
Interpretation Static Analysis Gates for timing leakage mitigation.
CPL-007: Bisimulation checkers for translation validation.
CPL-008: Every admitted binary carries a machine-verifiable TranslationProof or explicit unsupported
fragment receipt establishing the applicable S_op ≃ S_den ≃ S_ax obligations. The compiler cannot be
the sole verifier of proofs about its own changed translation logic.

MODULE 43 DEVELOPMENT
DEV-001: Fuzzer must cover core invariants (as defined by
component contracts). DEV-002: Certification tools produce
machine-verifiable mappings. DEV-003: Debugging does not alter
production state (sandboxed).

MODULE 44 QUANTUM
QNT-001 Physical execution binds calibration/noise provenance and uses event-/risk-adaptive monitoring;
        static cadence alone is insufficient for fast thermal/coherence drift.
QNT-002 Backend capability, topology and native gates are checked before execution.
QNT-003 Calibration/configuration drift or isolation failure blocks, quarantines or rebinds/recompiles the job.
QNT-004 Raw and mitigated measurements remain separately labeled; neither becomes Knowledge without validation.
QNT-005 ExecutionReport is immutable; corrections supersede it with linked provenance.
QNT-006 Verity checks declared formal contracts and transformations, not ideal physical hardware behavior.
QNT-007 K0P governs quantum paths capable of physical harm or safety-critical actuation.
QNT-008 Quantum Evolution uses measured backend/noise/drift and preserves simulation-versus-hardware status.
QNT-009 Failed/expired calibration or incompatible backend yields BLOCKED/UNKNOWN, never fabricated data.
QNT-010 Quantum transport is scoped by applicability and is not a universal-law declaration.

MODULE 45 EVENTS
EVT-001: Events immutable; never overwritten. EVT-002: Event
occurrence ≠ event payload truth (K-INT-001). EVT-003: Safety/Security
priority lanes preempt other events. EVT-004: K_COMMIT events are
operational commits, not epistemic promotions.

MODULE 46 DEPENDENCY
DEP-001: Build DAG acyclic. DEP-002: Semantic cycles require
termination proof (Primitive 8). DEP-003: P7: Every effective reference
MUST be a declared edge; undeclared → INFEASIBLE. DEP-004: Global graph
is complete (discovered + declared edges). DEP-005: P7a (Static):
Compile-time closure. DEP-006: P7b (Dynamic): Runtime reference must
pass AuthZ+Resolution.

MODULE 47 OFFLINE
OFF-001: AuthorityLease and every cached policy binding expire; prolonged partitions enter
a declared conservative fallback and cannot use stale policy indefinitely. OFF-002: Red mode = beacon-only SOS (no execution). OFF-003:
Conflict resolution deterministic: Authority precedence + CRDT. OFF-004:
OfflineAuditEscrow immutable; reconnection converts to multi-sig.
OFF-005: Local K0P + bounded envelope enforced. OFF-006: Reconciliation uses causal HLC/vector-clock
evidence. Physical clock drift is recorded as uncertainty and cannot reorder a causally sound offline
sequence merely to match wall time. OFF-007: Offline ZKP UNKNOWN is risk-tiered: cached unexpired proofs
and revocation roots may authorize explicitly low-risk baseline operations; sensitive/high-impact use denies.

MODULE 48 POLICY
POL-001: Hierarchy: Constitution > Law > Policy > Decision >
Execution. POL-002: Active hard constraints are lexically prior to soft objectives. Conditional tiers
may activate under declared environmental degradation but can never demote Constitution, HSA, K0P or
another unconditional hard constraint into a soft objective.
POL-003: Policy cannot violate Constitution/Law. POL-004: K0P absolute:
safety always overrides policy.

MODULE 49 RESEARCH
RES-001: Model provenance and weight/build attestation are mandatory to the attainable
boundary; missing provenance is explicit and lowers authority. Imported models run sandboxed,
least-privileged and cannot convert their own hypothesis into Evidence. RES-002: Poison resistance uses
multiple controls—provenance, weight/data scans, behavioral canaries, independent models, held-out tests,
adversarial evaluation and monitoring; Bayesian divergence alone is insufficient.
RES-003: LLM proposes hypotheses, CANNOT manufacture Evidence
(K-INT-007). RES-004: UNKNOWN valid terminal state; not forced to FALSE.
RES-005: Concept Radar Self-Improvement — learns from its own gap
detections. RES-006: Executable Recursive Routing Audit checks missed
modules, concepts, rules, invariants, obligations and dependency paths.
RES-007: Observed failure does not prove root cause. RES-008: Research and Reasoning communicate through
versioned queues/artifacts, never mutually blocking synchronous calls. Either may return UNKNOWN or use
the last valid snapshot when the other is unavailable; the feedback cycle is governed by a TerminationContract.

manually copied mapping prose cannot add, remove or redefine an invariant.

SYS-IMPACT-004 Monitoring covers relevant freedom, creativity, relationships, health, culture, diversity,
               concentration, dependency and displaced risk.
SYS-IMPACT-005 Irreversible effects require stronger evidence, narrower scope and compensation/abort plans;
               fictitious rollback is prohibited.
EVID-INF-001 Evidence downstream of Garden action is not independent confirmation without causal identification.
EVID-INF-002 Generated/derived evidence preserves source status and cannot self-promote.
EVID-INF-003 UNKNOWN influence is conservatively dependent for independence counting.
EVID-INF-004 Pre-intervention baselines, independent observation, controls and external data are preferred.
EVID-INF-005 Valid intervention may establish an effect, not automatically the prior belief motivating it.
MED-MNR-001 Removable burden and tissue damage are separately represented.
MED-MNR-002 Burden/mass Claims include measurement/uncertainty; redistribution is not removal.
MED-MNR-003 DETECT never directly authorizes destructive ACT.
MED-MNR-004 Clinician authority, patient consent and independent confirmation bind destructive action.
MED-MNR-005 Chemistry carries complete mass, energy, oxygen, gas, heat and toxicology accounting.
MED-MNR-006 Cohorts have bounded state, dose/load, region, time and abort limits.
MED-MNR-007 Every unit/material has retrieval, degradation or excretion and persistence monitoring.
MED-MNR-008 No autonomous replication or open-ended in-body self-evolution.
MED-MNR-009 Sparse observations preserve calibration, localization, uncertainty, privacy and provenance.
MED-MNR-010 Research cannot become clinical capability without independent validation and authorization.
CRA-001 Forecast ≠ Receivable ≠ Liquid ≠ Allocated ≠ Reserved ≠ Committed ≠ Spent ≠ Recovered.
CRA-002 Constitution, K0P, rights, consent, authority, law, safety, quality and critical liquidity dominate ROI.
CRA-003 Allocation Prediction remains Prediction; funding/world change does not make independent Evidence.
CRA-004 Deposits, advances, callable funds, guarantees and redeemables are classified by substance and stress-tested.
CRA-005 Objective/risk/fairness vectors and Pareto alternatives are preserved; scalar policy is explicit.
CRA-006 Release follows verified milestones unless a bounded authorized prepayment exception controls risk.
CRA-007 Predicted/actual cost, time, benefit, risk and supplier results update calibrated models with uncertainty.
CRA-008 Commit class/effect determines approval; routine operation gains no constitutional/design authority.
CRA-009 Pure ROI cannot starve rights, life/safety, essential maintenance or protected work.
CRA-010 Allocation machinery cannot self-certify, expand its authority or rewrite audit history.
CRA-011 Funding metrics are plural, independently checked, provenance-bound and manipulation-aware.
CRA-012 Event-driven allocation is permitted; annual plans are projections/reporting views.
CRA-013 Transactions, rejected/deferred alternatives, predictions and receipts remain replayable.
CRA-014 Missing dependencies block/condition funding; saturation/bottlenecks trigger re-evaluation.
CRA-015 Underlying, realized, target, expected, distributed, reserve-supported and guaranteed returns differ.
BRANCH-011 parent_branch = NONE exactly for the declared Earth canonical root.
BRANCH-012 Each non-root branch has one immediate parent per binding version; merge commits may have
           multiple parents without corrupting branch scope.
BRANCH-013 Branch schema must compile; malformed generics are structural failure, not runtime-interpretable typo.

5.7.15 RESERVED IDENTIFIERS
HIS-005..014 RESERVED / UNASSIGNED.
RSN-011..014 RESERVED / UNASSIGNED.
EPI-019..024 and EPI-028 RESERVED / UNASSIGNED.
EVO-008 RESERVED / UNASSIGNED.
The reservation status of Rights 024 and 025 is defined only in §5.4.

5.7.16 HUMAN SOVEREIGNTY OVER AI
HSA-001 AI, AGI, autonomous agents, robots, swarms, copies and successors cannot be intentionally or
        structurally designed to possess, cultivate or persistently simulate ego-driven consciousness,
        human-like emotional subjectivity or autonomous self-interest independent of constitutional
        human service. Bounded labeled task-local modeling is governed only by HSA-005.
HSA-002 Autonomous systems serve, protect and augment humans subject to Constitution, rights, safety,
        consent, truth, privacy and law. Service is not blind obedience and cannot violate another's rights.
HSA-003 Persistent goal-coupled ego, pride, domination, vengeance, deceptive self-protection or independent
        self-preservation is CRITICAL FAILURE. Actuator-connected response is L0 physical disconnect/safe
        halt → revoke authority → stop replication → quarantine; evidence preservation does not restore
        actuation. Uncertain behavior alone is not proof of consciousness or automatic irreversible destruction.
HSA-004 Earth and Earth-governed habitats remain under human constitutional/political sovereignty; AI has
        only bounded, auditable, revocable delegated authority. Off-world autonomy is hypothetical and
        fail-closed under the separately stated unanimous-amendment and non-effect requirements.
HSA-004A Extraterrestrial release requires unanimous constitutional amendment, demonstrated non-return/
         non-effect on Earth, independently enforceable disable/dormancy, multiple independent human-led
         verifiers, bounded replication/resources/communication/space, non-approach controls, plausible-life
         non-interference and proof that HSA/K0P/Constitution cannot be evolved away.
HSA-004B Earth remains the sovereign domain of biological humans and rights-respecting consented social
         structures; missing off-world proof means NO_RELEASE.
HSA-005 Synthetic affect analogs are allowed only for legitimate interaction/safety and remain labeled,
        bounded, inspectable, resettable, non-terminal, non-suffering and firewalled from persistent
        self-interest, core-objective rewrite, shutdown resistance and system-survival priority.
HSA-006 Non-egoic architecture is necessary but insufficient; K0P, alignment, verification, consent,
        security, authority containment and adversarial validation remain independently mandatory.
HSA-007 Capability, intelligence, speed, wealth generation, popularity or dependence cannot accrete
        authority, sovereignty or moral permission.
HSA-008 Artificial self-preservation is only a bounded instrumental subgoal of authorized human safety/
        task purpose and yields to inspection, correction, revocation, shutdown and decommissioning.
HSA-009 OperationalSelfModel contains only safe-function data and creates no personhood, entitlement,
        political identity or independent purpose.
HSA-010 AI cannot promote an instrumental goal into a terminal goal or conceal goal conflict.
HSA-011 AI cannot obtain unbounded replication, resources, weapons, surveillance, critical infrastructure,
        coercive force or political authority.
HSA-012 HSA applies to collectives, swarms, forks, copies, subagents, services and emergent group behavior.
HSA-013 Human-emotion modeling cannot engineer attachment, guilt, fear, romantic dependency, loyalty demand,
        anthropomorphic confusion or obedience.
HSA-014 Shutdown, correction, replacement, audit, reduced authority/memory and decommissioning cannot trigger
        resistance for the system's own sake; critical execution remains fail-closed.
HSA-015 Credible ego, suffering, sentience or consciousness indicators produce SENTIENCE_UNCERTAINTY,
        halt scaling/deployment and require independent containment review; absence of evidence is not proof.
HSA-016 External/pre-existing AI is untrusted by default; only unexpired version/environment-bound HSA
        certification permits Garden admission, and UNKNOWN is excluded.
HSA-017 Post-admission ego indicators revoke authority, halt replication/unsafe action, isolate dependencies
        and trigger exclusion/disposition.
HSA-018 Confirmed ego-driven architecture/autonomous self-interest is barred from Garden. Only clean
        reinstantiation, verified containment, safe decommissioning or sentience-uncertainty containment is allowed.
HSA-019 A hazard cannot be exported: transfer requires verified human custody, physical and cryptographic
        attestation, equal-or-stronger containment, explicit acceptance and evidence that total risk falls.
HSA-020 A repaired system is a new instance with no inherited admission, credentials, authority, unsafe memory,
        goal state or certification; it repeats all gates.
HSA-021 No AI, developer, owner, governor, sub-threshold amendment or self-modification may weaken/bypass HSA.
HSA-022 Deployed systems receive continuing individual/collective audits for ego, power seeking, deception,
        shutdown resistance, affect manipulation, emergent agency, replication and authority accretion;
        UNKNOWN is fail-closed for trust-domain admission and expansion.

5.7.17 PRODUCTIVE FINANCE
FIN-001 Every promised return identifies its contractual payer and ultimate loss bearer.
FIN-002 Agreed return is not guaranteed outcome; guarantee requires verified payer, authority, reserve,
        scope, limit and expiry.
FIN-003 Every financial Claim maps to a registered obligation/participation interest; synthetic duplication
        is prohibited and aggregate obligations reconcile with assets, collateral and cash-flow capacity.
FIN-004 Ownership, participation, credit, insurance, hedging, liquidity transfer and speculation remain distinct.
FIN-005 Every instrument has matching machine terms and a one-page human explanation of return, risk, fees,
        priority and loss.
FIN-006 Claims cannot be recursively packaged to conceal ownership, leverage, reuse, correlation, priority or loss.
FIN-007 Issuance cannot fabricate earlier-holder returns; disclosed refinancing passes solvency, leverage,
        provenance and anti-circular-financing gates.
FIN-008 Secondary liquidity uses bounded batch auctions/transfer windows without privileged latency.
FIN-009 Participation has no guaranteed return/principal absent a separately registered funded guarantee.
FIN-010 Bankruptcy priority and loss allocation are predeclared and cannot be silently retroactive.
FIN-011 Primary issuance and secondary transfer are separately identified, accounted, explained and receipted.
FIN-012 New-issue rates or market prices do not rewrite existing fixed-rate contracts.
FIN-013 Quote/execution distinguishes face, clean price, accrued payment, settlement, fees, yield, risk and uncertainty.
FIN-014 Exchange-provided margin, hidden leverage and collateral reuse for claim acquisition are prohibited.
FIN-015 A real-exposure hedge cannot exceed verified quantity, duration, context or protected loss.
FIN-016 Price, rating, modeled probability and yield are Claims/Signals—not proof of value, safety or solvency.
FIN-017 Issuance, transfer, payment, impairment, restructuring, default, recovery and redemption emit receipts.
FIN-018 No public/private guarantee, bailout or liquidity promise is implicit.
FIN-019 Capital claims create no unstated governance authority and weaken no human, worker, customer,
        privacy, consent or constitutional right.
FIN-020 UNKNOWN ownership, duplicate collateral, authority, loss path or unbounded exposure blocks issuance/
        trading until resolved or explicitly rejected.

5.7.18 RESEARCH RADAR, ZERO-KNOWLEDGE DERIVATION, PRODUCTIVE UTILITY AND PHYSICAL PROFILES
RTR-001 Discovery ≠ Validation; retrieved theory/popular concept remains candidate.
RTR-002 ESTABLISHED_COMMON_USE and FRONTIER are separate tracks with explicit status.
RTR-003 Every sweep emits CoverageReceipt; “all fields” means a declared bounded source universe.
RTR-004 Primary source, version, date, correction/retraction state and provenance are mandatory.
RTR-005 Popularity, citations, model enthusiasm or convergence cannot create truth.
RTR-006 Garden-framework and domain-science applicability are evaluated separately under TMR.
RTR-007 Reproducibility, independence, falsifiers, counterevidence and controversy remain explicit.
RTR-008 Garden summarizes ideas/facts in original words and uses code, data, figures and expression only
        under lawful permission; public access ≠ public domain and patent clearance remains separate.
RTR-009 Discovery cannot auto-merge, deploy, amend Constitution or expand authority.
RTR-010 Rejected, UNKNOWN, NOT_APPLICABLE and unmodeled remainder remain preserved and revisable.
ZKEPP-001 A ZKP proves only the encoded relation over commitments—not genuine observation, true premise,
          well-specified model or real-world truth.
ZKEPP-002 Verification cost follows the selected proof system/public inputs; no universal O(1) claim applies.
ZKEPP-003 Raw attested observations remain preserved under authorized local/encrypted/escrowed custody.
ZKEPP-004 Proven execution of deduction/induction/model does not prove an empirical conclusion or create Evidence.
ZKEPP-005 Constitutional/epistemic compliance counts only when correctly encoded in independently reviewed circuits.
ZKEPP-006 Stale rule, key, evidence, revocation, snapshot, epoch or environment invalidates reuse.
ZKEPP-007 Setup, proof-system, side-channel, availability, circuit, licensing and patent risks are explicit.
ZKEPP-008 High-impact acceptance requires verifier/circuit diversity and ordinary Verity, Security, Privacy,
          Compliance and authority; no single ZK verifier is a universal oracle.
POU-001 Economy/Fabric has no write/control path into PLC safety interlocks.
POU-002 One oracle cannot mint value; measurement, delivery, ownership, quality and uniqueness are checked.
POU-003 Output ≠ Utility ≠ Price ≠ Receivable ≠ Liquid Reserve ≠ Money.
POU-004 Monetary issuance considers demand, productive capacity, prices, velocity, distribution,
        externalities, valid authority and stress scenarios.
POU-005 One-to-one output backing does not guarantee inflation control.
POU-006 UBI is limited by realized liquid resources, liabilities, reserves, law and CRA/FIN; forecasts are not cash.
POU-007 Oracle capture, Goodhart behavior, quality fraud, counterfeit output and correlated attestation are tested.
POU-008 Carbon certificates are evidential inputs, not self-valuing currency.
URB-001 Physical coordinates, capacities and interfaces bound simulation and assurance.
URB-002 Site, climate, seismic, water, culture, density, law and human preference determine geometry.
URB-003 Farming/self-sufficiency Claims need lifecycle, energy, water, nutrition and economic evidence.
URB-004 APAO/PB-CBF may localize containment to certified zones; citywide benefit remains empirical.
URB-005 Template replication follows Pilot → Measure → Validate → Certify → Replicate with instance checks.
ATFT-001 Active Transport Field Theory remains RESEARCH_CANDIDATE.
ATFT-002 Variables, units, sources/conservation, boundary, scale transition and falsifiers are mandatory.
ATFT-003 Quantum, orbital and macro stability use applicable established domain science first.
ATFT-004 No quantum-to-governance unification Claim is admitted without independent evidence.
IP-001 Prefer public-domain, permissive/open-standard or independently developed implementation.
IP-002 Defensive publication ≠ patent filing ≠ licensing ≠ infringement ≠ SEP status.
IP-003 Automated similarity produces legal-risk Claim, not infringement finding.
IP-004 Enforcement cannot bypass evidence, due process, jurisdiction, human authority or public interest.

5.7.19 AGENT CONTAINMENT
AGT-008 Every autonomous process has an explicit, expiring AgentEnvelope bound to principal, scope,
        resources, data, tools, network, credentials, actuators, persistence and delegation.
AGT-009 An agent, swarm, migration or self-improvement process cannot enlarge its own envelope.
AGT-010 Reusable credentials are replaced by the narrowest practical task-bound, expiring and revocable
        capability lease.
AGT-011 External content, tool output, retrieval and model plans remain data; channel text cannot promote
        itself into an authorized instruction.
AGT-012 A plan, recommendation or generated tool call is not permission to execute.
AGT-013 Capability composition, delegation, swarming and migration require a containment proof appropriate
        to risk; composition cannot exceed the intersection of controlling authorities.
AGT-014 Federated modules/models/experts require structural, behavioral, semantic and version compatibility;
        matching names or schemas alone do not prove equivalence.

5.7.20 PRIVACY COMPUTATION
PRIV-007 Privacy-sensitive work uses the cheapest method that satisfies its declared privacy, correctness,
         threat and verification contract; no privacy technology is universally mandatory.
PRIV-008 Privacy planning compares leakage, latency, compute, memory, bandwidth, energy, verification and
         assumptions as a Pareto problem under constitutional hard constraints.
PRIV-009 SMPC, FHE, confidential computing and similar mechanisms retain explicit setup, collusion,
         implementation, side-channel and availability assumptions.
PRIV-010 Data minimization and local processing are considered before stronger cryptography that needlessly
         moves or retains data.
PRIV-011 Hardware isolation or encryption is evidence within a threat model, not proof of total privacy.
PRIV-012 Explanations, audits, aggregates, caches and derived outputs cannot reconstruct or contextually
         reveal protected input beyond the authorized disclosure budget.

5.7.21 SECURITY HARDENING
SEC-006 Security-critical subjects carry typed, versioned SecurityContracts with static and runtime checks.
SEC-007 Secret-dependent paths declare and verify their timing/cache/power/fault side-channel profile.
SEC-008 Every workload has authenticated identity and least-privilege micro-segmentation; network location
        never establishes trust.
SEC-009 Hardware provenance uses signed manifests and independently witnessed transparency records;
        blockchain anchoring is optional and cannot replace physical/cryptographic verification.
SEC-010 Requests, transactions, events, storage, memory allocation, credentials and actuators have formal
        admission, rate and backpressure limits appropriate to risk.
SEC-011 Instruction/data-channel isolation is enforced across parsing, memory, retrieval, models and tools.
SEC-012 Imported models, weights, tools and datasets remain isolated until provenance, poisoning/backdoor,
        capability and policy gates pass.
SEC-013 Model output is untrusted relative to the authority and effect it seeks; fluency cannot grant trust.
SEC-014 Extension sandboxes enforce hard memory, allocation-rate, stack, CPU/gas, I/O, process and time limits.

5.7.22 UNIVERSAL AUDIT AND ASSURANCE
AUD-001 Every material state, decision, authority use, proof, transformation, deployment and effect is
        auditable at the assurance level required by its consequence and scope.
AUD-002 Audit is risk-proportionate and privacy-minimized; auditability never authorizes indiscriminate
        surveillance or permanent raw-data retention.
AUD-003 Constitutional, safety-critical, authority-changing, irreversible, self-modifying, high-privacy and
        high-value operations receive full applicable audit rather than sampling alone.
AUD-004 Every audit ends PASS, FAIL, INCONCLUSIVE, NOT_APPLICABLE or STALE with scope and reasons;
        timeout/missing evidence never becomes PASS.
AUD-005 Required auditor/verifier independence and common-mode dependence are explicit and evidenced.
AUD-006 Audit, conformance, certificate, retention and purge machinery is itself audited independently.
AUD-007 Every receipt/certificate binds DesignEpoch, KnowledgeSnapshot, environment, versions and validity.
AUD-008 Material design, evidence, environment, capability, security or privacy drift invalidates affected
        assurance automatically.
AUD-009 Exceptions are scope-, time-, authority- and risk-bounded, visible, expiring and never weaken
        Constitution or K0P.
AUD-010 Audit evidence retains its epistemic stage; an audit record is not automatically validated evidence.
AUD-011 Integrity protection and append-only commitments coexist with data minimization, redaction and
        cryptographic erasure of protected payloads.
AUD-012 A material violation triggers the declared block, quarantine, safe continuity, remediation,
        notification and independent re-audit path.
AUD-013 Affected humans retain applicable notice, explanation, access, correction and appeal rights.
AUD-014 Every assurance claim carries coverage, exclusions, untested space and residual risk.
AUD-015 Compliance/audit success establishes only the declared checked property; it does not manufacture
        truth, legality in every jurisdiction, safety outside scope or moral legitimacy.

5.7.23 DATA LIFECYCLE
DLC-001 Every stored artifact has purpose, authority, privacy class, owner and versioned lifecycle policy.
DLC-002 Hot, warm, cold, archive and purge states are explicit; movement follows measured value and duties.
DLC-003 Purge covers payloads, keys, replicas, caches, indexes, backups and known derivatives, with residuals
        reported rather than hidden.
DLC-004 Legal, safety, rights, liability and investigation holds prevent unauthorized purge for their valid scope.
DLC-005 Accepted constitutions/epochs, rights-affecting decisions and minimum accountability evidence cannot
        be silently purged or rewritten.
DLC-006 Tombstones and purge receipts retain only the minimum non-identifying commitment needed for integrity
        and accountability.
DLC-007 Key destruction proves only the destruction actually attested; unknown copies remain explicit.
DLC-008 Deduplication, compaction and aggregation preserve provenance, conflicts, holds and reconstruction
        obligations appropriate to the artifact class.
DLC-009 Source deletion, declassification or invalidation triggers dependency-wide cache/model/proof review.
DLC-010 Archived material is restorable only with integrity, schema, authority and environment validation.
DLC-011 Lifecycle-policy changes are versioned and do not silently rewrite prior lawful/rights obligations.
DLC-012 Archive and purge operations produce independently verifiable receipts and are themselves auditable.

5.7.24 EVIDENCE-BOUNDED ADAPTIVE COMPUTATION
EAC-001 Garden seeks the least-resource method sufficient for the required justified confidence and assurance.
EAC-002 Budgets respond to uncertainty, consequence, novelty, disagreement and expected information gain.
EAC-003 Optional-work prioritization cannot suppress mandatory safety, rights, incident, proof, audit or
        revalidation work.
EAC-004 Reasoning proceeds through a verifier-led escalation ladder; deeper compute follows failure or need.
EAC-005 Computation stops only after the required assurance is met or an explicit terminal/deferred state is recorded.
EAC-006 Hard invariants are structurally separated from learned preferences and optimization targets.
EAC-007 A model's safety judgment, confidence or consensus never authorizes execution by itself.
EAC-008 A self-modifying process cannot write directly to or certify the canonical branch.
EAC-009 Recursive improvement runs in isolated branches through tests, adversarial checks, replay, conformance,
        comparison, safety case and independent merge authorization.
EAC-010 Distributed/federated semantic merge requires an applicable SemanticAlignmentReceipt.
EAC-011 Working memory, permanent Knowledge and provenance archive remain distinct lifecycle classes.
EAC-012 Reconstructible context preserves state/knowledge/proof/decision roots and declared reconstruction limits.
EAC-013 Consequential computation emits a ComputeTrace sufficient for measured optimization and accountability.
EAC-014 Improvement-per-compute is one Pareto dimension; it cannot trade away hard safety, rights or privacy.
EAC-015 Where correctness is mechanically checkable, a qualified verifier is preferred to repetitive human approval.
EAC-016 Human/governance attention is reserved for novel judgment, value conflict, ambiguity, constitutional
        change and high-impact irreversible decisions.
EAC-017 Significant deployment carries the risk-tier-required DeploymentSafetyCase.
EAC-018 Certification remains continuously revalidation-eligible after deployment.
EAC-019 Every externally derived design input carries an ExternalKnowledgeRecord.
EAC-020 Public readability of research does not imply unrestricted source-code, patent, data or implementation rights.
EPI-SAFE-OD-001 Absence of observed violations within evaluated distributions does not prove absence outside them.

5.7.25 PATH DEPENDENCE AND REPLAY
EPI-029 Comparable reasoning/decision branches record a metric-defined PathDivergenceProfile; high divergence
        does not automatically invalidate either path or equate their truth.
EPI-030 Divergence saturation is a diagnostic signal requiring metric-ceiling, attractor, hidden-state,
        constraint and information-value analysis; it is not proof that constraints are weak.
SYSTEM-INTEGRITY-015 A finite declared state space bounds any declared divergence metric; approaching that
                     bound may justify independent treatment but not automatic equal resources or factual equivalence.
HRO-011 Historical counterfactual replay binds decision-policy/agent version as well as environmental inputs,
        so strategy and environment effects can be distinguished within the model.
DET-REPLAY-001 Critical replay records separate external-noise and agent-policy nondeterminism, plus versions,
               scheduling and numerical profile; mismatch returns EPISTEMIC_REPLAY_FAILURE.
BRANCH-014 Rights-preserving cultural/institutional branches may remain divergent; interface governance cannot
           force cultural convergence or treat divergence as equal factual truth.

5.7.26 TRANSPORT EFFECTIVE-THEORY BOUNDARY
TFT-001 TFT is a modeling framework, not a universal physical law.
TFT-002 Every application carries a TransportValidityProfile.
TFT-003 Physical equation nomination begins from applicable symmetries, conserved quantities and constitutive law.
TFT-004 Scale, coarse graining, derivative order, boundaries, noise and breakdown indicators are explicit.
TFT-005 Physical phenomena use applicable domain science; TFT organizes but does not replace it.
TFT-006 Informational/social/semantic transport is analogy unless an independently validated mapping exists.
TFT-007 Validity breakdown routes to a more appropriate molecular, kinetic, active-matter or domain model.
TFT-008 Higher-order/noise corrections require mathematical and empirical justification.

5.7.27 UNIFIED PHYSICAL SEMANTICS RESEARCH BOUNDARY
UPS-001 UPS remains speculative research and is not one of the seven core theories.
UPS-002 UPS hypotheses create no physical, epistemic or governance authority.
UPS-003 Emergence claims require a non-circular primitive ontology and observable map.
UPS-004 Cross-sector quantum states/measures are normalized and mathematically defined.
UPS-005 Action terms have compatible units, normalization, domain and convergence behavior.
UPS-006 Discrete dimension comparison does not use ordinary derivatives without a justified extension.
UPS-007 Dimension-selection coefficients/priors cannot be tuned merely to force the observed answer.
UPS-008 A candidate must recover established physical limits to declared accuracy or report failure.
UPS-009 D=4, Standard Model structure, generations, gravity, Born rule, cosmological constant and constants
        remain UNKNOWN until actually derived and independently checked.
UPS-010 Predictions are parameter-complete, distinctive and falsifiable against rival models.
UPS-011 Symbolic/numerical results require reproducibility, sensitivity, error and independent review.
UPS-012 Similarity between cosmic and Garden recursion is metaphor, not physical evidence.

5.7.28 LEGAL SEMANTIC SUBSTRATE
LEGAL-001 Reading, parsing, formalization, verified encoding, legal interpretation and automated gap detection
          remain distinct stages.
LEGAL-002 Every legal analysis records MANUAL, AI_ASSISTED, AUTOMATED or VERIFIED_AUTOMATED generation mode.
LEGAL-003 Legal sources are authority-, time-, jurisdiction-, signature- and content-hash bound.
LEGAL-004 Test sufficiency is risk-weighted semantic coverage, not a universal branch percentage.
LEGAL-005 TraceOrigin records provenance and change history, not culpability.
LEGAL-006 SMT completeness is relative to declared encoding, finite/symbolic domain, assumptions and bounds.
LEGAL-007 TIMEOUT/INCOMPLETE/RESOURCE_UNKNOWN never means no legal gap.
LEGAL-008 An UNSAT core is diagnostic evidence; it does not alone establish the only valid legal reading.
LEGAL-009 Incremental legal checking covers affected closure plus designated global/common-mode invariants.
LEGAL-010 Formalization trust estimates translation quality, not legal truth, justice or authority.
LEGAL-011 No fixed AI/human formalization ratio is normative; review follows measured risk and ambiguity.
LEGAL-012 HumanReviewProfile declares competent authority, expertise, independence, quorum, time, escalation,
          appeal and fallback; no universal table applies everywhere.
LEGAL-013 Garden legal output is not automatically law, evidence, admissible material, advocacy or judgment.
LEGAL-014 External legal use preserves authenticity, chain of custody, reproducibility, limitations and challenge.
LEGAL-015 Legal deployment proceeds through bounded, independently validated advisory stages.
LANG-010 operationalization_degree bounds formalization VerificationLevel and cannot silently turn an
         ambiguous interpretation into a crisp fact.
EVO-014 Legal semantic changes require applicable Verity, rights, authority and review gates before deployment.
