PART VI — COMPLETE 50-MODULE SPECIFICATION
====================================================================================================

COMPONENT 1 — IDENTITY
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Universal Module Contract
Purpose      : Persistent entity identity, authentication, credential history, pseudonyms and reputation linkage.
Inputs       : Genesis proof, credentials, signatures, identity events, reputation evidence.
Outputs      : EntityRef, authenticated identity, credential status, pseudonym bindings, reputation profile reference.
Rules        : Entity ≠ Identity; credentials are revocable/versioned; reputation ≠ human worth; identity does not create authority.
Failure      : Invalid/revoked credential → DENY + Security event; uncertain binding → UNKNOWN/REVIEW.
Dependencies : Knowledge(10), Security(22), Privacy(2), Economy(3), Events(45), Time(20).
Why          : Every authority, consent, provenance and accountability path needs a stable, auditable subject.
Status       : ARCHITECTURALLY SPECIFIED; implementation/proof pending unless separately established.

Internal State (GSL-typed):
type IdentityState = {
  entity_id: Hash,
  entity_kind: EntityKind,
  public_key: PublicKey,
  credential_chain: List[Credential],
  active_credential: CredentialRef,
  status: ACTIVE | SUSPENDED | REVOKED,
  reputation_profile: ReputationProfileRef,
  pseudonyms: Map[Purpose, Pseudonym],
  last_rotation: Time,
  last_authn: Time,
  revocation_history: List[RevocationEvent],
  stasis_flag: Bool,
  stasis_bond: ValueRef?,
  metadata_hash: Hash,
  provenance: Provenance,
  version: SemVer,
  created_at: Time,
  updated_at: Time
}

EntityKind = Person | Organization | Community | Locality | Device | Infrastructure | Garden.

Credential = {
  credential_id, holder, issuer,
  credential_type: Biometric | Key | Password | HardwareToken | ZKP,
  public_key?, validity, scope, signature, status, provenance
}
Pseudonym = { pseudonym_id, linked_to_entity, purpose, taint_label, consent_ref, active, created_at, expiry }

ReputationProfile = {
  contribution, honesty, reliability, competence, prosociality,
  generosity, consistency, stewardship,
  domain, evidence, uncertainty, temporal_history, provenance
}
A single global reputation scalar is non-normative. Historical Karma/Bayesian scalar machinery may be used only as a declared per-domain implementation profile; it may not represent human worth or override rights.

Functions:
Register(genesis_proof) → EntityRef
  Requires a valid genesis/identity proof; creates immutable entity genesis.
Authenticate(credential, challenge) → Bool
  Requires ACTIVE, unrevoked, scope/time-valid credential; failed/revoked attempts emit Security events.
Rotate(new_public_key, proof) → Credential
  Old credential becomes SUPERSEDED; entity history/reputation/provenance survive rotation.
Revoke(entity, reason, authority) → Event
  Only the entity itself or valid delegated/governed authority may revoke within scope; active credentials become invalid.
UpdateReputation(evidence_event, domain) → ReputationProfile
  Updates only evidence-supported dimensions; all changes preserve evidence, uncertainty, provenance and appealability.
StasisRequest(bond) → State
  Freezes the applicable active and passive reputation-update profile, including ordinary time decay,
  under the declared policy. Safety revocations, factual corrections and immutable history still apply;
  counterfactual decay may be recorded separately without changing the frozen public value.
ReleaseStasis(authority) → State
  Returns to ACTIVE under the declared policy.

Invariant references: ID-001, ID-002, ID-003, ID-004, ID-005, ID-006.

Math: M4.CRYPTO signatures/PQC; M3.PR calibrated reputation evidence; M1.GR credential DAG; M3.PV provenance.
Proofs: P2, P3, P4a, P6, P11, P14.

COMPONENT 2 — PRIVACY
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: User / Epistemic Role: Enforces consent, data tainting,
erasure, and VSS (Verifiable Secret Sharing) escrow.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type PrivacyState = { consent_records:
Map[Entity, List[ConsentRecord]], taint_graph: Map[DataRef, TaintLabel],
erasure_receipts: List[ErasureReceipt], vss_shares: Map[DataRef,
List[VSSShare]], revocation_queue: List[ConsentRevocation], ttl_policy:
TTLPolicy, last_sweep: Time }

Artifacts: ConsentRecord = { consent_id: Hash, entity: EntityRef,
data_ref: DataRef, purpose: Purpose, scope: Scope, valid_from: Time,
valid_until: Time, revocation_flag: Bool, evidence: EvidenceSet }

TaintLabel = { data_ref: DataRef, classification: PUBLIC | PRIVATE |
SENSITIVE | RESTRICTED | SOVEREIGN, propagation_rules: Set[Rule],
source: EntityRef, timestamp: Time }

ErasureReceipt = { data_ref: DataRef, tombstone_hash: Hash,
key_destruction_proof: Proof, auditor_signature: Signature, timestamp:
Time, jurisdiction: Jurisdiction }

VSSShare = { share_id: Hash, data_ref: DataRef, guardian_id: EntityRef,
share_epoch: UInt, guardian_credential_epoch: UInt, share_fragment: Bytes,
threshold_policy_ref: PolicyRef, validity, revocation_root, attestation }

Functions: GrantConsent(entity: Entity, data: DataRef, purpose: Purpose,
scope: Scope) → ConsentRecord Precondition: Entity must be authenticated
(Identity 1). Postcondition: ConsentRecord appended to state. Data taint
set to PRIVATE. Contract condition: No PII processing without explicit
ConsentRecord.

RevokeConsent(consent_id: Hash) → ErasureReceipt Precondition: Entity
must be owner or Governance order. Postcondition: Consent marked
revoked. Taint propagation triggers cascade. Action: Schedule key
destruction via Curation(21) + Security(22).

TaintPropagate(source: DataRef, derived: DataRef) → TaintLabel Rule:
Taint(derived) = MAX(Taint(source_i)) ∪ Taint(transformation). Algebra:
PRIVATE ⊓ PUBLIC = PRIVATE. SENSITIVE ⊕ PUBLIC = SENSITIVE. Contract condition:
Derived data never has lower taint than its source.

Declassify(source, policy, authority) → ReclassificationReceipt
  A source downgrade never silently downgrades a derivative. It invalidates affected derived views,
  models, caches and proofs and schedules dependency-aware re-evaluation against every remaining source,
  purpose, consent and transformation taint.

EmergencyAccess(data_ref: DataRef, quorum_proof: Set[VSSShare]) →
Plaintext Precondition: Requires the current versioned threshold policy, current share epoch, unrevoked
guardian credentials and a valid revocation root. Postcondition: Data decrypted; event logged to Events(45)
as EMERGENCY. Contract condition: Emergency access NEVER overrides
K0P/Constitution (only Privacy).

Update Protocol: ConsentState: REQUESTED → GRANTED → ACTIVE → REVOKED →
ERASED. Taint: Input → Propagate → MAX(Source) → Final. Erasure: Request
→ KeyDestroy → Tombstone → Audit.

Invariant references: PRIV-001, PRIV-002, PRIV-003, PRIV-004, PRIV-005, PRIV-006.

Dependencies & Math: Math: M3.IF (Information Flow), M4.CRYPTO (VSS),
M1.A (Capability). Proofs: P2 (Non-Amp), P11 (Info-Flow), P14
(Provenance).

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

COMPONENT 3 — ECONOMY
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: User / Operational Role: Garden-wide value: wallets,
atomic transactions, UBI distribution, exchange, lending.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type EconomyState = { wallets: Map[Entity,
Wallet], assets: Map[AssetID, Asset], transaction_log:
List[Transaction], ubi_ledger: Map[Entity, UBIReceipt],
exchange_order_book: List[Order], lending_pool: Map[Entity, Loan],
gas_price: Float, total_supply: Map[AssetID, Float],
last_ubi_distribution: Time, reserve_fund: Float, inflation_rate: Float
}

Artifacts: Wallet = { wallet_id: Hash, owner: EntityRef, balances:
Map[AssetID, Float], transaction_log_hash: Hash, lock_expiry:
Map[AssetID, Time], created_at: Time, version: UInt }

Transaction = { tx_id: Hash, from: EntityRef, to: EntityRef, asset:
AssetID, amount: Float, timestamp: Time, signatures: Set[Signature],
atomic_bundle: Bool, status: PENDING | COMMITTED | ABORTED }

Asset = { asset_id: Hash, type: Currency | Security | Utility |
CarbonCredit | UBI, total_supply: Float, metadata: AssetMetadata,
mint_authority: EntityRef, burn_authority: EntityRef, decimals: UInt }

UBIReceipt = { entity: EntityRef, epoch: UInt, amount: Float,
lock_expiry: Time, distributed_at: Time }

Loan = { loan_id: Hash, lender: EntityRef, borrower: EntityRef,
collateral: AssetRef, principal: Float, interest_rate: Float, due_date:
Time, status: ACTIVE | REPAID | DEFAULTED }

Functions: Transfer(from: Entity, to: Entity, asset: AssetID, amount:
Float) → Transaction Precondition: balance(from) ≥ amount. UBI lock not
active. Postcondition: Debit from, credit to. Atomic (all or nothing).
Operational condition: Sum(debits) = Sum(credits). No negative balance.

DistributeUBI(epoch: UInt) → DistributionManifest Precondition: Called by Time(20) scheduler on the UBI
interval with a funded, authorized manifest. Postcondition: idempotent bounded batches produce Merkle-
committed UBI receipts. A failed batch is retried or compensated without retaining the entire population
transaction in memory. Contract condition: cumulative committed distribution ≤ liquid authorized reserve.

Exchange(buy_asset: AssetID, sell_asset: AssetID, amount: Float) →
Transaction Precondition: Order book has matching orders; cryptographic
auction. Postcondition: Atomic swap with price discovery. Contract condition:
Oracle price feed (M3.PR) used for fair valuation.

Lend(borrower: Entity, lender: Entity, amount: Float, terms: CreditCertificateTerms)
→ Loan Precondition: terms pass the current risk, affordability, disclosure, authority and applicable
collateral policy. No universal 1.5 collateral constant exists. Postcondition: the obligation and loss
waterfall are registered before funding. Default enters bounded workout, restructuring or resolution;
it does not trigger an unbounded simultaneous liquidation cascade.

Update Protocol: Wallet: Balance_Before → Balance_After (Atomic). UBI:
Scheduled → Distributed → Locked → Unlocked. Loan: Request → Approval →
Active → Repaid | Defaulted → Seized.

Invariant references: ECON-001..005, SAF-005.

Dependencies & Math: Deps: Identity(1), Time(20), Policy(48),
Carbon(37), Runtime(13). Math: M4.OPT (Optimization), M3.Game (Game
Theory), M1.R (Resource). Proofs: P1 (State Invariants), P4 (Hard
Safety).

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

PUBLIC BENEFICIAL-OWNERSHIP / PRIVATE-PROFILE BOUNDARY
- Person-specific asset or wealth limits are private Policy/Profile data and never appear in the public
  module specification. Economy loads them only through an authorized private overlay where applicable.
- The valuation unit/currency and valuation method for any applied policy MUST be explicit in its private
  execution context without being promoted into a public default.
- Ultimate beneficial ownership resolves to a Person or Community. A Company/Organization may hold,
  manage or administer assets legally but is not treated as a sovereign human-equivalent beneficiary.
- Private limits do not redefine the GSL VALUE object, public economic policy or universal human rights.

COMPONENT 4 — INTENT
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: User / Semantic Role: Declares what an Entity wants, its
relative priorities, and the hard constraints it must not violate.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type IntentState = { entity: EntityRef,
active_goals: List[Goal], preferences: List[Preference],
hard_constraints: List[Constraint], soft_constraints: List[Constraint],
version: SemVer, last_update: Time, provenance: Provenance }

Artifacts: Goal = { goal_id: Hash, description: String, metrics:
Map[Variable, TargetValue], target_value: Float, deadline: Time, status:
ACTIVE | ACHIEVED | ABANDONED }

Constraint = { constraint_id: Hash, predicate: GSL_Expr → Bool,
severity: FATAL | ERROR | WARNING, source: RuleRef | IntentRef, version:
SemVer }

Preference = { preference_id: Hash, goal_ref: GoalRef, weight_or_rank:
Float | UInt, description: String }

Functions: DeclareIntent(entity: Entity, goals: List[Goal], constraints:
List[Constraint]) → IntentState Precondition: Entity authenticated
(Identity 1). Postcondition: IntentState updated; previous version
superseded. Contract condition: Intent ≠ Authority (declaring a goal does not
grant permission).

ResolveIntent(entity: Entity, action: Action) → Feasibility
Precondition: Action must be expressible in GSL. Postcondition: Returns
FEASIBLE | INFEASIBLE | CONDITIONAL. Logic: ALL hard constraints must
pass. Soft constraints reduce utility score.

ConstraintCheck(action: Action) → Bool Precondition: Action must be
GSL-typed. Postcondition: If any FATAL constraint violated → INFEASIBLE.
Operational condition: Constraints never override K0P/Constitution (lexical
priority).

Update Protocol: Intent: DRAFT → DECLARED → ACTIVE → SUPERSEDED. Goal:
ACTIVE → ACHIEVED (if metrics met) or ABANDONED (if deadline passes).

Invariant references: INT-001, K-INT-010, INT-002, INT-003, INT-004.

Dependencies & Math: Deps: Reasoning(50), Policy(48), Knowledge(10).
Math: M3.Decision (Decision Theory), M4.OPT (Optimization). Proofs: P2
(Non-Amp), P3 (Scope), P4 (Hard Safety).

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

COMPONENT 5 — AGENT
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: User / Operational Role: Manages delegated authority.
Principal (Entity) → Delegate (Entity or System). Bounded by
CapabilityToken.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type AgentState = { agency_graph: DAG,
active_sessions: Map[SessionID, AgentSession], capability_tokens:
Map[TokenID, CapabilityToken], swarm_policies: Map[SwarmID,
SwarmPolicy], revocation_ledger: List[RevocationEvent], max_depth: UInt
= 5, last_revocation_check: Time, pending_requests:
Queue[DelegationRequest], active_swarms: Map[SwarmID, Set[AgentRef]] }

Artifacts: Agency = { agency_id: Hash, principal: EntityRef, delegate:
EntityRef | SystemID, capability_set: Set[CapabilityTokenID], scope:
Scope, validity: Time, revocation_criteria: Set[Predicate], depth: UInt,
delegation_proof: Signature, status: DRAFT | ACTIVE | SUSPENDED |
REVOKED }

CapabilityToken = { token_id: Hash, capability: Resource + Operation +
Scope, resource_limits: ResourceLimits, context_hash: Hash, DesignEpoch,
policy_root, issuer_credential_epoch, expiry: Time, nonce: UInt,
issuer: EntityRef, signature: Signature, revoked: Bool }

AgentSession = { session_id: Hash, agency_ref: AgencyRef, nonce: UInt,
expiry: Time, signature: Signature, capability_token_ref:
CapabilityTokenRef }

SwarmPolicy = { swarm_id: Hash, agents: Set[AgentRef], quorum: UInt,
max_resource: ResourceLimits, coordinator: AgentRef,
termination_condition: Predicate }

Functions: Delegate(principal: Entity, delegate: Entity, caps:
Set[Capability]) → Agency Precondition: principal authenticated,
delegate exists, depth(principal)+1 ≤ max_depth, ∀ cap ∈ caps: cap ⊆
Capability(principal). Postcondition: Agency created with status=ACTIVE.
CapabilityTokens generated with bounded resource limits. Contract condition:
Capability(delegate) ⊆ Capability(principal). No amplification.

ExecuteAsAgent(agency_ref: AgencyRef, action: Action, token:
CapabilityToken) → Outcome Precondition: Session ACTIVE, token.expiry >
Now, token.revoked == FALSE, action.scope ⊆ token.scope. Postcondition:
If action passes K0P (Safety 9) and Policy(48) → EXECUTED. Else →
REJECTED. Contract condition: Agent authority ≤ Principal authority (verified at
token creation).

Revoke(agency_ref: AgencyRef, authority: AuthRef) → Event Precondition:
Caller must be Principal OR Governance(30) with valid proof.
Postcondition: agency.status = REVOKED. All child tokens invalidated
immediately. Contract condition: Revocation is irreversible and globally
propagated via Fabric(18).

SwarmCoord(swarm_id: SwarmID, action: Action) → Outcome Precondition:
Active swarm exists, ≥ quorum agents approve, principal-wide cumulative and rate budgets pass,
Σ(resources) ≤ swarm.max_resource, and each action is authorized by the intersection of its sub-agent
token, swarm token and principal token. Horizontal splitting cannot expand capability or total resources.

Update Protocol: Agency: DRAFT → SUBMITTED → ACTIVE → SUSPENDED →
REVOKED. Session: REQUESTED → CHALLENGED → ACTIVE → EXPIRED | REVOKED.
Token: GRANTED → ACTIVE → REVOKED | EXPIRED. Swarm: FORMING → ACTIVE →
VOTING → EXECUTING → DISSOLVED.

Invariant references: AGT-001..007, NAT-004.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Identity(1), Security(22), Reasoning(50),
Runtime(13). Write: Events(45), Knowledge(10), Curation(21). Math: M1.GR
(Graph Theory), M1.A (Authority/Capability Algebra), M3.Game (Game
Theory), M3.PV (Provenance). Proofs: P2, P4, P6, P15, AGT-P01.

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
LAYER 2 — FOUNDATION (7)
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

COMPONENT 6 — LANGUAGE
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Foundation / Semantic Role: The definitive semantic
compiler. Translates Human intent → GSL AST and GSL AST → Human
narrative. Enforces type/effect soundness. Ambiguity is first-class.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type LanguageState = { ast_cache: Map[Hash,
GSL_AST], artifact_registry: Map[LanguageArtifactID, LanguageArtifact],
ambiguity_registry: Map[Hash, AmbiguityTrace], type_environment:
TypeEnv, effect_context: EffectContext, version: SemVer,
last_compilation: Time, translation_memory: Map[GSL_Expr, HumanExpr] }

Artifacts: LanguageArtifact = { artifact_id: Hash, expression_pattern:
String, semantic_forms: Set[GSL_AST], domain: Domain, ambiguity_rules:
Set[Rule], cultural_variants: Map[Locale, GSL_AST],
compatible_gsl_types: Set[Type], incompatible_interpretations:
Set[String], provenance: Provenance, epistemic_status: EpistemicStatus,
version: SemVer }

GSL_AST = { type: Type, effects: EffectSet, dependencies:
Set[DependencyRef], proof_obligations: Set[ProofObligation], expr:
GSL_Expr }

AmbiguityTrace = { id: Hash, input: String, candidates: Set[GSL_AST],
resolution_strategy: CONTEXTUAL_EVIDENCE | EXPLICIT_HUMAN_DOMAIN_AUTHORITY | QUARANTINED_SANDBOX,
resolved_ast: Option<GSL_AST>, status: AMBIGUOUS | RESOLVED | INSUFFICIENT_CONTEXT | NO_CONSENSUS,
timestamp:
Time }

GSL EBNF (v1.0)

Program ::= Module Module ::= ‘module’ ID (‘:’ ID)? ‘{’ Import Stmt* ‘}’
Import ::= ‘import’ ID (‘as’ ID)? Stmt ::= Decl | Def | Rule | Assert |
Query | Eval | Contract | Proof | Patch Decl ::= ‘entity’ ID Extends?
‘{’ Field* ‘}’ | ‘artifact’ ID Extends? ‘{’ Field* ‘}’ | ‘claim’ ID ‘{’
Field* ‘}’ | ‘pattern’ ID ‘{’ Field* ‘}’ | ‘relation’ ID ‘{’ Field* ‘}’
| ‘role’ ID ‘{’ Field* ‘}’ | ‘evidence’ ID ‘{’ Field* ‘}’ Extends ::=
‘:’ ID (‘,’ ID) Field ::= ID ‘:’ Type (‘=’ Expr)? (‘@’ Attribute)? Def
::= ‘def’ ID ‘(’ Param ‘)’ ‘->’ Type (‘effects’ ‘[’ Effect* ’]’)? ‘{’
Expr ‘}’ Rule ::= ‘rule’ ID ‘:’ Condition ‘=>’ Effect Contract ::=
‘contract’ ID ‘{’ Pre* Post* Invariant* ProofObligation* ‘}’ Proof ::=
‘proof’ ID ‘:’ ProofType ‘{’ Obligation* ‘}’ Expr ::= Literal | Ref |
Lambda | Let | If | Match | Block | Query | Action | Transaction Type
::= ID | ‘[’ Type ’]’ | ‘{’ FieldType* ‘}’ | ‘Ref’ ‘<’ Type ‘>’ | ‘Fn’
‘(’ Type* ‘)’ ‘->’ Type Literal ::= String | Int | Float | Bool |
TimeLiteral | HashLiteral

Functions: Parse(human_input: String, context: Context) → Set[GSL_AST]
Precondition: Input must be UTF-8 encoded. Postcondition: Returns one or
more GSL_AST candidates. Contract condition: If multiple interpretations exist →
AMBIGUOUS (not silently resolved).

TypeCheck(ast: GSL_AST) → Bool Precondition: AST must be syntactically
valid. Postcondition: Verifies type consistency against GSL type system
(M0). Contract condition: WellTyped(e) ⇒ Meaning(e) defined (Type Soundness).

Render(ast: GSL_AST, locale: Locale, access: AccessProfile) → HumanText
Precondition: AST must be semantically grounded. Postcondition: Returns
human-readable narrative via Explanation(35) + Access(36). Contract condition:
Explanation does not amplify epistemic status (P2).

Disambiguate(candidates: Set[GSL_AST], context: Context) → GSL_AST
Precondition: Candidates must be type-compatible. Postcondition: If
context resolves → RESOLVED. Else → INSUFFICIENT_CONTEXT. Contract condition:
Ambiguity is never collapsed without explicit evidence (K-INT-012).

If independent parser frontends produce disjoint AST sets, the deterministic result is NO_CONSENSUS.
The input enters a non-executing SemanticAmbiguitySandbox for evidence or competent human/domain
clarification. Governance may choose a lawful policy under explicit ambiguity; it cannot manufacture
semantic truth or force one AST to become epistemically correct.

Update Protocol: LanguageArtifact: DRAFT → REGISTERED → ACTIVE →
SUPERSEDED. AST: Parsed → TypeChecked → Grounded → Rendered. Ambiguity:
CANDIDATE → AMBIGUOUS → RESOLVED (with evidence) | INSUFFICIENT_CONTEXT.

Invariant references: LANG-001, LANG-002, LANG-003, LANG-004, LANG-005, LANG-006, LANG-007, LANG-008, LANG-009.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Knowledge(10), Search(31), Reasoning(50),
Locale(40), Access(36). Write: Knowledge(10), Events(45). Math: M0 (Type
Theory, Logic), M1.ALG (Category Theory), M1.RF (Refinement Algebra),
M4.COMP (Compiler Theory). Proofs: P2, P3, P6, P8.

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

COMPONENT 7 — MATHEMATICS
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Foundation / Semantic Role: The universal formal
substrate. Provides theories, algorithms, solvers, and numerical methods
for all 50 components.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type MathState = { loaded_theories:
Map[TheoryID, TheoryArtifact], active_algorithms: Map[AlgorithmID,
AlgorithmArtifact], solver_pool: Map[SolverID, SolverArtifact],
numerical_cache: Map[ProblemSignature, NumericalResult],
dimensional_registry: Map[Unit, Dimension], complexity_bounds:
Map[AlgorithmID, ComplexityClass], last_calibration: Time }

Artifacts: TheoryArtifact = { theory_id: Hash, name: String,
formal_statement: GSL_Expr, domain: Domain, mathematical_structure: M0 |
M1 | M2 | M3 | M4, assumptions: Set[Assumption],
applicability_conditions: Set[Predicate], validity_domain: Scope,
proof_basis: ProofCertificate, empirical_basis: EvidenceSet, algorithms:
Set[AlgorithmRef], solvers: Set[SolverRef], error_bounds: Map[Variable,
Float], known_failures: Set[FailureMode], fallback_theories:
Set[TheoryRef], provenance: Provenance, version: SemVer, lifecycle:
HYPOTHESIS | FORMALIZING | CONDITIONALLY_VERIFIED | VALIDATED }

AlgorithmArtifact = { algorithm_id: Hash, theory_ref: TheoryRef,
complexity: ComplexityClass, implementation_refs: Set[CodeRef],
precision: Float, performance: Map[Resource, Float] }

SolverArtifact = { solver_id: Hash, algorithm_ref: AlgorithmRef,
constraints: Set[Constraint], precision: Float, performance:
Map[Resource, Float] }

NumericalResult = { problem: ProblemSignature, value: Represented<ValuePayload>,
error_estimate: Represented<Error>, confidence: Probability?, method: AlgorithmRef,
assumptions: Set[Assumption], convergence_status, residual?, precision, provenance, timestamp: Time }

Functions: SelectTheory(problem: ProblemSignature) → TheoryArtifact
Precondition: Problem must be GSL-typed. Postcondition: Returns the
best-fitting theory (via Reason 50). Contract condition: Theory ≠ Algorithm ≠
Solver ≠ Execution.

ApplySolver(theory: TheoryRef, inputs: Set[Value]) → NumericalResult
Precondition: Solver must be applicable to the theory domain.
Postcondition: Returns result with error bounds and confidence.
Operational condition: Dimensional consistency enforced (M1.U: 5kg ≠ 5kWh).

VerifyProof(theory: TheoryRef, proof: ProofTerm) → Bool Precondition:
Proof must be syntactically valid. Postcondition: Returns TRUE only if
proof is structurally sound. Contract condition: Math supplies formal machinery;
Verity decides proof validity.

ComposeTheory(t1: TheoryRef, t2: TheoryRef) → TheoryArtifact
Precondition: Theories must be compatible (assumptions, domains, units).
Postcondition: Returns a composite theory with merged invariants.
Operational condition: Composition preserves P6 (associative/commutative where
applicable).

Update Protocol: Theory: HYPOTHESIS → FORMALIZING →
CONDITIONALLY_VERIFIED → VALIDATED. Solver: REGISTERED → CALIBRATED →
QUARANTINED (if drift detected). Algorithm: DRAFT → TESTED → APPROVED
(by Verity).

Invariant references: MATH-001, MATH-002, MATH-003, MATH-004, MATH-005.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Knowledge(10), Reasoning(50), Research(49).
Write: Knowledge(10), Events(45). Math: M0-M4 (all subdomains). M1.U,
M1.R, M3.CA, M4.OPT, etc. Proofs: P2, P3, P5, P12.

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Representation rule: semantic meaning and numerical representation are orthogonal. Mathematics(7) implements the Mathematical Representation Policy defined in Part III; fixed Float/Decimal encodings are domain implementations, not universal semantic meaning.

COMPONENT 8 — VERITY
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Foundation / Epistemic / Trust Anchor Role: The formal
proof engine. Discharges P1–P24. Proves structural soundness, NOT
empirical truth. Part of the Trust Anchor.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type VerityState = { proof_obligations:
Map[ObligationID, ProofObligation], discharged_certificates:
Map[ObligationID, ProofCertificate], counterexample_registry:
Map[ObligationID, CounterExample], validation_profiles: Map[Entity,
ValidationProfile], theorem_dependency_graph: DAG, prover_config:
ProverConfig, last_proof_attempt: Time, trust_root_measurement: Hash }

Artifacts: ProofObligation = { obligation_id: Hash, statement: GSL_Expr,
dependencies: Set[ObligationID], required_assumptions: Set[Assumption],
timeout: Duration, proof_class: THEOREM | LEMMA | INVARIANT |
MODEL_CHECK, status: PENDING | ATTEMPTED | DISCHARGED | FAILED | BLOCKED
}

ProofCertificate = { certificate_id: Hash, obligation_ref: ObligationID,
proof_term: Bytes, assumptions_used: Set[Assumption], verifier_version:
SemVer, discharged_at: Time, signature: Signature }

CounterExample = { obligation_ref: ObligationID, counterexample:
GSL_Expr, trace: ExecutionTrace, severity: CRITICAL | HIGH | MEDIUM |
LOW, found_at: Time }

ValidationProfile = { entity: Entity, formal_status: PROVEN |
CONDITIONALLY_PROVEN | UNPROVEN | REFUTED, empirical_status: VALIDATED |
PARTIALLY_VALIDATED | UNVALIDATED | REFUTED, statistical_status:SUPPORTED | INCONCLUSIVE | CONTRADICTED, testimonial_status: ATTESTED |
UNATTESTED, inferential_status: SOUND | UNSOUND, hypothetical_status:
PLAUSIBLE | UNTESTED | FALSIFIED }

Functions: Verify(obligation: ProofObligation) → ProofCertificate |
CounterExample Precondition: Obligation must be well-formed (GSL typed).
Postcondition: Returns certificate if proof is sound; else
counterexample. Contract condition: Verity proves structural soundness, NOT
empirical truth (K-INT-007).

Discharge(obligation: ProofObligation) → Bool Precondition: All
dependencies must be DISCHARGED. Postcondition: Attempts proof via
theorem prover (SMT/Coq/Lean). Contract condition: Proof checker is minimal Trust
Anchor; cannot be modified by Evolution.

Falsify(obligation: ProofObligation, timeout: Duration) → CounterExample
| None Precondition: Obligation must be active. Postcondition: Searches
for counterexample; returns None if none found. Contract condition: Falsification
survival ≠ proof (only CONDITIONALLY_APPLICABLE).

Validate(artifact: Artifact, profile: ValidationProfile) → Bool
Precondition: Artifact must have evidence attached. Postcondition:
Updates ValidationProfile based on available proofs/evidence. Contract condition:
Governance cannot change epistemic truth (K-INT-008).

Update Protocol: Obligation: PENDING → ATTEMPTED → DISCHARGED | FAILED |
BLOCKED. Certificate: GENERATED → VERIFIED → REVOKED (if new evidence
contradicts).

Invariant references: VER-001, VER-002, VER-003, VER-004, VER-005.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Language(6), Mathematics(7), Knowledge(10),
Reasoning(50). Write: Knowledge(10), Events(45). Math: M0 (Proof Theory,
FOL, Hoare Logic), M3.PR (Statistical verification), M4.COMP (Complexity
bounds). Proofs: P2, P6, P8.

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

COMPONENT 9 — SAFETY
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Foundation / Operational / Trust Anchor Role: The
absolute hardware-enforced safety veto. Lexical priority over all other
concerns. Part of the Trust Anchor.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type SafetyState = { active_envelope:
SafetyEnvelope, historical_envelopes: List[SafetyEnvelope],
interrupt_state: K0P_Interrupt, controlled_actuation_log:
List[ControlledActuationRecord], envelope_health: Map[EnvelopeID,
HealthStatus], last_hardware_diagnostic: Time, fallback_plans:
Map[EnvelopeID, FallbackPlan] }

Artifacts: SafetyEnvelope = { envelope_id: Hash, safe_set: Set[State],
boundary: Map[Variable, (Float, Float)], deadline: Duration,
fallback_plan: FallbackPlan, active_measurements: Map[SensorID, Float],
version: SemVer, status: VALID | STALE | OVERRIDDEN }

K0P_Interrupt = { interrupt_id: Hash, source: SensorID | ActuatorID,
timestamp: Time, action: ABORT | FALLBACK | EMERGENCY_STOP, proof:
ProofCertificate, severity: FATAL | CRITICAL | WARNING }

ControlledActuationRecord = { actuation_id: Hash, path_verified: Bool,
timestamp: Time, fallback_triggered: Bool, outcome: SAFE | UNSAFE |
ABORTED }

Functions: Enforce(action: Action) → Outcome Precondition: Action must
be GSL-typed. Postcondition: Checks all actuation paths against K0P. If
violation → IMMEDIATE KILL/FALLBACK. Contract condition: ∀ physical actuation
path (online/offline) must pass K0P (SAF-002).

Monitor(envelope: SafetyEnvelope) → HealthStatus Precondition: Envelope
must be active. Postcondition: Continuously checks sensors against
envelope.boundary. Contract condition: If envelope breached → K0P_Interrupt
triggered.

Bypass(emergency: EmergencyContract) → Bool Precondition: Must be
Emergency(38) with valid Authorization. Postcondition: May bypass
Policy/AuthZ, but NEVER K0P/Constitution. Contract condition: Emergency bypass is
bounded, audited, and auto-expires (max 300s).

Audit(actuation: ControlledActuationRecord) → Bool Precondition:
Actuation record must exist. Postcondition: Verifies that K0P was
enforced; generates ZKEAP proof. Contract condition: Post-execution log is
immutable and auditable.

Update Protocol: Envelope: VALID → STALE (sensor drift) → UPDATED
(recalibration) → OVERRIDDEN (only during Emergency). Interrupt: IDLE →
TRIGGERED → HANDLED → RESOLVED. Actuation: REQUESTED → VERIFIED →
EXECUTED (if K0P passes) | ABORTED (if fails).

Invariant references: SAF-001..008.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Hardware(16), Time(20), Mobility(23),
Energy(25), Industry(27), Emergency(38). Write: Events(45),
Knowledge(10). Math: M2 (Control Theory, Differential Equations), M4.OPT
(Safety constraints lexically prior), M3.PR (Probabilistic safety
margins). Proofs: P1, P4, P4a, P19.

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

COMPONENT 10 — KNOWLEDGE
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Foundation / Epistemic / Epistemic Trust Substrate Role:
The immutable recording substrate for Observations, Evidence, Claims,
Theories, Patterns, Traces, and Snapshots. Enforces K-INT-001–015.
Stores audit evidence but is NOT the sole validation authority.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type KnowledgeState = { artifact_store:
Map[ArtifactID, StoredArtifact], interpretation_layer: Map[ArtifactID,
Interpretation], snapshot_registry: Map[SnapshotID, KnowledgeSnapshot],
consistency_sweep_log: List[SweepEvent], epistemic_dependency_graph:
Graph, usage_metrics: Map[ArtifactID, UsageStats],
novelty_detection_queue: Queue[Anomaly], last_sweep: Time, version:
SemVer }

Artifacts: StoredArtifact = { artifact_id: Hash, type: OBSERVATION |
EVIDENCE_CANDIDATE | VALIDATED_EVIDENCE | CLAIM | PATTERN | THEORY |
TRACE | SNAPSHOT | AUDIT_EVIDENCE, payload_hash: Hash, timestamp: Time,
provenance: Provenance, authority: Authorization, validity_scope: Scope,
version: SemVer }

Interpretation = { artifact_ref: ArtifactID, current_status: ACTIVE |
RETRACTED | SUPERSEDED | CONTRADICTED | UNKNOWN, epistemic_status:
EpistemicStatus, status_history: List[(Status, Time, Reason)] }

UsageStats = { artifact_ref: ArtifactID, query_count: UInt,
citation_count: UInt, conflict_count: UInt, last_accessed: Time,
freshness_score: Float }

AuditEvidence = { evidence_id: Hash, gsscc_id: GSSCC_ID, source: System
| Entity, test_or_audit: “Sweep” | “Provenance” | “Compliance” |
“Adversarial”, timestamp: Time, result: PASS | FAIL | INCONCLUSIVE,
artifact_refs: Set[ArtifactID], proof_refs: Set[ProofCertificate],
version: SemVer, signature: Signature }

UncertaintyPropagationProfile = { dependency_structure: Graph,
correlation_assumptions: [AssumptionRef], epistemic_component: Float,
aleatoric_component: Float, calibration_method: MethodRef,
propagation_rule: SUM | PRODUCT | DEPENDENT_JOIN | MONTE_CARLO,
error_bound: Float }

EpistemicDebt = { stale_evidence_weight: Float, unresolved_conflicts:
[ConflictRef], correlated_evidence_ratio: Float, assumption_expiry_days:
Float, unverified_transformations: Float, total_debt_score: Float }

EpistemicDebt Semantics: total_debt_score is a bounded aggregate of
stale evidence, unresolved conflicts, correlated evidence, expired
assumptions, and unverified transformations. The metric is an
optimization-pressure signal, not an epistemic promotion signal. If
total_debt_score > configured threshold, Knowledge MUST expose the debt
to Evolution and a mandatory ImprovementCycle MUST be admitted through
Research(49) and/or Reasoning(50), subject to the normal authority,
safety, proof, budget, and rollback gates. Low debt does NOT by itself
justify a change.

Functions: Ingest(artifact: StoredArtifact, authority: Authorization) →
ArtifactID Precondition: Authority must be valid (Security 22).
Provenance must be attached. Postcondition: Artifact appended to
immutable store. Returns ArtifactID. Contract condition: Records are append-only.
No deletion (K-INT-005).

Validate(artifact: ArtifactID, gate: ValidationGate) → Bool
Precondition: Artifact must exist. Gate must be Verity(8) or
Research(49). Postcondition: If passes, artifact promoted to
VALIDATED_EVIDENCE or CLAIM. Contract condition: ModelGenerated artifacts cannot
be promoted without external orthogonal attestation (K-INT-007).

UpdateInterpretation(artifact: ArtifactID, new_status: Status, reason:
Reason) → Interpretation Precondition: Artifact must exist. Reason must
be evidenced. Postcondition: Interpretation layer updated with new
status; history appended. Contract condition: Record monotonicity ≠ belief
monotonicity. The artifact remains; its status changes.

Snapshot(epoch: Time, event_boundary: EventID) → KnowledgeSnapshot
Precondition: Epoch must be > last snapshot. Postcondition: Frozen view
of interpretation layer created. Contract condition: Snapshot is immutable and
consistent.

ConsistencySweep() → List[Anomaly] Precondition: Called periodically
(K-INT-003). Postcondition: Detects contradictions, staleness, and
unresolved conflicts. Contract condition: Anomalies escalate to
Governance/Research (K-INT-003).

Update Protocol: Artifact Lifecycle: OBSERVATION → EVIDENCE_CANDIDATE
(if ModelGenerated) → VALIDATED_EVIDENCE (via Verity/Research) → CLAIM →
KNOWLEDGE. Retraction: ACTIVE → RETRACTED (tombstone, key destruction).
Supersession: ACTIVE → SUPERSEDED (new version linked). Contradiction:
ACTIVE → CONFLICTED (resolution via Verity/Research). Unknown: UNKNOWN
remains UNKNOWN (never collapses).

Invariant references: KNW-001, K-INT-001, KNW-002, K-INT-002, KNW-003, K-INT-003, KNW-004, K-INT-004, KNW-005, K-INT-005, KNW-006, K-INT-006, KNW-007, K-INT-007, KNW-008, K-INT-008, KNW-009, K-INT-009, KNW-010, KNW-011, KNW-012, KNW-013, KNW-014, KNW-015.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Reasoning(50), Search(31), Explanation(35),
Verity(8), Research(49), Time(20). Write: Curation(21), Events(45),
Compliance(34). Math: M3.PR (Epistemic Lattice), M3.CA (Causal
Inference), M1.GR (Graph Theory), M3.PV (Provenance), M4.CRYPTO
(Signatures, hashes). Proofs: P1, P2, P3, P7, P14, P20.

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

LATE EPISTEMIC MAINTENANCE BINDING
Knowledge(10) is the primary state carrier for Continuous Epistemic Revalidation and the Epistemic Sweep/Saturation profile defined in Part IV. Its implementation therefore also maintains revalidation metadata, invalidation/dependency triggers, SweepRecord history and SaturationProfile references. Acceptance once is not permanent trust.

COMPONENT 11 — CONSTITUTION
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Universal Module Contract
Purpose      : Highest-order human-rights and constitutional rule registry; hardware-rooted veto boundary.
Inputs       : GSL-typed proposed actions/transitions, RightRecords, amendment proposals, proofs, valid governance records.
Outputs      : PASS | VETO | INCONCLUSIVE, constitutional version, registry root, amendment history.
Rules        : Constitution > Law > Policy > Decision; Emergency never overrides Constitution; reserved/on-hold rights have no invented semantics.
Failure      : Any active-right violation → VETO; unresolved constitutional applicability → low-authority safe state/escalation.
Dependencies : Safety(9), Hardware(16), Verity(8), Governance(30), Law(12), Policy(48), Knowledge(10), Time(20).
Why          : Optimization, voting, model capability or emergency must never silently become authority over fundamental rights.
Status       : RIGHTS 001–027 registry current; RIGHT-024 reserved; RIGHT-025 deliberately ON HOLD.

RightStatus = ACTIVE | RESERVED | ON_HOLD | SUPERSEDED.
RightID = stable versioned identifier such as RIGHT-001 … RIGHT-027; registry is extensible and is NOT hard-coded to a 16-bit mask.

RightRecord = {
  right_id: RightID,
  name: String,
  description: String?,
  predicate: GSL_Expr? ,
  status: RightStatus,
  enforcement: HARD_VETO | GOVERNED | NONE,
  valid_from: Time,
  supersedes: RightID?,
  provenance: Provenance,
  version: SemVer
}
ConstitutionState = {
  active_version: SemVer,
  rights_registry: Map[RightID, RightRecord],
  active_right_ids: Set[RightID],
  registry_root: Hash,
  constitutional_gate_hash: Hash,
  amendment_history: List[AmendmentRecord],
  pending_amendments: List[AmendmentProposal],
  last_veto: Time
}
ConstitutionalGate = { gate_id, registry_root, active_right_set_hash, hardware_signature, last_verified }
AmendmentRecord = { amendment_id, proposal, vote_result, escrow_proof?, ratification_time, old_registry_root, new_registry_root, version, provenance }

Functions:
Check(action_or_transition) → PASS | VETO | INCONCLUSIVE
  Evaluates every applicable ACTIVE right; RESERVED/ON_HOLD slots have no predicate and cannot be guessed.
Veto(action_or_transition) → Bool
  Any applicable hard-right violation blocks execution before ordinary optimization.
Amend(proposal, votes, proof) → AmendmentRecord
  Historical profile: ≥90% quorum and ≥6 months deliberation for constitutional amendment, subject to valid constitutional process and proof. Old versions remain immutable.
VerifyRegistry() → Bool
  Hardware/Verity check of registry root, version lineage and active-right set.

Invariant references: CON-RT-001, RIGHT-017, CON-RT-002, RIGHT-024, CON-RT-003, RIGHT-025, CON-RT-004, CON-RT-005, CON-RT-006, CON-RT-007.

Math: M0 formal logic/Hoare contracts; M1.GR version/precedence DAG; M4.CRYPTO registry roots/attestation.
Proofs: P4a, P6, P14, P16, P19.

COMPONENT 12 — LAW
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Constitutional / Normative Role: Evolvable normative
rules. Jurisdiction resolution. Conflict-of-laws lexicographic
precedence. Law ≤ Constitution.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type LawState = { active_rules: Map[RuleID,
LawRule], jurisdiction_map: Map[Jurisdiction, Set[RuleID]],
precedence_graph: DAG, conflict_resolution_log:
List[ConflictResolution], version: SemVer, last_amendment: Time }

Artifacts: LawRule = { rule_id: Hash, jurisdiction: Jurisdiction,
predicate: GSL_Expr → Bool, precedence: CONSTITUTION | LAW | POLICY |
DECISION, consequence: PERMITTED | PROHIBITED | MANDATORY, version:
SemVer, source: GovernanceRef | HumanProposal }

JurisdictionMap = { jurisdiction: Jurisdiction, applicable_laws:
Set[RuleID], conflict_resolution_strategy: LEXICOGRAPHIC | GOVERNANCE |
CUSTOM }

ComplianceMapping = { mapping_id: Hash, external_standard: URI,
external_content_hash, publisher_signature?, retrieval_metadata, monitoring_policy,
internal_rule: LawRuleRef, equivalence_proof: ProofCertificate, version: SemVer,
validity, invalidation_triggers }

Functions: Resolve(context: Context, action: Action) → RuleSet
Precondition: Context must include jurisdiction. Postcondition: Returns
applicable set of laws (lexicographic precedence). Contract condition:
Constitution > Law > Policy > Decision.

ConflictResolution(rule1: LawRule, rule2: LawRule, context: Context) →
LawRule Precondition: Both rules must apply to the same action.
Postcondition: Returns the higher-precedence rule. Equal-rank contradictory MANDATORY/PROHIBITED rules
produce LEGAL_CONFLICT/UNRESOLVED with the conflict set, affected jurisdictions and escalation path.
Governance or a competent legal authority may select an operational policy but cannot declare one rule
epistemically true. High-impact action remains blocked until lawfully resolved; explicitly authorized
low-risk continuity may use the intersection of permissions.

Amend(proposal: LawAmendment) → LawRule Precondition: Must not violate
Constitution/K0P (K-INT-011). Postcondition: New law added or existing
law updated (67% quorum). Contract condition: Law ≤ Constitution. Law cannot
degrade Reason/Verity (K-INT-008).

Update Protocol: LawRule: DRAFT → PROPOSED → VOTED → ACTIVE → REPEALED |
SUPERSEDED.

Invariant references: LAW-001, LAW-002, K-INT-008, LAW-003, LAW-004.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Constitution(11), Governance(30), Policy(48),
Locale(40). Write: Knowledge(10), Events(45). Math: M1.GR (Graph
Theory), M0 (Rule Algebra, Deontic Logic), M3.LE (Learning). Proofs: P4,
P6, P16.

LAYER 3 — OPERATIONAL (10)
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

COMPONENT 13 — RUNTIME
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Operational / Infrastructure Role: Orchestrates
WorkPackages. Schedules tasks (priority/deadline). Allocates resources.
Manages circuit breakers. Enforces RuntimeState ⊨ ComponentSpec.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type RuntimeState = { active_workpackages:
Map[WorkPackageID, WorkPackage], resource_pool: ResourcePool,
circuit_breakers: Map[BreakerID, CircuitBreakerState], schedule_queue:
PriorityQueue, active_contexts: Set[ExecutionContext],
resource_allocations: Map[TaskID, ResourceTicket], failure_counter:
Map[ComponentID, UInt], last_gc: Time }

Artifacts: WorkPackage = { package_id: Hash, tasks: List[Task],
dependencies: DAG
, deadline: Time, resource_requirements: ResourceLimits, priority: UInt,
status: PENDING | SCHEDULED | RUNNING | COMPLETED | FAILED | ABORTED }

ResourceTicket = { ticket_id: Hash, resource_type: CPU | Memory | Energy
| Bandwidth | Storage, quantity: Float, allocated_to: TaskID, expiry:
Time, priority: UInt }

CircuitBreakerState = { breaker_id: Hash, state: CLOSED | OPEN |
HALF_OPEN, failure_count: UInt, last_trip: Time, reset_threshold: UInt }

ExecutionAdmissionToken = { workpackage_hash, code_hash, proof_and_certificate_roots,
DesignEpoch, environment_commitment, authority_scope, risk_class, validity, revocation_root,
hardware_verifier_profile, signature }

Functions: Schedule(workpackage: WorkPackage) → ScheduleResult
Precondition: Resource requirements must be ≤ available pool.
Postcondition: Tasks assigned to resources respecting priority/deadline.
Operational condition: Deadlines met or WorkPackage aborted.

Allocate(resource: ResourceType, quantity: Float, task: TaskID) →
ResourceTicket Precondition: Request must be authorized by Policy(48).
Postcondition: Resource reserved; returns ticket. Fail if insufficient.
Operational condition: Total allocated ≤ total capacity (no oversubscription).

Trip(breaker_id: BreakerID) → Bool Precondition: Failure threshold
exceeded. Postcondition: Breaker moves to OPEN; halts new work.
Operational condition: Circuit breakers prevent cascading failures.

Monitor(task: TaskID) → HealthStatus Precondition: Task must be RUNNING.
Postcondition: Detects timeouts, resource exhaustion, stalls. Contract condition:
If timeout → abort; log to Events(45).

Update Protocol: WorkPackage: PENDING → SCHEDULED → RUNNING → COMPLETED
| FAILED | ABORTED. CircuitBreaker: CLOSED → OPEN → HALF_OPEN → CLOSED
(after cooldown).

Invariant references: RTE-001, RTE-002, RTE-003, RTE-004, RTE-005, RTE-006, RTE-007.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Knowledge(10), Time(20), Economy(3),
Dependency(46), Execution(14). Write: Events(45), Knowledge(10). Math:
M4.OPT (Scheduling Algorithms), M3.LE (Queuing Theory), M1.GR (Task
Graph DAG). Proofs: P1, P6, P5L, P7.

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

COMPONENT 14 — EXECUTION
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Operational / Infrastructure Role: Dispatches authorized
WorkPackages. Enforces idempotency (AT_LEAST_ONCE), atomic transactions
(ALL or NOTHING), and outcome tracking. Final gate before K0P.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type ExecuteState = { active_executions:
Map[ExecutionID, ExecutionContext], transaction_log:
List[ExecutionReceipt], pending_commits: Map[TransactionID, TxState],
idempotency_cache: Map[ActionHash, ExecutionReceipt],
compensation_stack: Map[TransactionID, CompensationPlan], last_commit:
Time, active_rollbacks: Set[TransactionID] }

Artifacts: ExecutionContext = { exec_id: Hash, workpackage_id:
WorkPackageID, preconditions: Set[Predicate], actions: List[Action],
rollback_plan: CompensationPlan, timeout: Duration, status: PREPARED |
STARTED | PARTIAL | COMPLETED | FAILED | COMPENSATED | ABORTED | UNKNOWN
}

CP1_Certificate = { code_hash, param_schema, param_range, effect_set,
resource_bound, safety_env, deadline, env_assumptions,
environment_commitment } Rule: IF environment_commitment changes → CP1
invalid → escalate to CP2.

ExecutionReceipt = { receipt_id: Hash, execution_ref: ExecutionID,
outcome: Outcome, result_hash: Hash, timestamp: Time, proof:
ProofCertificate, signature: Signature }

CompensationPlan = { transaction_id: Hash, steps: List[Action], status:
PENDING | EXECUTED | FAILED }

Functions: Prepare(workpackage: WorkPackage) → ExecutionContext
Precondition: WorkPackage must be authorized, carry a locally verifiable ExecutionAdmissionToken,
pass the applicable proof/evidence gate and pass K0P. Postcondition: ExecutionContext created with
status=PREPARED. Emergency uses only pre-authorized emergency contracts and local K0P; it is not a
proof-free bypass. Irreversible actions require an irreversibility and recovery plan.

Execute(execution: ExecutionContext) → Outcome Precondition: Execution
must have status=PREPARED or STARTED. Postcondition: Performs actions
atomically only while effects remain logically reversible. External and irreversible effects use
prepare/commit barriers, idempotency, bounded partial-failure states and compensation. Contract condition: K0P choke point
mandatory for all ControlledActuation (SAF-002).

Commit(execution: ExecutionContext) → ExecutionReceipt Precondition: All
actions must be COMPLETED. Postcondition: State changes finalized;
receipt appended to Knowledge(10). Contract condition: Irreversible actions
require explicit audit trail.

Rollback(execution: ExecutionContext) → Bool Precondition: Execution
must be FAILED or ABORTED. Postcondition: CompensationPlan executed;
reversible software state is restored where possible and committed external effects are addressed.
The receipt states RESTORED, COMPENSATED, REMEDIATION_REQUIRED or IRREVERSIBLE_RESIDUAL. Compensation
is never mislabeled as restoration of the physical past.

Audit(receipt: ExecutionReceipt) → Bool Precondition: Receipt must
exist. Postcondition: Verifies proof against Verity(8) and logs to
Events(45). Contract condition: Audit logs are immutable and tamper-evident.

Update Protocol: Execution: PREPARED → STARTED → PARTIAL → COMPLETED |
FAILED → COMPENSATED (rollback) | ABORTED. Transaction: PENDING →
COMMITTED (irreversible) | ROLLED_BACK.

Invariant references: EXE-001, EXE-002, EXE-003, EXE-004, EXE-005, EXE-006.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Runtime(13), Safety(9), Knowledge(10),
Verity(8), Authority(1/22). Write: Events(45), Knowledge(10). Math: M0
(State-Transition Systems), M4.CRYPTO (Signatures), M3.PR (Probabilistic
outcome tracking). Proofs: P1, P4, P9.

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

COMPONENT 15 — NATIVE
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Operational / Infrastructure / Trust Anchor Role: The
stack-based GSL bytecode VM. Sandboxed, gas-metered, deterministic.
Enforces NAT-001..010 and the current native safety contracts. Part of the Trust Anchor.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type NativeState = { current_context:
ExecutionContext, gas_meter: GasMeter, loaded_bytecode: Map[Hash,
GNR_Bytecode], sandbox_policies: Map[MemoryRegion, SandboxRule],
instruction_cache: Map[PC, DecodedInstr], active_syscalls:
List[SyscallRecord], last_attestation: Time }

Artifacts: GNR_Bytecode = { code: Bytes, entry_point: UInt, stack_size:
UInt, memory_limit: UInt, version: SemVer, hash: Hash }

ExecutionContext = { context_id: Hash, caller: EntityRef, gas_remaining:
UInt, stack: Stack, memory: Memory, program_counter: UInt, flags:
StatusFlags, ctr: CapabilityTagRegister }

GasMeter = { initial_gas: UInt, consumed: UInt, remaining: UInt,
instruction_count: UInt }

Functions: Load(bytecode: GNR_Bytecode, context: ExecutionContext) →
Bool Precondition: Bytecode must be signed and verified by
Compilation(42). Postcondition: Bytecode loaded into isolated memory.
Returns success. Contract condition: Code segment is read-only. Self-modification
→ abort.

Interpret() → Outcome Precondition: ExecutionContext must be active.
Postcondition: Fetch-decode-execute loop. Continues until HALT or gas
exhaustion. Contract condition: Deterministic (same input → same output,
excluding external IO).

Sandbox(syscall: Syscall, context: ExecutionContext) → Bool
Precondition: Syscall must be whitelisted. Postcondition: Returns TRUE
if syscall is permitted by current CapabilityToken. Contract condition: No kernel
access. Memory bounds enforced (SEC-014).

Attest(execution: ExecutionContext) → AttestationReport Precondition:
Execution must be complete. Postcondition: Returns hardware-signed
report (hash, gas, output). Contract condition: Attestation is verifiable offline
by Security(22).

Update Protocol: Bytecode: LOADED → VERIFIED → EXECUTING → HALTED |
OUT_OF_GAS | FAULTED. Context: IDLE → RUNNING → PAUSED → RESUME →
TERMINATED.

Invariant references: NAT-001..010.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Hardware(16), Language(6), Compilation(42),
Security(22), Runtime(13), Time(20). Write: Knowledge(10), Events(45).
Math: M0 (Type Safety, Stack Semantics), M4.COMP (Complexity), M4.CRYPTO
(Hardware attestation). Proofs: P1, P4, P6, P19.

COMPONENT 16 — HARDWARE
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Infrastructure / Trust Anchor Role: The physical
substrate. Provides CPU, memory, storage, sensors, actuators. Digital
Twin calibration. OTP secure boot. K0P enforcement. Physical Root of
Trust.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type HardwareState = { device_id: DeviceID,
device_model: DeviceModel, digital_twin: DigitalTwin,
physical_measurements: Map[SensorID, Measurement], actuator_state:
Map[ActuatorID, ActuatorState], otp_hash: Hash, tpm_attestation:
AttestationReport, thermal_profile: ThermalState, last_diagnostic: Time
}

Artifacts: DeviceModel = { device_id: Hash, hardware_version: SemVer,
capabilities: Set[Capability], calibration_data: CalibrationRecord,
thermal_limits: (Float, Float) }

DigitalTwin = { model_id: Hash, physical_ref: DeviceRef,
state_estimates: Map[Variable, Dist], fault_predictions: Map[Component,
Probability], snapshot_epoch: UInt, calibration_expiry: Time, drift_budget,
last_physical_recalibration: Time, last_sync: Time }

Measurement = { sensor_id: SensorID, value: Float, unit: Unit,
uncertainty: Float, timestamp: Time, calibration_ref: CalibrationID }

Functions: Boot() → Bool Precondition: OTP hash must match secure boot
firmware. Postcondition: Hardware initializes. TPM attestation
generated. Contract condition: OTP immutable. Only signed firmware loads
(A-PHYS).

Sense(sensor: SensorID) → Measurement Precondition: Sensor must be
active and calibrated. Postcondition: Returns timestamped measurement
with uncertainty. Contract condition: Digital twin error bounded (A-PHYS).

Actuate(actuator: ActuatorID, command: Command) → Outcome Precondition:
Command must pass K0P gate (Safety 9). Postcondition: Physical action
executed; event logged. Contract condition: Every ControlledActuation path passes
K0P (SAF-002).

SelfTest() → DiagnosticReport Precondition: Hardware must be idle and the calibration environment must
be inside a declared stable thermal envelope. Active thermal excursions defer baseline replacement and
may update only an incident profile. Postcondition: Runs diagnostics; updates thermal_profile. Contract condition: If
failure detected → trigger Emergency(38).

Update Protocol: Hardware: BOOTING → READY → RUNNING → FAULTED
(escalate). DigitalTwin: SYNC → UPDATED → CALIBRATED. Firmware: LOADED →
VERIFIED → EXECUTING → ROLLBACK (if corrupt).

Invariant references: HWR-001, HWR-002, HWR-003, HWR-004, HWR-005, HWR-006.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Mathematics(7), Safety(9), Fabric(18),
Security(22), Time(20), Native(15). Write: Events(45), Knowledge(10).
Math: M2 (Thermal Dynamics), M3.PR (Measurement uncertainty), M4.CRYPTO
(OTP, TPM, PQC). Proofs: P1, P4, P19.

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

COMPONENT 17 — NETWORK
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Infrastructure / Operational Role: Provides secure,
authenticated, and timely communication between Garden nodes and
external systems. Enforces PQC, QoS, and revocation propagation.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type NetworkState = { active_sessions:
Map[SessionID, NetworkSession], route_table: RouteTable,
pending_packets: Queue[NetworkPacket], qkd_session_pool:
Map[QKDSessionID, QKDSession], revocation_cache: CuckooFilter,
connection_health: Map[PeerID, HealthStatus], last_sync: Time,
partition_status: CONNECTED | PARTITIONED | OFFLINE }

Artifacts: NetworkPacket = { packet_id: Hash, header: PacketHeader,
payload: Bytes, signature: Signature, timestamp: Time }

QKDSession = { session_id: Hash, endpoint1: EntityRef, endpoint2:
EntityRef, keys: List[Key], expiry: Time, quantum_channel_ref:
ChannelRef }

RouteTable = { destination: IP | GardenAddr, next_hop: EntityRef |
DeviceRef, metric: Float, validity: Time, signature: Signature }

Functions: Send(packet: NetworkPacket, destination: EntityRef) → Bool
Precondition: Packet must be signed; destination must be reachable.
Postcondition: Packet routed to destination; QoS/priority applied.
Operational condition: All comms authenticated and encrypted (PQC). No cleartext
PII.

Receive(packet: NetworkPacket) → Bytes Precondition: Packet must pass
integrity and authentication checks. Postcondition: Decrypted payload
delivered to component. Contract condition: A Cuckoo filter is only a fast hint. Saturation or a positive result
invokes a signed exact revocation structure before consequential rejection; revoked senders remain blocked.

SyncTime(peer: EntityRef) → Time Precondition: Peer must be trusted
(Security 22). Postcondition: Synchronizes HLC (Hybrid Logical Clock)
via PTP/GNSS. Contract condition: Drift <1ms for R4/AUTO domains.

Revoke(entity: EntityRef) → Bool Precondition: Caller must be
Security(22) or Governance(30). Postcondition: Revocation propagated to
all nodes through signed epoch roots plus dynamically resized/rotated filters. Load monitoring, exact
fallback and re-indexing prevent saturation from becoming permanent node isolation.

Update Protocol: Session: HANDSHAKE → ESTABLISHED → EXPIRED | REVOKED.
Packet: QUEUED → ROUTED → DELIVERED → ACKNOWLEDGED. Partition: CONNECTED
→ PARTITIONED → RECONNECTING → RECONCILED.

Invariant references: NET-001, NET-002, NET-003, NET-004, NET-005, NET-006.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Hardware(16), Security(22), Time(20),
Fabric(18), Offline(47). Write: Events(45), Knowledge(10). Math: M1.GR
(Graph Theory), M3.PR (Probability), M4.CRYPTO (PQC, QKD, Signatures).
Proofs: P1, P6, P7, P11.

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

COMPONENT 18 — FABRIC
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Operational / Distributed Role: Provides distributed
state replication, consensus (CRDT/DRC/HEMT), and reconciliation across
Garden nodes.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type FabricState = { crdt_state: Map[CRDTID,
CRDT_State], drc_log: List[DRC_Event], hemt_root: Hash,
membership_epoch: UInt, pending_deltas: Queue[CRDT_Delta],
consistency_profile: ConsistencyProfile, last_merge: Time,
partition_state: Map[NodeID, PartitionStatus] }

Artifacts: CRDT_State = { type: CRDTType, payload: Bytes, delta: Bytes,
timestamp: Time }

DRC = { event_order: List[EventID], logical_time: HLC, causal_frontier:
Set[EventID], conflict_policy: CONFLICT_POLICY, merge_fn: MergeFunction,
membership_epoch: UInt, seed: Hash, version_set: Set[Version] }

HEMT_Root = { root_hash: Hash, epoch: UInt, timestamp: Time }

Functions: Replicate(state: CRDT_State, peers: Set[NodeID]) → Bool
Precondition: State must be validated. Postcondition: Deltas propagated
to peers. Contract condition: CRDT convergent.

Merge(state1: CRDT_State, state2: CRDT_State) → CRDT_State Precondition:
States must be compatible. Postcondition: Returns merged state via CRDT
merge function. Contract condition: Merge is associative, commutative,
idempotent.

Commit(state: CRDT_State) → HEMT_Root Precondition: State must be
converged. Postcondition: State appended to HEMT; new root generated.
Operational condition: HEMT async merging without global lock.

CommitProofCarryingState(manifest, state_chunks, obligation_chunks) → HEMT_Root
  No state becomes visible until every content-addressed state/proof chunk, environment commitment and
  ObligationGraph root is present and verified. Commit is an atomic manifest-pointer publication;
  incomplete serialization remains PENDING and cannot race ahead of its proof payload.

ResolveConflict(state1: CRDT_State, state2: CRDT_State) → CRDT_State
Precondition: Conflict detected. Postcondition: Conflict resolved via
DRC conflict_policy. Contract condition: Conflict resolution deterministic.

Update Protocol: CRDT: LOCAL → PENDING → MERGED → COMMITTED. HEMT:
APPEND → REHASH → ROOT_UPDATE.

Invariant references: FAB-001, FAB-002, FAB-003, FAB-004, FAB-005.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Network(17), Hardware(16), Security(22),
Time(20), Runtime(13). Write: Events(45), Knowledge(10). Math: M1.GR
(Graph Theory), M3.PR (Probabilistic consensus), M1.CONC
(Concurrency/Consistency). Proofs: P1, P6, P10a-f.

COMPONENT 20 — TIME
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Operational Role: Unified temporal model. Provides
timestamps, duration, ordering, and synchronization.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type TimeState = { physical_time: Float,
logical_time: UInt, hlc: HLC, validity_windows: Map[Entity, Time],
deadlines: Map[TaskID, Time], wcet: Map[ProcessID, Duration],
causal_graph: DAG, drift_bounds: (Float, Float), last_sync: Time }

Artifacts: TemporalContext = { event_time: Time, obs_time: Time,
proc_time: Time, commit_time: Time, validity_range: Interval }

HLC = { wall_clock: Float, logical_counter: UInt, node_id: NodeID }

TimestampedEvent = { id: Hash, time: Time, type: EventCategory, payload:
Bytes }

Functions: Now() → Time Precondition: None. Postcondition: Returns
current timestamp (physical + logical). Contract condition: Monotonic.

Order(event1: EventID, event2: EventID) → CausalOrder Precondition:
Events must exist. Postcondition: Returns causal order (BEFORE | AFTER |
CONCURRENT | UNKNOWN). Contract condition: If A happens-before B, then HLC(A) < HLC(B) in lexicographic logical
order. Physical wall timestamps may be equal or uncertain and do not alone determine causality.

Synchronize(peer: EntityRef) → Time Precondition: Peer must be trusted.
Postcondition: incorporates PTP/GNSS observations without stepping the exposed logical clock backward.
A hardware monotonic counter and HLC logical increment absorb physical step-backs; a ClockCorrectionReceipt
records uncertainty and the new synchronization epoch. Contract condition: declared drift bound or DEGRADED_TIME.

Validate(time: Time, scope: Scope) → Bool Precondition: Time must be
valid. Postcondition: Returns TRUE if time is within validity window.
Operational condition: Outside validity → STALE.

Update Protocol: Time: SYNC → UPDATED → VALIDATED. Deadline: SET →
MONITOR → MET | MISSED.

Invariant references: TIM-001, TIM-002, TIM-003, TIM-004.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Network(17), Hardware(16), Security(22),
Knowledge(10), Events(45). Write: Events(45), Knowledge(10). Math: M2
(Relativistic Lorentz), M3 (HLC causal ordering). Proofs: P1, P6.
