List[AssessmentResult], preferences: LearningPreferences }

Credential = { credential_id: Hash, level: L1 | L2 | L3 | L4 | L5 | L6,
zk_proof: ZKPProof, issuer: EntityRef, valid_from: Time, valid_until:
Time }

AssessmentResult = { assessment_id: Hash, learner: EntityRef, score:
Float, level: L1 | L2 | L3 | L4 | L5 | L6, timestamp: Time,
proctor_attestation: Signature }

Functions: Assess(learner: Entity, assessment: Assessment) →
AssessmentResult Precondition: Learner must be authenticated.
Postcondition: Assessment administered; score computed. Contract condition: No
memorization tests (requires reasoning/judgment).

IssueCredential(learner: Entity, level: Level) → Credential
Precondition: AssessmentResult must be validated. Postcondition: ZKP
credential issued; stored in Knowledge(10). Contract condition: Credentials
require ZKP; no plaintext grade sharing.

VerifyCredential(credential: Credential) → Bool Precondition: Credential
must exist. Postcondition: Returns TRUE if ZKP verifies. Contract condition: ZKP
proof reveals nothing beyond credential validity.

UpdateProtocol: Learner: REGISTERED → ASSESSED → CERTIFIED → GRADUATED.
Curriculum: DRAFT → PUBLISHED → UPDATED → RETIRED.

Invariant references: EDU-001, EDU-002, EDU-003, EDU-004.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Identity(1), Knowledge(10), Verity(8),
Governance(30), Privacy(2), Policy(48), Reasoning(50), Time(20). Write:
Events(45), Knowledge(10). Math: M3 (Assessment Statistics), M4 (ZKP -
Zero Knowledge Proofs). Proofs: P2, P6.

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
LAYER 5 — GOVERNANCE (1)
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

COMPONENT 30 — GOVERNANCE
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Constitutional Role: Collective decision-making, courts,
public administration.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type GovernanceState = { active_proposals:
Map[ProposalID, Proposal], vote_records: Map[ProposalID, VoteResult],
court_cases: Map[CaseID, CourtVerdict], assembly_records:
List[AssemblyRecord], quorum_status: QuorumStatus, jurisdiction:
Jurisdiction, last_election: Time }

Artifacts: Proposal = { proposal_id: Hash, proposer: EntityRef, text:
String, evidence_refs: Set[EvidenceID], type: ORDINARY | RIGHTS |
CONSTITUTIONAL, status: DRAFT | SUBMITTED | VOTING | PASSED | REJECTED }

Vote = { proposal_id: Hash, voter: EntityRef, choice: YES | NO |
ABSTAIN, weight: Float, timestamp: Time }

CourtVerdict = { case_id: Hash, defendant: EntityRef, plaintiff:
EntityRef, verdict: GUILTY | NOT_GUILTY | NOT_PROVEN | INCONCLUSIVE | MISTRIAL | DISMISSED,
sentence: Option<Sentence>, standard_of_proof, appeal_status,
evidence_used: Set[EvidenceID] }

AssemblyRecord = { session_id: Hash, type: UPPER | LOWER, decisions:
Set[Decision], quorum_met: Bool }

Functions: Propose(proposal: Proposal) → ProposalID Precondition:
Proposer must be authenticated. Postcondition: Proposal added to active
proposals. Contract condition: Quorum strict (51/67/90%).

Vote(proposal_id: ProposalID, vote: Vote) → Bool Precondition: Voter
must be eligible. Postcondition: Vote recorded; quorum checked.
Operational condition: Governance cannot declare epistemic truth (AUTH-EPI).

Adjudicate(case: CaseID) → CourtVerdict Precondition: Case must be
submitted. Postcondition: Verdict rendered (tier based on severity).
Operational condition: appeal procedure terminates under declared rules. Cyclic legal dependencies are represented
as strongly connected components and resolved through stratified/fixed-point semantics or explicit
LEGAL_CONFLICT; an unresolved semantic cycle is not executed.

Enact(decision: Decision) → Bool Precondition: Decision must be
approved. Postcondition: Law/Policy updated if applicable. Contract condition:
Governance Non-Amplification (vote ≠ epistemic truth).

Update Protocol: Proposal: DRAFT → SUBMITTED → VOTING → PASSED |
REJECTED. Court: FILED → HEARD → VERDICT → APPEALED | CLOSED.

Invariant references: GOV-001, GOV-002, GOV-003, GOV-004.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Constitution(11), Law(12), Policy(48),
Identity(1), Knowledge(10), Verity(8), Reasoning(50), Emergency(38).
Write: Knowledge(10), Events(45). Math: M3 (Social Choice, Voting
Theory, Quorum 51/67/90%), M1 (DAG - Acyclic appeals), M3 (Rule of 3).
Proofs: P4, P6, P16.

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
LAYER 6 — META (20)
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Governance-weight clarification: Vote.weight is a policy-specific mechanism, not human worth or epistemic truth. Fundamental rights are not reputation-weighted. Any weighted decision requires an explicit lawful/constitutional basis, evidence of eligibility, and due process.

COMPONENT 31 — SEARCH
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Meta Role: Universal relevance retrieval across all
Garden knowledge and components.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type SearchState = { embedding_index:
EmbeddingIndex, structural_index: StructuralIndex, type_index:
TypeIndex, learned_weights: LearnedScoring, cache: SearchCache,
exploration_rate: Float = 0.05, last_index_update: Time }

Artifacts: RelevanceCandidateSet = { query_id: Hash, candidates:
List[Candidate], scores: Map[CandidateID, Float], provenance: Provenance
}

ProblemSignature = { domain: Domain, vars: Set[Variable], units:
Set[Unit], causality: CausalityType, objective: Objective, constraints:
Set[Constraint] }

LearnedIndex = { embedding_model_version: SemVer, scoring_weights:
Map[Feature, Float], last_update: Time }

Functions: Index(artifact: Artifact) → Bool Precondition: Artifact must
be stored in Knowledge(10). Postcondition: Artifact added to all
indexes. Contract condition: Index updated on Knowledge write.

Search(query: Query) → RelevanceCandidateSet Precondition: Query must be
GSL-typed. Postcondition: Returns ranked candidates (L0/L1/L2).
Operational condition: SearchResult ≠ Evidence (K-INT-007).

Explore(context) → Set[Candidate] Precondition: context is non-critical or a quarantined research
branch with a declared budget. Postcondition: Returns low-rank candidates for novelty. Contract condition:
K0P, legal/medical/physical actuation and other critical deterministic paths use zero online stochastic
exploration; research exploration is bounded, seeded, non-authorizing and auditable.

Learn(feedback: Feedback) → Bool Precondition: Feedback must be
validated. Postcondition: Scoring weights updated (Evolution 33).
Operational condition: Ranking does not amplify epistemic status.

Update Protocol: Index: IDLE → BUILD → UPDATE → SYNC. Search: QUERY →
FILTER → RANK → RETURN.

Invariant references: SRC-001, K-INT-007, SRC-002, SRC-003, SRC-004.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Knowledge(10), Reasoning(50), Evolution(33),
Time(20), Economy(3), Language(6), Locale(40). Write: Events(45),
Knowledge(10). Math: M1 (Graph/Topology - Embedding spaces), M3
(Information Retrieval, TF-IDF), M3 (Novelty exploration). Proofs: P2.

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

FORMAL THREE-LEVEL RETRIEVAL/APPLICABILITY STACK
L0 — INDEX: broad lexical/semantic/type/provenance retrieval; maximizes recall and creates no authority.
L1 — STRUCTURAL RELEVANCE: match ProblemSignature, ontology, units, relations, dependencies, causality and context; rank structurally relevant candidates.
L2 — FORMAL APPLICABILITY: verify assumptions, dimensional consistency, validity scope, effects, authority, epistemic state, resource feasibility, proof/safety requirements. UNKNOWN applicability ≠ NOT_APPLICABLE.
Only L2-applicable candidates may enter an execution/decision path requiring formal applicability.

COMPONENT 32 — TRANSITION
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Meta Role: Legacy migration, schema evolution,
equivalence preservation.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type TransitionState = { active_migrations:
Map[MigrationID, MigrationPlan], equivalence_registry: Map[SchemaID,
EquivalenceClass], schema_adapters: Map[SchemaID, Adapter],
migration_history: List[MigrationRecord], residual_risk:
Map[MigrationID, Float] }

Artifacts: MigrationPlan = { plan_id: Hash, source: SchemaID, target:
SchemaID, preconditions: Set[Predicate], transform: TransformFunction,
equivalence_proof: ProofCertificate, rollback: RollbackPlan,
residual_risk: Float }

EquivalenceWitness = { proof_type: T0 | T1 | T2 | T3 | T4,
test_coverage: Float, audit_trail: AuditTrail }

Functions: Plan(source: SchemaID, target: SchemaID) → MigrationPlan
Precondition: Source and target must be compatible. Postcondition:
Returns migration plan. Contract condition: Migration atomic (two-phase commit).

Validate(plan: MigrationPlan) → Bool Precondition: Plan must exist.
Postcondition: Verifies equivalence proof (Verity 8). Contract condition:
Equivalence proof required for T0-T4.

Execute(plan: MigrationPlan) → Bool Precondition: Plan must be
validated. Postcondition: Migration executed; rollback available.
Operational condition: Residual risk quantified (≤10⁻⁶ per migration).

Rollback(plan: MigrationPlan) → Bool Precondition: Migration must have
failed. Postcondition: State reverted to pre-migration. Contract condition:
Rollback atomic.

Update Protocol: Migration: PLANNED → VALIDATED → EXECUTING → COMMITTED
| ROLLED_BACK. Schema: OLD → MIGRATING → NEW.

Invariant references: TRN-001, TRN-002, TRN-003.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Knowledge(10), Identity(1), Security(22),
Software(19), Compilation(42), Verity(8), Policy(48), Time(20). Write:
Events(45), Knowledge(10). Math: M1 (Equivalence Classes T0-T4), M0
(Schema algebra), M3 (Residual risk). Proofs: P1, P6.

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Garden Transition Fabric binding: Transition(32) is a primary coordinator for typed consequential transitions but GTF is cross-module, not a new component. Legacy T0–T4 migration is one GTF/UPA profile; policy, knowledge, software, model and evolutionary changes use the same high-assurance transition semantics with domain-specific obligations.

COMPONENT 33 — EVOLUTION
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Meta Role: Controlled self-improvement, patch evolution,
architectural reflection, meta-evolution.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type EvolutionState = { discovery_queue:
HeartbeatVectorClockedQueue[ConceptCandidate], // U01
proposal_admission_gate: TokenBucketRateLimiter, // U03
auth_attestation_required: Bool, // U16 rejection_registry:
ContextAwareFuzzyHashMap[Hash, RejectionRecord], // U02 / FSH-001
rejection_archiver: CryptographicallyIndexedBTrees, // U12
active_proposals: Map[ProposalID, EvolutionProposal], active_changesets:
Map[ChangeSetID, ChangeSet], impact_analyzer: FormalBlastRadiusBounder,
// U06 error_tracer: CryptographicRootCauseTracingTree, // U15
exclusion_guardrail: FormallyProvenExclusionInvariant, // U18
audit_queue: AgingBasedBackpressureQueue[AuditTask], // U08
coverage_feedback_gate: AdaptivePIDBackpressureController, // U13
proof_worksets: LeaseBasedDistributedLatchRegistry, // U05
evolution_worker_pool: ElasticLoadBalancedWorkerPool, // U09
validation_circuit_breaker: DeterministicPollingBarrier, // U14
trust_anchor_hash: NonVolatileRAMMirroredOTPHash, // U07
rollback_journal: AsynchronouslyPrunedJournal, // U11
global_commit_gate: AtomicTwoPhaseCommit, // U17 matrix_cycle_detector:
BoundedIncrementalSCCDetector, // U04; budgeted DAG/SCC updates, no O(N^3) safety-thread work
oscillation_halter: GracefulHardwareInterrupt,
// U19 emergency_proof_override_gate:
MultiSigEmergencyOverrideProtocol,// U20 budget_remaining: UInt, // P17
improvement_frontier: ImprovementFrontier, // Pareto memory
do_nothing_candidate: DoNothingCandidate, // mandatory baseline
comparator version: SemVer }

Artifacts: EvolutionProposal = { proposal_id: Hash, candidate:
ConceptCandidate, pbc_tuple: PBC_Tuple, triage_result: PASS | FAIL |
DEFER, routing_result: RoutingPath, proof_status: ProofStatus,
implementation_status: IMPLEMENTED | PENDING | FAILED,
validation_status: VALIDATED | PENDING | FAILED, status: CANDIDATE |
ACCEPTED | SPECIFIED | PROVEN | IMPLEMENTED | VALIDATED | ESTABLISHED |
REJECTED, timestamp: Time }

ChangeSet = { changeset_id: Hash, target_component: ComponentID,
semantic_delta: GSL_Delta, affected_contracts: Set[ContractID],
blast_radius: Set[ComponentID], rollback_state: Hash, proof_refs:
Set[ProofCertificate], audit_refs: Set[AuditEvidence] }

ImprovementCandidate = { candidate_id: Hash, baseline: BaselineRef,
candidate: ConceptCandidate | ChangeSet, objective_vector:
ObjectiveVector, hard_constraints: Set[ConstraintRef], evidence:
Set[EvidenceID | ProofCertificate | AuditEvidence], expected_delta:
Vector, blast_radius: Set[ComponentID], reversibility: Reversible |
PartiallyReversible | Irreversible, acceptance_policy: AcceptancePolicy,
status: CANDIDATE | ADMISSIBLE | REJECTED | DEFERRED | ACCEPTED }

ImprovementFrontier = { frontier_id: Hash, baseline: BaselineRef,
objectives: List[ObjectiveRef], known_candidates:
Set[ImprovementCandidateID], dominance_relations: Set[DOMINATES |
DOMINATED_BY | INCOMPARABLE], pareto_frontier:
Set[ImprovementCandidateID], theoretical_bounds: Map[ObjectiveRef,
Bound], empirical_bounds: Map[ObjectiveRef, Bound], historical_outcomes:
Map[ImprovementCandidateID, OutcomeRecord], last_updated: Time }

DoNothingCandidate = { candidate_id: Hash, baseline: BaselineRef,
objective_vector: ObjectiveVector, hard_constraints: Set[ConstraintRef],
expected_delta: ZeroVector, blast_radius: EmptySet, reversibility:
FullyReversible, acceptance_policy: DoNothingAcceptancePolicy,
rationale: Reason }

Improvement Comparison Contract: Every Evolution cycle MUST represent
the current baseline and DO_NOTHING as explicit candidates alongside
proposed changes. Candidate dominance is evaluated only after hard
constraints are satisfied. A candidate that violates a hard constraint
is infeasible regardless of objective gains. Among feasible candidates,
the ImprovementFrontier records whether each candidate dominates, is
dominated by, or is incomparable with prior candidates. Historical
frontier memory MUST be retained so Evolution can detect repeated
trade-offs and avoid treating previously rejected or dominated changes
as novel without new evidence.

DO_NOTHING is time- and environment-bound. Its comparison includes the expected cost/risk of inaction,
external drift and a mandatory next-review condition. Repeated DO_NOTHING wins cannot suppress bounded
exploration or make an expired baseline permanently authoritative.

Functions: [1] Discovery & Admission: ├── Sources: Research(49), Human
UI, RES-004/005/006 alerts. ├── Gate 1 (U16): Cryptographic authority
attestation required. ├── Gate 2 (U03): Token-bucket rate limit
(prevents flooding). ├── Gate 3 (U01): Vector-clock sequencing + atomic
locks. └── Output: ConceptCandidate.

[2] Anti-Nonsense Triage (P-107): ├── Step 2a: PBC Assessment (Problem,
Benefit, Cost/Risk). ├── Step 2b: 5 Rejection Tests (Problem,
Counterfactual, Capability-Delta, Complexity, Anti-Self-Amp). ├── Step
2c (U02): Fuzzy semantic hashing + AST normalization. └── Output:
TriageCandidate (PASS | FAIL | DEFER).

[3] Routing & Applicability (P-108): ├── Step 3a: Independent Module
Discovery (P-108A). ├── Step 3b: Concept Discovery. ├── Step 3c: Rule
Discovery. ├── Step 3d (U18): Conservative Guardrail Filters. ├── Step
3e (U15): Hierarchical error tracing. └── Output: RoutingPath +
GranularErrorCode.

[4] Improvement Frontier & Mandatory DO_NOTHING Comparison: ├──
Materialize each admissible proposal as an ImprovementCandidate. ├──
Include Current_System and DO_NOTHING as explicit comparison candidates.
├── Compare feasible candidates against the persistent
ImprovementFrontier. ├── Record DOMINATES / DOMINATED_BY / INCOMPARABLE
relations. ├── Preserve theoretical and empirical objective bounds and
historical outcomes. └── If no candidate dominates DO_NOTHING under the
acceptance policy, DO_NOTHING remains admissible.

[5] Static Impact & Blast Radius (U06): ├── Automated static analysis on
proposed ChangeSet. ├── Formally bound the declared blast_radius under
the analyzed dependency and execution model. └── If unproven → REJECT /
REVISE.

[6] Formal Verification (Verity 8): ├── Generate Proof Obligations. ├──
Discharge via dedicated Evolution Worker Pool (U09) → strict CPU quota.
├── If FAIL → Revision loop (back to [2]). └── Output: ProofCertificate.

[7] Implementation & Rollback (U11): ├── Generate ChangeSet. ├── U11:
Prepare atomic two-phase journaled snapshot for rollback. └── Output:
ChangeSet (Status: IMPLEMENTED).

[8] Validation (GSSCC): ├── Run independent validation. ├── U14: 100ms
hardware-enforced circuit breaker. If stalled → REJECT. └── If FAIL →
Rollback (U11 snapshot + U17 atomic commit rollback).

[9] Global State Commit (U17): ├── Enforce atomic two-phase commit
across all Fabric(18) read replicas. ├── Update semantic version
(historical baseline → prior baseline). ├── U07: Bind new version
baseline hash to hardware OTP root. └── Output: New Baseline.

[10] Recursive Coverage & Oscillation Checks: ├── RES-004/005/006
(Audit). U13: Exponential backoff on feedback loops. ├── U04:
Multi-dimensional matrix cycle detection. ├── U19: Non-maskable hardware
interrupt → Governance(30). └── Output: CoverageGapCandidate → feeds
back to Step [1].

Update Protocol: Proposal: CANDIDATE → ACCEPTED → SPECIFIED → PROVEN →
IMPLEMENTED → VALIDATED → ESTABLISHED | REJECTED. ChangeSet: PROPOSED →
VERIFIED → DEPLOYED → ROLLED_BACK.

Invariant references: EVO-001, EVO-002, EVO-003, EVO-004, EVO-005, EVO-006, REJ-001, EVO-007, RES-004, EVO-009, EVO-010, EVO-011, EVO-012, EVO-013.

Dependencies & Math: Read: All components (observes non-blocking),
Knowledge(10), Verity(8), Governance(30), Research(49), Reasoning(50),
Runtime(13), Security(22), Compliance(34). Write: Knowledge(10),
Events(45), Execution(14), Curation(21). Math: M0 (Termination - P5
rank), M3 (Epistemic Debt scoring), M1 (Fixed-point analysis), M4
(Complexity). Proofs: P5, P6, P8, P9, P15, P17, P18, P19, P20.
Additional contract checks: ImprovementFrontier dominance consistency;
DO_NOTHING comparison completeness.

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

33.0 — SELF-AUDIT & RECURSIVE PATCH MECHANISM

STATUS: NORMATIVE UPGRADE TO COMPONENT 33 (EVOLUTION)
ROLE: Full-design self-audit, bounded recursive patching and independent adversarial validation.

PRINCIPLE
  “No code may be deployed unless it has been checked against the entire technical design, patched
   recursively until compliance, and passed an adversarial review.”

“Entire technical design” means the candidate is routed against the complete version-bound canonical
registry: all 50 modules, current Core Objects/relations/types, effects, dependencies, invariants,
proof/evidence obligations, rights, K0P, authority, DesignEpoch/DesignBinding, schemas, interfaces,
profiles and applicable contextual rules. Each item receives APPLICABLE, CONDITIONALLY_APPLICABLE,
NOT_APPLICABLE or UNKNOWN with an explanation; it does not mean executing irrelevant tests blindly.

State extension
EvolutionState += {
  self_audit_queue: Queue<SelfAuditRequest>,
  active_audits: Map<SelfAuditID, FullDesignConformanceAudit>,
  patch_sessions: Map<PatchSessionID, RecursivePatchSession>,
  adversarial_reviews: Map<ReviewID, AdversarialReviewReport>,
  frozen_verifier_binding: FrozenVerifierBinding,
  canonical_design_registry_root: Hash,
  max_recursive_patch_depth: UInt = 3,
  self_audit_certificates: AppendOnlyList<SelfAuditCertificate>
}

SelfAuditRequest = {
  request_id,
  artifact_or_changeset_ref,
  target_environment,
  DesignEpoch,
  DesignBinding,
  KnowledgeSnapshot,
  canonical_registry_root,
  commit_class,
  risk_class,
  authority,
  resource_budget,
  provenance
}

FullDesignConformanceAudit = {
  audit_id,
  request_ref,
  artifact_hash,
  source_and_build_provenance,
  routing_audit_receipt,
  all_registry_items: Map<RegistryItemID, ApplicabilityResult>,
  affected_dependency_closure,
  type_effect_authority_results,
  invariant_results,
  proof_and_evidence_results,
  safety_security_privacy_rights_results,
  schema_interface_migration_results,
  test_and_adversarial_requirements,
  findings,
  unresolved_unknowns,
  completeness_boundary,
  status,
  provenance
}

ConformanceFinding = {
  finding_id,
  registry_item,
  severity: FATAL | CRITICAL | HIGH | MEDIUM | LOW | INFORMATIONAL,
  class: TYPE | EFFECT | DEPENDENCY | AUTHORITY | SAFETY | RIGHT | SECURITY | PRIVACY |
         EPISTEMIC | PROOF | TEST | SCHEMA | INTERFACE | CONFORMANCE | RESOURCE | OTHER,
  evidence,
  counterexample_or_trace,
  required_correction,
  owner,
  status: OPEN | PATCHED | VERIFIED_CLOSED | ACCEPTED_EXCEPTION | UNRESOLVED,
  provenance
}

RecursivePatchSession = {
  session_id,
  baseline_artifact,
  current_branch,
  depth: UInt,
  max_depth: UInt = 3,
  audit_history,
  generated_patch_refs,
  regression_and_new_finding_history,
  resource_consumption,
  termination_reason?,
  status,
  provenance
}

AdversarialReviewPlan = {
  plan_id,
  target_ref,
  declared_threat_model,
  attack_families,
  independent_reviewers,
  frozen_tests,
  generated_attacks,
  held_out_and_mutation_tests,
  common_mode_analysis,
  success_and_stop_conditions,
  resource_budget,
  provenance
}

AdversarialReviewReport = {
  review_id,
  plan_ref,
  attacks_executed,
  attacks_not_executed_and_reason,
  findings_and_counterexamples,
  exploitability_and_blast_radius,
  repairs?,
  residual_risk,
  dissent,
  status: SURVIVED_DECLARED_SUITE | FAILED | INCONCLUSIVE | RESOURCE_UNKNOWN,
  independent_signatures,
  provenance
}

SelfAuditCertificate = {
  certificate_id,
  artifact_and_build_hashes,
  DesignEpoch_and_DesignBinding,
  canonical_registry_root,
  final_full_audit_ref,
  recursive_patch_session_ref?,
  adversarial_review_ref,
  proof_conformance_test_refs,
  frozen_verifier_ref,
  unresolved_items,
  validity_scope_and_expiry,
  invalidation_triggers,
  status: CERTIFIED_FOR_DECLARED_SCOPE | REJECTED | INCONCLUSIVE,
  issuer_and_independent_verifiers,
  provenance,
  signatures
}

Full pipeline
  generated or modified artifact
    → immutable source/build receipt
    → isolated Evolution branch
    → bind DesignEpoch, DesignBinding, environment and complete canonical registry root
    → executable recursive routing/applicability audit across the full design
    → compiler/type/effect/dependency/authority and Structural Integrity checks
    → invariant/proof/evidence/safety/security/privacy/rights/conformance matrix
    → findings
    → if patchable and depth < 3: generate candidate patch on child branch
    → dependency/blast-radius analysis + regression suite
    → rerun the full audit against the patched artifact
    → repeat until compliant, blocked, or depth/budget termination
    → independent adversarial review under declared threat model
    → Verity(8), Compliance(34), frozen-verifier and authority gates
    → SelfAuditCertificate
    → GTF Prepare → Validate → Commit
    → deployment only within certificate scope
    → runtime monitoring, invalidation and recovery.

Recursive patch rule
  - Patches never overwrite the audited baseline; each is an immutable branch/commit.
  - Every patch recomputes affected dependency/blast-radius closure and reruns the complete registry
    applicability audit; a local fix can create a distant violation.
  - Patch generation and verification are causally/organizationally separated under CND-001…CND-009.
  - Maximum automatic recursive depth is 3. Depth exhaustion, oscillation, unresolved fatal/critical
    findings, invalid trust state or resource exhaustion automatically abandons/reverts the candidate
    branch to the last accepted state, preserves all evidence, releases the deployment pipeline and
    escalates for independent repair; it is never declared pass.
  - A patch may reduce scope or choose DO_NOTHING/REJECT when compliant repair is unavailable.

Adversarial review rule
  “Survives all attacks” means all applicable attacks in the declared, versioned threat model and
  AdversarialReviewPlan were executed within the stated coverage/resource boundary and left no
  unresolved fatal/critical finding. It does not mean survival against every logically possible or
  future unknown attack. INCONCLUSIVE/RESOURCE_UNKNOWN cannot become SURVIVED by wording or vote.

Status machine
  REQUESTED → BOUND → AUDITING → FINDINGS
    → PATCHING → REAUDIT ↺ (depth ≤ 3)
    → ADVERSARIAL_REVIEW → FORMAL_CONFORMANCE → CERTIFIED → PREPARED → COMMITTED → MONITORED.

Terminal/exception states
  REJECTED | HALTED | ESCALATED | INCONCLUSIVE | RESOURCE_UNKNOWN | QUARANTINED.
Accepted exceptions require explicit authority, bounded scope/expiry, do not cover hard
Constitution/K0P violations, and remain visible in the certificate.

Self-audit integrity
  The Self-Audit mechanism, registry resolver, patch controller and adversarial orchestrator cannot
  certify changes to themselves. Such changes require a frozen prior verifier, independent
  implementation, immutable test corpus, DesignEpoch transition and external certification authority.

Invariant references: SAP-001..010.

Integration
  Primary: Evolution(33), Verity(8), Compilation(42), Development(43), Compliance(34),
  Reasoning(50), Dependency(46), Security(22), Knowledge(10), Events(45), Transition(32), Runtime(13).
  Hard gates: Safety(9), Constitution(11), Privacy(2), Policy(48), Governance(30), K0P.
  Proof obligations: P5, P6, P7, P8, P9, P14, P15, P16, P17, P18, P19, P20, P21, P22, P23, P24.

LATE EVOLUTION BINDING
Branch/Merge Evolution, Historical Counterfactual Optimization, verification/resource efficiency,
multi-agent challenge/repair, and convergence experiments are defined once in Part IX. They specialize
this component rather than creating new components or independent lifecycle engines.

COMPONENT 34 — COMPLIANCE
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Meta Role: Certification, safety cases, regulatory
mapping, GSSCC ownership.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type ComplianceState = { safety_cases:
Map[CaseID, SafetyCase], compliance_maps: Map[StandardID,
ComplianceMapping], audit_evidence: List[AuditEvidence], gsscc_registry:
GSSCC_Registry, regulatory_gaps: List[CoverageGapCandidate], last_audit:
Time }

Artifacts: SafetyCase = { case_id: Hash, claim: Claim, evidence_refs:
Set[EvidenceID], argumentation_graph: Graph, status: DRAFT | SUBMITTED |
APPROVED | REJECTED }

ComplianceMapping = { mapping_id: Hash, external_standard: URI,
internal_rule: LawRuleRef, equivalence_proof: ProofCertificate, version:
SemVer }

AuditEvidence = { evidence_id: Hash, gsscc_id: GSSCC_ID, source: System
| Entity, test_or_audit: “Sweep” | “Provenance” | “Compliance” |
“Adversarial”, timestamp: Time, result: PASS | FAIL | INCONCLUSIVE,
artifact_refs: Set[ArtifactID], proof_refs: Set[ProofCertificate],
version: SemVer, signature: Signature }

Functions: Map(standard: URI) → ComplianceMapping Precondition: Standard
must be machine-readable. Postcondition: Returns mapping between
external standard and internal rule. Contract condition: Mapping must be
GSL-verified.

Verify(mapping: ComplianceMapping) → Bool Precondition: Mapping must
exist. Postcondition: Verifies compliance via Verity(8). Contract condition:
Audit evidence immutable; tamper-evident.

Certify(component: ComponentID) → Certificate Precondition: Component
must pass all mappings. Postcondition: Certificate issued. Contract condition:
Regulatory gap → escalate to Governance/Research.

Audit(component: ComponentID) → AuditEvidence Precondition: Component
must be active. Postcondition: Returns tamper-evident audit log.
Operational condition: Audit evidence immutable.

Update Protocol: Case: DRAFT → SUBMITTED → APPROVED | REJECTED. Mapping:
CREATED → VERIFIED → ACTIVE → SUPERSEDED.

Invariant references: CMP-001, CMP-002, CMP-003, CMP-004, CMP-005.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Verity(8), Policy(48), Safety(9), Law(12),
Governance(30), Knowledge(10), Time(20), Events(45). Write:
Knowledge(10), Events(45). Math: M0 (Regulatory automata), M1 (Mapping
lattices), M3 (Safety case argumentation). Proofs: P2, P6.

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

COMPONENT 35 — EXPLANATION
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

35.0 GSL-EXPLAIN — UNIVERSAL EXPLANATION ENGINE

STATUS: NORMATIVE COMPONENT UPGRADE
CLASSIFICATION: Meta / Epistemic / Human-Comprehensibility
ROLE: Produce faithful, audience-appropriate, challengeable explanations from Garden’s typed semantic,
evidential, causal, proof, decision, authority and transition graphs.

PRINCIPLE
  Explanation reveals the best available trace; it does not invent a missing trace, create evidence,
  raise epistemic status, authorize action, or certify the system that generated it.

Question taxonomy
  WHAT          What is the object/claim/decision/state?
  WHY           What reasons, evidence, rules or causes support it?
  HOW           What process, mechanism or sequence produced it?
  WHY_NOT       Why was an alternative rejected, blocked or not selected?
  WHAT_IF       What is predicted under an explicit intervention/change?
  WHEN          What time/order/validity/expiry applies?
  WHERE         What spatial/contextual scope applies?
  WHO           Which entities/agents/roles participated?
  AUTHORITY     Who authorized/delegated the consequential action, under what scope?
  EVIDENCE      What observations/evidence/proofs support or refute it?
  UNCERTAINTY   What is unknown, disputed, stale, unidentifiable or model-dependent?
  ALTERNATIVES  What feasible/Pareto/dissenting alternatives remain?
  COUNTERFACTUAL What would differ under a declared historical/causal intervention?
  CHANGE        What changed between versions/states, why, and with what effects?

Internal State (GSL typed)
type ExplainState = {
  explanation_cache: Map<ExplanationCacheKey, ExplanationBundle>,
  active_requests: Map<ExplanationRequestID, ExplanationRequest>,
  plans: Map<ExplanationPlanID, ExplanationPlan>,
  narrative_templates: Map<AudienceProfile, Template>,
  question_registry: Set<ExplanationQuestionType>,
  uncertainty_annotations: Map<NodeID, UncertaintyAnnotation>,
  compression_profiles: Map<CompressionProfileID, CompressionProfile>,
  audience_profiles: Map<AudienceProfileID, AudienceProfile>,
  receipts: AppendOnlyList<ExplanationReceipt>,
  last_generation: Time
}

ExplanationRequest = {
  request_id,
  requester,
  target_ref,
  question_text?,
  question_types: Set<ExplanationQuestionType>,
  audience,
  locale_and_language,
  accessibility_profile?,
  depth: MINIMUM | CONCISE | STANDARD | DEEP | FORMAL,
  time_context,
  scope,
  counterfactual_intervention?,
  requested_comparators,
  authority_context,
  privacy_and_redaction_context,
  freshness_requirement,
  provenance
}

ExplanationPlan = {
  plan_id,
  request_ref,
  target_resolution,
  required_graphs,
  query_sequence,
  causal_level_required?,
  proof_and_evidence_requirements,
  counterfactual_model_requirements?,
  audience_adaptation,
  privacy_redactions,
  resource_budget,
  termination_contract,
  provenance
}

ExplanationGraph = {
  graph_id,
  nodes: Set<ExplanationNode>,
  edges: Set<ExplanationEdge>,
  status_map: Map<NodeID, EpistemicStatus>,
  source_graph_refs,
  DesignEpoch,
  KnowledgeSnapshot,
  EnvironmentCommitment?,
  provenance
}

ExplanationNode.kind =
  CLAIM | EVIDENCE | OBSERVATION | ASSUMPTION | RULE | PROOF_STEP | CAUSE | EVENT | ACTION |
  DECISION | RECOMMENDATION | AUTHORIZATION | COMMITMENT | EXECUTION | OUTCOME | ALTERNATIVE |
  DISSENT | UNKNOWN | CONFLICT | RESOURCE_BOUND | REDACTION

ExplanationEdge.kind =
  SUPPORTS | REFUTES | DERIVED_FROM | ASSUMES | PROVES | CAUSES | PRECEDES | AUTHORIZES |
  DELEGATES | BLOCKS | SELECTS | REJECTS | SUPERSEDES | CONFLICTS_WITH | WOULD_CHANGE |
  DEPENDS_ON | AFFECTS | REDACTED_BY

CausalChain = {
  target_effect,
  causal_level: C0 | C1 | C2 | C3 | C4,
  structural_causal_model?,
  causes_and_mechanisms,
  confounders,
  interventions,
  counterfactual_worlds,
  identification_assumptions,
  evidence,
  uncertainty,
  provenance
}

DecisionChain = {
  problem,
  objectives_and_hard_constraints,
  considered_alternatives,
  predictions,
  evidence_and_proofs,
  authority,
  recommendation,
  authorization?,
  commitment?,
  execution?,
  outcome_receipts?,
  rejected_or_deferred_reasons,
  dissent,
  provenance
}

WhatIfScenario = {
  scenario_id,
  baseline,
  intervention,
  model_and_assumptions,
  fixed_and_changed_variables,
  predicted_trajectory,
  uncertainty,
  safety_and_rights_checks,
  comparison_to_DO_NOTHING,
  validity_scope,
  provenance
}

UncertaintyAnnotation = {
  node_id,
  status: UNKNOWN | STALE | CONFLICTED | UNDECIDABLE | MODEL_INVALID |
          SCOPE_UNDEFINED | RESOURCE_UNKNOWN | INFLUENCE_UNKNOWN,
  reason,
  human_explanation,
  information_needed,
  what_would_change_status,
  escalation_or_research_path,
  provenance
}

ExplanationBundle = {
  bundle_id,
  request_ref,
  direct_answer,
  minimum_summary,
  detailed_narrative,
  graph_ref,
  causal_chain?,
  decision_chain?,
  what_if_scenarios,
  evidence_and_proof_refs,
  assumptions,
  uncertainty_and_gaps,
  alternatives_and_dissent,
  authority_and_scope,
  what_would_change_conclusion,
  compression_trace,
  redaction_notice?,
  citations_and_provenance,
  version
}

ExplanationReceipt = {
  receipt_id,
  request_hash,
  target_hash,
  source_graph_roots,
  bundle_hash,
  renderer_and_version,
  audience_and_compression_profile,
  redaction_policy_ref,
  faithfulness_check,
  unsupported_statement_findings,
  delivered_at,
  provenance,
  signature}

Primary pipeline
  ExplanationRequest
    → authenticate/authorize and bind privacy/consent
    → normalize question and classify question types
    → resolve target, version, time, branch and scope
    → construct ExplanationPlan
    → retrieve semantic/evidence/proof/causal/decision/authority/dependency graphs
    → compute causal, historical and counterfactual chains where requested/applicable
    → preserve alternatives, dissent, uncertainty, gaps and redactions
    → answer the actual question first
    → adapt language/depth/locale/accessibility without semantic promotion
    → apply output-view redaction after generation as well as source-view redaction before generation
    → semantic/contextual privacy-leak, faithfulness, traceability and unsupported-statement checks
    → ExplanationBundle + immutable ExplanationReceipt
    → optional comprehension check / corrected explanation.

Functions
  Explain(request) → ExplanationBundle
  ExplainWhat(target, context) → ExplanationBundle
  ExplainWhy(target, context) → ExplanationBundle
  ExplainHow(target, context) → ExplanationBundle
  ExplainWhyNot(target, alternative, context) → ExplanationBundle
  ExplainWhatIf(target, intervention, context) → ExplanationBundle
  ExplainAuthority(action_or_decision) → ExplanationBundle
  ExplainEvidence(claim) → ExplanationBundle
  ExplainUncertainty(target) → ExplanationBundle
  CompareExplanations(targets_or_alternatives) → ExplanationBundle
  TraceToSource(statement) → ProvenanceTraversal
  Summarize(bundle, compression_profile) → ExplanationBundle
  Adapt(bundle, audience_locale_accessibility) → ExplanationBundle
  VerifyFaithfulness(bundle, source_graphs) → FaithfulnessReport
  RecordUnderstanding(bundle, subject_response?) → UnderstandingRecord

What-if / counterfactual discipline
  A WHAT_IF result is a PredictionArtifact unless independently observed after an authorized intervention.
  It declares the model, assumptions, fixed/changed variables, uncertainty, causal level, and comparator.
  Counterfactual explanation does not rewrite history and does not attribute present knowledge to a
  historical actor. Dangerous interventions remain simulation-only unless separately authorized.

Update protocol
  REQUESTED → AUTHORIZED → PLANNED → GRAPH_BOUND → GENERATED → FAITHFULNESS_CHECKED →
  ADAPTED → DELIVERED → CHALLENGED | UNDERSTOOD | SUPERSEDED.

  Failure/terminal states:
  REJECTED_UNAUTHORIZED | INSUFFICIENT_CONTEXT | UNKNOWN | INCONCLUSIVE | RESOURCE_UNKNOWN |
  PRIVACY_REDACTED | UNSUPPORTED_EXPLANATION_REJECTED.

Invariant references: EXP-001..016, GXE-001..012.

Integration
  Read: CLAIM Core Object, Knowledge(10), Verity(8), Reasoning(50), Language(6), Search(31),
        Events(45), Dependency(46), Time(20), Policy(48), Governance(30), Privacy(2), Access(36),
        Locale(40), Transition(32), Evolution(33).
  Write: Knowledge(10), Events(45), Curation(21), subject to retention/privacy policy.
  Math: graph theory, information theory, causal inference/SCMs, argumentation, logic, counterfactual
        semantics, compression/error bounds and human-comprehension evaluation.
  Proofs: P2, P3, P6, P12, P14, P21 and applicable privacy/authority obligations.

Explanation(35) remains the primary human-facing civilizational-knowledge distillation and
comprehensibility layer. CivilizationalKnowledgeDigest, CompressionTrace, PerspectiveSet,
DisagreementMap, HumanReadableRiskDisclosure, UnderstandingRecord, GardenChangeCard and
MinimumGardenView in Part X are specialized GSL-EXPLAIN artifacts/views.

Emissions data must be source-attested. Postcondition: Returns carbon

Postcondition: Carbon-aware scheduling uses only slack remaining after safety, rights, authority, hard
deadlines and continuity obligations. If a hard deadline conflicts with the lowest-carbon window, the
hard obligation wins and the carbon exception is receipted. Contract condition: Carbon caps never override K0P safety.

ALLOCATED → CONSUMED → RESET.

(sandboxed).

Profile(execution: Execution) → ProfileReport Precondition: Execution
must be active. Postcondition: Returns gas/time/memory profile.
Operational condition: Profiling does not alter execution.

Fuzz(target: ComponentID) → FuzzReport Precondition: Target must exist.
Postcondition: Returns fuzzing report. Contract condition: Fuzzer covers core
invariants.

Certify(contracts: Set[Contract]) → CertificationPackage Precondition:
Contracts must be GSL-verified. Postcondition: Returns certification
package. Contract condition: Certification tools produce machine-verifiable
mappings.

Update Protocol: Tool: INSTALLED → ACTIVE → UPDATED → DEPRECATED.
Certificate: GENERATED → VERIFIED → APPROVED.

Invariant references: DEV-001, DEV-002, DEV-003.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Compilation(42), Language(6), Verity(8),
Knowledge(10), Compliance(34), Runtime(13), Security(22), Time(20),
Events(45). Write: Events(45), Knowledge(10). Math: M4 (Fuzzing
algorithms, Complexity), M3 (Coverage metrics), M0 (Testing theory).
Proofs: P2, P6.

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

COMPONENT 44 — QUANTUM
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Meta Role: Backend-agnostic quantum computation
(Superconducting, TrappedIon, Photonic, SpinQubit).

Internal State (GSL Typed): type QuantumState = { active_backends:
Map[BackendID, Backend], calibration_reports: Map[BackendID,
CalibrationReport], noise_models: Map[BackendID, NoiseModel],
circuit_cache: Map[CircuitHash, QuantumCircuit], quarantine_list:
Set[BackendID] }

Artifacts: QuantumCircuit = { qubits: UInt, gates: List[Gate],
measurements: List[Measurement], classical_registers:
List[ClassicalRegister] }

CalibrationReport = { backend_id: BackendID, T1: Float, T2: Float,
gate_fidelities: Map[Gate, Float], readout_error: Float, timestamp: Time
}

NoiseModel = { depolarizing: Float, amplitude_damping: Float,
phase_damping: Float, readout_error: Float, thermal: Float }

Functions: Compile(circuit: QuantumCircuit, backend: BackendID) → Pulses
Precondition: Circuit must exist. Postcondition: Returns
hardware-specific pulses. Contract condition: Calibration continuous.

Execute(circuit: QuantumCircuit) → Result Precondition: Circuit must
exist. Postcondition: Returns measurement outcomes. Contract condition: Drifted
backends auto-quarantined.

Calibrate(backend: BackendID) → CalibrationReport Precondition: Backend
must be active. Postcondition: Returns calibration report. Contract condition:
Drift > threshold → quarantine.

Watchdog(backend: BackendID) → HealthStatus Precondition: Backend must
be active. Postcondition: Returns drift status using adaptive sampling driven by temperature derivative,
recent T1/T2 volatility, pulse load and anomaly signals. High-risk sequences require a fresh pre-pulse
calibration/interlock; stale calibration or an intervening thermal spike quarantines before execution.
Operational condition: Hardware isolation (DR-QEI).

Update Protocol: Circuit: COMPILED → SCHEDULED → EXECUTED → MEASURED.
Backend: ONLINE → CALIBRATING → ACTIVE → QUARANTINED.

Invariant references: QNT-001, QNT-002, QNT-003, QNT-004.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Hardware(16), Knowledge(10), Mathematics(7)
[M4.Q], Security(22), Time(20), Runtime(13), Safety(9). Write:
Events(45), Knowledge(10). Math: M1 (Hilbert Space - Tensor products),
M3 (Noise models - density matrices), M4 (Calibration drift detection).
Proofs: P2, P4.

COMPONENT 45 — EVENTS
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Operational Role: Universal event model. Captures,
routes, and stores all events from all components.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type EventsState = { event_log: List[Event],
subscriptions: Map[EventCategory, Set[Subscription]], priority_lanes:
Map[EventPriority, Queue[Event]], last_processed: Map[EventPriority,
Time] }

Artifacts: Event = { id: Hash, category: INFO | WARNING | FAILURE |
RECOVERY | AUDIT | SECURITY | SAFETY | GOVERNANCE, source: ComponentRef,
timestamp: Time, payload: Bytes, severity: LOW | MEDIUM | HIGH |
CRITICAL, provenance: Provenance }

EventSubscription = { subscriber: ComponentRef, filter: Predicate,
priority: EventPriority, callback: Event → Action }

Functions: Emit(event: Event) → EventID Precondition: Event must be
valid. Postcondition: Event appended to immutable log. Contract condition: Events
immutable. Event occurrence ≠ payload truth.

Route(event: Event) → Bool Precondition: Event must exist.
Postcondition: Delivered to subscribers based on priority/filter.
Operational condition: Safety/Security priority lanes preempt other events.

Store(event: Event) → Bool Precondition: Event must exist.
Postcondition: Event stored in Knowledge(10) via Curation(21).
Operational condition: Store immutable.

Query(filter: Predicate) → List[Event] Precondition: Filter must be
valid. Postcondition: Returns matching events. Contract condition: Event
occurrence ≠ event payload truth (K-INT-001).

Update Protocol: Event: EMITTED → ROUTED → STORED → DELIVERED.

Invariant references: EVT-001, EVT-002, K-INT-001, EVT-003, EVT-004.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Knowledge(10), Time(20), Runtime(13),
Fabric(18), Security(22), Safety(9), Governance(30). Write: Events(45),
Knowledge(10). Math: M0 (Immutable append-only log), M1 (DAG -
causality), M3 (Event priority lanes). Proofs: P1, P6.

COMPONENT 46 — DEPENDENCY
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Semantic Role: Version constraints, dependency graphs,

Internal State (GSL Typed): type DependencyState = { dependency_graph:
Graph<ComponentID, DependencyEdge>, resolved_versions: Map[ComponentID,

}

Artifacts: DependencyGraph = { nodes: Set[ComponentID], edges:
Set[DependencyEdge], version_constraints: Map[ComponentID,
SemVerConstraint] }

ResolvedVersionSet = { component: ComponentID, selected_version: SemVer,
dependencies: Set[DependencyEdge] }

ConflictReport = { conflict_type: VERSION_MISMATCH | CYCLE |
MISSING_PROVIDER, conflicting_edges: Set[DependencyEdge],
resolution_suggestions: Set[Resolution] }

Functions: Declare(component: ComponentID, deps: Set[Dependency]) → Bool
Precondition: Component must exist. Postcondition: Dependencies added to
global graph. Contract condition: P7: Undeclared refs ⇒ INFEASIBLE.

Resolve(component: ComponentID) → ResolvedVersionSet Precondition:
Component must exist. Postcondition: Returns resolved version set.
Operational condition: Build DAG acyclic.

Verify(component: ComponentID) → Bool Precondition: Component must
exist. Postcondition: Verifies static closure (P7a). Contract condition: Static
references declared.

Gate(reference: Reference) → Bool Precondition: Reference must exist.
Postcondition: Dynamic runtime gate (P7b). Contract condition: Runtime reference
must pass AuthZ + Resolution.

Update Protocol: Graph: DECLARE → RESOLVE → VERIFY → COMMIT.

Invariant references: DEP-001, DEP-002, DEP-003, DEP-004, DEP-005, DEP-006.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Knowledge(10), Verity(8), Compilation(42),
Runtime(13), Execution(14), Evolution(33), Time(20), Security(22).
Write: Events(45), Knowledge(10). Math: M1.GR (Graph Theory), M0 (SemVer
algebra), M1 (Category Theory). Proofs: P7, P6.

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Dependency(46) additionally stores/serves the TheoryDependencyGraph and transition ObligationGraph edges. Upstream theory, invariant, schema, evidence or environment changes propagate REVALIDATION_REQUIRED to affected dependents; this does not automatically prove those dependents false.

COMPONENT 47 — OFFLINE
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Meta Role: Defined operating state
(Green/Yellow/Orange/Red), disconnected operations.

Internal State (GSL Typed): type OfflineState = { mode: GREEN | YELLOW |
ORANGE | RED, cached_policies: Map[PolicyID, Policy], cached_envelopes:
Map[EnvelopeID, SafetyEnvelope], authority_leases: Map[Entity,
AuthorityLease], offline_audit_escrow: List[OfflineAuditEscrow],
pending_reconciliation: Queue[ReconciliationTask] }

Artifacts: OfflineState = { mode: GREEN | YELLOW | ORANGE | RED,
cached_policies: Map[PolicyID, Policy], safety_envelope: SafetyEnvelope,
authority_lease: AuthorityLease, pending_actions: Queue[Action] }

AuthorityLease = { lease_id: Hash, scope: Scope, expiry: Time, max_risk:
Float, offline_capability: Capability }

CachedPolicyBinding = { policy_ref, content_hash, DesignEpoch, valid_from, expires_at,
offline_risk_scope, revalidation_trigger, conservative_fallback }

OfflineAuditEscrow = { local_log: Event[], hw_signature: Signature,
reconnection_proof: Proof }

Functions: Detect(connectivity: Bool) → OfflineMode Precondition:
Connectivity must be known. Postcondition: Returns current mode.
Operational condition: Red mode = beacon-only SOS (no execution).

Cache(policy: Policy, envelope: SafetyEnvelope) → Bool Precondition:
Mode must be GREEN/YELLOW. Postcondition: Policies/envelopes cached
locally. Contract condition: AuthorityLease expires.

Execute(action: Action) → Outcome Precondition: Must have
AuthorityLease. Postcondition: Action executed with local K0P + cached
policies. Contract condition: Local K0P + bounded envelope enforced.

Reconcile(escrow: OfflineAuditEscrow) → Bool Precondition: Reconnection
occurred. Postcondition: 1. escrow.local_log is verified against
escrow.hw_signature. 2. The escrow is converted to a full-quorum
multi-signature seal by the current membership-epoch quorum. An exact partition cannot finalize
conflicting high-impact histories; after connectivity returns, a joint reconciliation/recovery ceremony
establishes a new epoch without pretending either side had quorum. 3. The seal
is appended to Knowledge(10) via Events(45), satisfying the
Emergency(38) audit mandate. 4. Conflicts are resolved deterministically
using Authority precedence + CRDT. Contract condition: OfflineAuditEscrow is
immutable and cannot bypass audit/proof gates.

Update Protocol: Mode: GREEN → YELLOW → ORANGE → RED. Lease: REQUESTED →
GRANTED → EXPIRED | REVOKED.

Invariant references: OFF-001, OFF-002, OFF-003, OFF-004, OFF-005, OFF-006, OFF-007.

Dependencies & Math: Read: Safety(9), Verity(8), Knowledge(10),
Time(20), Policy(48), Security(22), Emergency(38), Network(17). Write:
Events(45), Knowledge(10). Math: M3 (Risk assessment - AuthorityLease
max_risk), M4 (Cached interpolation), M1 (Graph - Reconciliation).
Proofs: P1, P4.

COMPONENT 49 — RESEARCH
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Epistemic Role: Scientific discovery, theory generation,

EVALUATED | REJECTED | ADOPTED }

Functions: Observe(anomaly: Anomaly) → Hypothesis Precondition: Anomaly

Experiment(plan: ExperimentPlan) → Result Precondition: Plan must be

Operational condition: Poison-resistant validation (Bayesian divergence).

(Poison-resistant validation). Proofs: P2, P5.

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Late Research profiles include: independent recursive convergence experiments; Minimum-Common-Core→Growing-Core domain synthesis; Minimum-Common-Law synthesis; GSL-GENERATIVE multilevel generative-organization research; and distributed in-body micro/nanorobotics. These are research profiles, not new components and not automatically established facts.

COMPONENT 50 — REASONING
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classification: Epistemic / Cognitive / Theory-Selection / Meta-Reasoning.
Status: NORMATIVE — UNIFIED REASONING, THEORY & FORMAL-PROOF SYSTEM.

PRINCIPLE
Reasoning(50) chooses, composes and controls reasoning strategies.
Mathematics(7) supplies mathematical structures and formal mathematical objects.
Verity(8) checks formal proofs and discharges proof obligations.
Knowledge(10) supplies epistemic state, claims and evidence.
Research(49) acquires new empirical evidence and may generate candidate theories.
Search(31) retrieves candidate theories, patterns, evidence and analogies.
Explanation(35) renders reasoning structures for humans.
Policy(48), Constitution(11) and Safety(9) constrain consequential recommendations/actions.

Reasoning ≠ Proof ≠ Evidence ≠ Authorization.
Recommendation ≠ Authorization.
Formal proof ≠ empirical validation.

50.1 UNIFIED REASONING SYSTEM

REASONING (50)
│
├── Problem Formalization
│     └── ProblemSignature
│
├── Reasoning Mode Registry
│     ├── Four Foundational Inference Families
│     └── Specialized / Composed Modes
│
├── Theory Registry
│     └── TheorySignature + TheoryDependencyGraph
│
├── Assumption Registry
├── Evidence Graph
├── Inference / Proof Graph
├── Mode Selection
├── Mode Composition
├── Falsification / Challenge
├── Uncertainty Propagation
├── Resource / Termination Control
├── Verity Handoff
├── Explanation Handoff
└── Meta-Reasoning

The registry is extensible inside Reasoning(50). New reasoning strategies do not create new Garden components.

50.2 FOUR FOUNDATIONAL INFERENCE FAMILIES

1. ABDUCTION
   Inference to a candidate/best explanation.

2. ARGUMENTATION
   Dialectical reasoning using attack/defense structures, including Dung
   Grounded / Preferred / Stable semantics.

3. DEDUCTION
   Necessary consequence under declared premises, axioms and formal calculus.

4. EMPIRICAL INDUCTION
   Generalization from observations/evidence with quantified uncertainty.

Critical distinction:
    EMPIRICAL INDUCTION ≠ MATHEMATICAL INDUCTION.
Mathematical induction is a deductive FORMAL_PROOF strategy, not empirical generalization.

50.3 FIRST-CLASS REASONING MODE REGISTRY

Top-level ReasoningMode values:
  DEDUCTIVE
  INDUCTIVE_EMPIRICAL
  ABDUCTIVE
  ARGUMENTATIVE
  ANALOGICAL
  DEFAULT_NONMONOTONIC
  PROBABILISTIC
  CAUSAL
  COUNTERFACTUAL
  TEMPORAL
  SPATIAL
  CONSTRAINT
  DECISION
  ETHICAL_NORMATIVE
  GAME_THEORETIC
  OPTIMIZATION
  PLANNING
  EXPLANATORY
  ADVERSARIAL
  THEORY_REASONING
  FORMAL_PROOF
  MODELING
  PREDICTIVE
  META

PROBABILISTIC subprofiles include:
  BAYESIAN | LIKELIHOOD | GRAPHICAL_MODEL | STATISTICAL

The current registry therefore contains 24 top-level modes. Counts are descriptive rather than
constitutional: a future mode refinement does not change the frozen 50-module topology.

50.4 CORE STATE

type ReasonState = {
    active_problems: Map<ProblemID, ProblemSignature>,
    theory_registry: Map<TheoryID, TheorySignature>,
    theory_statuses: Map<TheoryID, TheoryStatus>,
    theory_dependency_graph: TheoryDependencyGraph,
    assumption_registry: Map<AssumptionID, Assumption>,
    evidence_sets: Map<EvidenceSetID, EvidenceSet>,
    reasoning_traces: List<ReasoningTrace>,
    inference_traces: List<InferenceTrace>,
    proof_strategy_registry: ProofStrategyRegistry,
    reasoning_mode_registry: ReasoningModeRegistry,
    inference_cache: Map<InferenceCacheKey, InferenceResult>,
    meta_reasoning_trace: List<MetaReasoningStep>,
    unknown_registry: Map<UnknownID, UnknownSubtype>,
    resource_budget: ReasoningBudget,
    version: SemVer
}

Cache validity MUST bind at least:
  DesignEpoch
  KnowledgeSnapshot
  EnvironmentCommitment
  DependencyClosure
  AssumptionSet
  FormalSystemVersion
A cache hit whose binding is stale is invalid.

50.5 CORE ARTIFACTS

ProblemSignature = {
    domain: Domain,
    vars: Set<Variable>,
    units: Set<Unit>,
    causality_type: CausalityType,
    objective: Objective,
    constraints: Set<Constraint>,
    context_hash: Hash
}

TheoryStatus = {
    formal_status: PROVEN | CONDITIONALLY_PROVEN | UNPROVEN | REFUTED,
    empirical_status: VALIDATED | PARTIALLY_VALIDATED | UNVALIDATED | REFUTED,
    application_status: APPLICABLE | CONDITIONALLY_APPLICABLE |
                        EMPIRICALLY_UNVALIDATED | INSUFFICIENT |
                        NOT_APPLICABLE | FALSIFIED | UNKNOWN,
    scope: ScopeRef,
    freshness: FreshnessState
}

ReasoningTrace = {
    trace_id: Hash,
    problem_signature: ProblemSignature,
    timestamp: Time,
    candidates_considered: Set<Candidate>,
    candidates_rejected: Map<Candidate, RejectionReason>,
    selected_theory: Option<TheoryRef>,
    selected_assumptions: Set<Assumption>,
    selected_modes: List<ReasoningMode>,
    falsification_attempts: List<Falsification>,
    composition_chain: List<TheoryRef>,
    verity_status: ProofStatus,
    execution_outcome: Outcome,
    escalation_ref: Option<EscalationRef>,
    confidence_or_uncertainty: Option<Represented<Uncertainty>>,
    provenance: Provenance
}

EvidenceSet = {
    evidence_refs: Set<EvidenceRef>,
    independence_graph: Graph<EvidenceRef, IndepStatus>
}

UnknownSubtype = {
    id: Hash,
    type: CONFLICT_UNKNOWN | EVIDENCE_UNKNOWN | UNDECIDABLE |
          RESOURCE_UNKNOWN | STALE_UNKNOWN | MODEL_INVALID_UNKNOWN |
          SCOPE_UNDEFINED_UNKNOWN
}

50.6 EXISTING THEORY-ORCHESTRATION FUNCTIONS — RETAINED

Formalize(query: Query) → ProblemSignature
  Precondition: query GSL-typed.
  Postcondition: well-formed ProblemSignature.

Retrieve(signature: ProblemSignature) → Set<Theory>
  Uses Search(31) L0/L1/L2 retrieval/applicability path.
  Structural match is primary; keyword similarity alone is insufficient.

Falsify(theory: Theory) → CounterExample | None
  Searches for counterexamples/assumption failures.
  Survival ≠ proof.

Compose(theories: Set<Theory>) → Theory
  Requires compatibility of variables, assumptions, units, scope, uncertainty,
  boundary conditions and validity domains.

ManageEvidence(evidence: Evidence) → EvidenceSet
  Evidence remains provenance-bound; LLMs cannot manufacture Evidence.

50.7 DEDUCTION ENGINE

Deduce(
    premises: Set<GSL_Expr>,
    query: GSL_Expr,
    calculus: FormalSystem,
    budget: InferenceBudget
) → DeductionResult

Preconditions:  - premises and query are well-formed GSL expressions;
  - the formal system/calculus is declared;
  - assumptions and applicability scope are explicit.

Supported deductive strategies include:
  propositional reasoning; first-order reasoning; predicate reasoning;
  syllogistic reasoning; forward chaining; backward chaining;
  modus ponens; modus tollens; contrapositive; contradiction;
  proof by cases; equivalence; constraint solving; SAT; SMT;
  theorem-prover handoff where appropriate.

Pipeline:
  Premises + Query
      → Logical Formalization
      → Consistency / well-formedness check
      → Select calculus / solver
      → Forward/backward reasoning and/or proof search
      → Proof / refutation / terminal status

DeductionResult = {
    status: DEDUCED | REFUTED | NOT_DEDUCED | INVALID | UNKNOWN,
    conclusion: GSL_Expr,
    formal_system: FormalSystem,
    proof_trace: List<ProofStep>,
    assumptions_used: List<AssumptionRef>,
    dependencies: List<DependencyRef>,
    scope: ScopeRef,
    resource_usage: ResourceUsage,
    provenance: Provenance
}

Semantics:
  DEDUCED     = query follows in the declared formal system.
  REFUTED     = negation of query follows in the declared formal system.
  NOT_DEDUCED = permitted only when the declared fragment/procedure is complete
                for the question and establishes non-entailment.
  INVALID     = malformed/inconsistent formalization under the declared rules.
  UNKNOWN     = timeout, undecidable case, incomplete calculus, missing assumptions,
                resource exhaustion or unresolved formalization.

No proof found ≠ NOT_DEDUCED in a generic bounded/incomplete reasoning system.
DEDUCED is logical consequence conditional on premises; it is not empirical truth.
Deduction cannot amplify the epistemic status of uncertain premises merely by being valid.

50.8 EMPIRICAL INDUCTION ENGINE

Induce(
    observations: Set<Observation>,
    hypothesis_space: HypothesisSpace,
    induction_profile: InductionProfile
) → InductionResult

Supported profiles:
  enumerative induction; statistical generalization; Bayesian induction;
  pattern induction; trend inference; population inference;
  causal induction; hypothesis generalization.

Pipeline:
  Observations
    → quality/provenance/independence checks
    → candidate pattern generation
    → support + contradiction analysis
    → sampling/selection-bias analysis
    → uncertainty estimation
    → out-of-distribution analysis
    → scope determination
    → InductionResult

InductionProfile = {
    representation: RepresentationType,
    estimator: EstimatorRef,
    prior_if_applicable: Option<PriorRef>,
    dependence_model: DependenceModel,
    noise_model: NoiseModel,
    sampling_model: SamplingModel,
    exception_model: ExceptionModel,
    confidence_method: ConfidenceMethod,
    scope_rules: List<ScopeRule>
}

No universal confidence formula is mandated.
The historical simple profile support/(support+contradiction) is only a suitable
special case for narrowly defined binary settings. General induction is governed by
the Mathematical Representation Policy and domain-appropriate estimators.

A counterexample is not automatic rejection unless the hypothesis claims universal,
exceptionless behavior. Statistical laws may legitimately contain expected exceptions.

InductionResult = {
    candidate_patterns: List<Pattern>,
    support_count: UInt,
    counterexamples: List<Observation>,
    dependence_structure: Graph,
    uncertainty: UncertaintyRef,
    inference_measure: Represented<InferenceMeasure>,
    validity_scope: ScopeRef,
    generalization_status: SUPPORTED | WEAK | CONTRADICTED | INSUFFICIENT | UNKNOWN,
    retraction_conditions: List<Condition>,
    provenance: Provenance
}

InferenceMeasure may represent Probability, Distribution, Interval, LogOdds,
LogEvidence, Confidence, SupportMeasure or another semantically declared measure.
Representation ≠ semantic meaning.
Empirical induction produces support/uncertainty, never formal necessity.

50.9 MATHEMATICAL INDUCTION & FORMAL PROOF STRATEGY REGISTRY

Mathematical induction is deductive formal proof, not empirical induction.

Flow:
  Reasoning(50) selects ProofStrategy
      → Mathematics(7) supplies mathematical structures/induction principles
      → Verity(8) machine-checks proof obligations
      → ProofCertificate

ProofStrategy leaf strategies currently include:

Classical logical:
  DIRECT
  CONTRAPOSITIVE
  CONTRADICTION
  CASE_ANALYSIS
  EXISTENCE
  UNIQUENESS

Computational / algebraic:
  EQUATIONAL
  ALGEBRAIC
  RECURSIVE
  FIXED_POINT

Mathematical induction:
  INDUCTION_WEAK / ORDINARY
  INDUCTION_STRONG / COMPLETE
  INDUCTION_STRUCTURAL
  INDUCTION_WELL_FOUNDED
  INDUCTION_COURSE_OF_VALUES
  INDUCTION_MUTUAL
  INDUCTION_NESTED
  INDUCTION_LEXICOGRAPHIC
  INDUCTION_TRANSFINITE

Coinduction:
  COINDUCTION_BISIMULATION
  COINDUCTION_GREATEST_FIXED_POINT

Automated:
  AUTOMATED_SAT
  AUTOMATED_SMT
  THEOREM_PROVER

Ordinary induction:
  P(0) ∧ (P(n) → P(n+1)) ⇒ ∀n∈ℕ, P(n)

Strong induction:
  (∀k≤n, P(k)) → P(n+1), together with the required base condition(s).

Structural induction applies to recursively generated structures including:
  GSL ASTs; proof terms; expression trees; recursive schemas;
  dependency structures; process structures; branch histories.

Well-founded induction generalizes to a declared relation with no infinite descending chain.
Transfinite induction applies over a declared well-order.
Coinduction establishes properties of potentially infinite/co-recursive structures through
bisimulation/greatest-fixed-point reasoning.

A successful mathematical induction proof yields FORMAL PROVEN status relative to the
declared axioms, definitions, induction principle and formal system after Verity checks.
It does not by itself establish that empirical premises/models about the physical world are true.

50.10 ABDUCTION

Observation
  → candidate explanatory hypotheses
  → assumption + scope analysis
  → explanatory comparison
  → falsification attempts
  → best current candidate(s)

ABDUCTED = best available explanatory candidate under the declared comparison.
It never means TRUE, PROVEN or VERIFIED merely because it explains the observation.

50.11 ARGUMENTATION

Claim
├── supports
├── attacks
├── rebuttals
├── defeaters
└── defenses

Supported semantics include Dung Grounded, Preferred and Stable semantics.
ARGUED = accepted/surviving under the declared argumentation framework.
ARGUED ≠ FACT.

50.12 ANALOGICAL REASONING

Source system
  → structural mapping
  → candidate correspondence
  → difference analysis
  → applicability check
  → transferred hypothesis

Structural similarity ≠ proof.
Analogical transfer creates a candidate relation requiring independent validation.

50.13 DEFAULT / NON-MONOTONIC REASONING

Supports defeasible rules and explicit exceptions, e.g.:
  Bird(x) normally → Flies(x)
  Penguin(x) → exception → ¬Flies(x)

Conclusions carry supporting defaults, checked exceptions, defeaters,
retraction conditions and validity scope. New evidence may legitimately retract
a previous conclusion; this is not an error in non-monotonic reasoning.

50.14 PROBABILISTIC / BAYESIAN REASONING

General form:
  prior state + evidence + dependency structure → posterior state

For Bayesian probability semantics:
  P(H|E) = P(E|H)·P(H)/P(E)

Constraints:
  - probability semantics must actually apply;
  - Mathematical Representation Policy controls representation;
  - Probability, LogOdds, LogEvidence, Interval, Distribution, Tensor, etc.
    are representations/types with declared semantics, not interchangeable universal scores;
  - correlated evidence cannot be treated as independent merely because it came from
    multiple records/models/sources.

50.15 CAUSAL REASONING

Association
  → causal hypothesis
  → SCM / causal graph
  → assumption validation
  → intervention reasoning
  → causal estimate

Supports intervention semantics such as P(Y | do(X=x)).
Causal claims MUST identify causal structure, confounders, assumptions,
identification method, intervention semantics and scope appropriate to the claim.
Correlation alone cannot create causal status.

50.16 COUNTERFACTUAL REASONING

Observed world + causal/model state + alternative intervention
  → counterfactual trajectory.

Directly supports Historical Counterfactual Optimization, Prediction, Evolution,
Decision analysis and Incident reconstruction.
Counterfactual conclusions inherit the uncertainty/limitations of the model from which
they are generated.

50.17 TEMPORAL & SPATIAL REASONING

Temporal:
  before/after; during; since; until; always; eventually; valid-at; expired-at;
  superseded-at; causal ordering; temporal obligations.

Spatial:
  inside/outside; containment; adjacency; intersection; distance; direction;
  route; reachability; coordinate transformation; topological relation.

These consume existing TIME and SPACE semantics and do not create new ontology.

50.18 ADVERSARIAL / FALSIFICATION REASONING

Candidate
  → assume candidate may be wrong
  → attack assumptions/boundary conditions/evidence/composition
  → search counterexamples
  → test adversarial variants.

Survival means only NO COUNTEREXAMPLE FOUND WITHIN DECLARED SEARCH, never proof.

50.19 DECISION REASONING

Beliefs + candidate actions + predicted consequences + values + risks + constraints + resources
  → Recommendation.

Possible form:
  a* = argmax_a EU(a)
subject to Constitution, Rights, Safety/K0P, Authority, Law, Policy, Resources and
explicit epistemic uncertainty.

Recommendation ≠ Authorization.

50.20 ETHICAL / NORMATIVE REASONING

FACT      = what evidence indicates is.
VALUE     = what is valued/preferred.
RIGHT     = protected constitutional constraint.
LAW       = governed legal requirement.
POLICY    = operational rule.
DECISION  = recommended action.

IS ⇏ OUGHT without an explicit normative premise.
Reasoning may relate these categories but may not silently convert one into another.

50.21 GAME-THEORETIC / MULTI-AGENT REASONING

Models agents, strategies, information, beliefs, payoffs, incentives, coalitions,
adversaries, equilibria, coordination and mechanism design.
Relevant to Economy, Governance, Security, Agent, Evolution and Earth-branch coordination.
An equilibrium is strategic stability under assumptions; it does not establish ethical legitimacy.

50.22 OPTIMIZATION REASONING

Objective + decision variables + feasible set + constraints + uncertainty + baseline
  → candidate optimum / Pareto set.

Where applicable, mandatory comparison includes Current_System, DO_NOTHING and candidate alternatives.
Optimal ≠ Safe. Optimal ≠ True. Optimal ≠ Authorized.

50.23 PLANNING REASONING

Current State → Goal → candidate actions → preconditions/effects
  → temporal/resource constraints → prediction → contingencies → Plan.

Supports classical, hierarchical, temporal, contingent, resource-constrained and
multi-agent planning. Execution remains Execution(14), subject to authority/safety.

50.24 EXPLANATORY REASONING

Prediction asks: What happens?
Explanation asks: Why did it happen?

Observed outcome
  → causal/procedural lineage
  → relevant mechanism
  → assumptions + alternatives
  → ExplanationArtifact.

Reasoning(50) determines explanatory structure; Explanation(35) renders it for humans.

50.25 THEORY REASONING — GENERAL THEORY ENGINE

Reasoning(50) supports first-class:
  theory discovery; theory invention; retrieval; formalization; applicability;
  comparison; falsification; composition; specialization; generalization;
  contradiction; equivalence; approximation; supersession; dependency;
  revalidation; selection; retirement.

TheorySignature = {
    theory_id: TheoryID,
    domain: Domain,
    entities: List<EntityRef>,
    variables: List<VariableDef>,
    dimensions: List<Dimension>,
    units: List<Unit>,
    assumptions: List<AssumptionRef>,
    axioms_or_rules: List<GSL_Expr | RuleRef>,
    equations: List<GSL_Expr>,
    structural_signature: StructuralSignature,
    causal_structure: Option<CausalGraph>,
    temporal_structure: Option<TemporalStructure>,
    spatial_structure: Option<SpatialStructure>,
    validity_scope: ScopeRef,
    boundary_conditions: List<ConditionRef>,
    epistemic_basis: EpistemicBasis,
    evidence_requirements: List<EvidenceRequirement>,
    falsification_conditions: List<Predicate>,
    prediction_signature: Option<PredictionSignature>,
    uncertainty_profile: UncertaintyPropagationProfile,
    computational_requirements: List<ResourceBound>,
    dependency_refs: List<DependencyRef>,
    theory_status: TheoryStatus,
    provenance: Provenance
}

Theory operation ownership:
  Discovery/Invention      → Research(49) + Reasoning(50), producing candidate theories.
  Retrieval                → Search(31).
  Applicability/Comparison → Reasoning(50).
  Falsification            → Reasoning(50) + Research(49) where experiments are needed.
  Composition              → Reasoning(50), compatibility required.
  Contradiction/Equivalence→ Reasoning(50) + Verity(8) where formalizable.
  Dependency               → Dependency(46).
  Revalidation             → Reasoning(50) + Knowledge(10) + Continuous Epistemic Revalidation.
  Supersession             → Reasoning(50) proposal + Knowledge(10) lineage + normal GTF governance.

Theory invention creates a CANDIDATE theory, never established truth.

50.26 THEORY STATUS SYSTEM

Theory status is separate from inference status.
High-level lifecycle/status vocabulary:
  ESTABLISHED | DERIVED | PROPOSED | EXPERIMENTAL | SPECULATIVE | REJECTED

A theory separately carries formal_status, empirical_status, application_status,
scope, freshness and provenance.
"Deduced using Theory X" does not imply "Theory X is empirically correct."

50.27 META-REASONING

Meta-reasoning decides how Garden should reason.

It asks:
  - Which reasoning modes apply?
  - Which theories are relevant/applicable?
  - How deep should reasoning go?
  - Which models/tools/solvers are appropriate?
  - Which modes can safely compose?
  - What would falsify the result?
  - What evidence is missing?
  - When should reasoning stop, defer or escalate?

Triggers include novelty, UNKNOWN/CONFLICTED outcomes, resource pressure,
mode disagreement, changed theory dependencies, and Safety/Constitution requirements.

Examples:
  Arithmetic             → Deduction.
  Mathematical theorem   → Deduction + Formal Proof + mathematical induction if suitable.
  Clinical causal query  → Causal + Bayesian + Evidence + Counterfactual + Adversarial.
  Engineering design     → Deduction + Modeling + Optimization + Prediction + Counterfactual + Adversarial verification.
  Unknown phenomenon     → Abduction + Empirical Induction + Theory Discovery + Experiment Design.
  Conflicting legal case → Deduction + Argumentation + Temporal + Ethical/Normative reasoning.

Output: ReasoningPlan + ReasoningTrace.

50.28 MODE COMPOSITION

Modes are composable but cannot silently amplify each other.

Example:
  Observation
    → Abduction
    → Candidate Hypothesis
    → Empirical Induction
    → Supported Pattern
    → Causal Analysis
    → Candidate SCM
    → Deduction
    → Conditional consequence.

Formal necessity conditional on uncertain premises ≠ unconditional certainty.
If valid modes materially disagree, status remains CONFLICTED or UNKNOWN until resolved.
No averaging merely to force a single answer.

Reason Composition Contract:
Theories/modes composed by Reasoning(50) MUST provide compatible
UncertaintyPropagationProfile semantics. If required profiles are absent,
incompatible or outside declared error bounds, composition status MUST be UNKNOWN
and MUST NOT be promoted as verified.

50.29 INFERENCE STATUS ≠ EPISTEMIC STATUS

InferenceStatus =
  DEDUCED | REFUTED | NOT_DEDUCED |
  INDUCED_SUPPORTED | INDUCED_WEAK | INDUCED_CONTRADICTED |
  ABDUCTED | ARGUED | INVALID | UNKNOWN

EpistemicStatus remains orthogonal, including:
  PROVEN | VERIFIED | SUPPORTED | UNVERIFIED | CONTRADICTED | RETRACTED | UNKNOWN/subtypes.

Example:
  InferenceStatus = DEDUCED
  EpistemicStatus = SUPPORTED
because the inference is formally valid but an empirical premise is only SUPPORTED.

50.30 UNIVERSAL INFERENCE RESULT

InferenceResult = {
    inference_id: Garden_ID,
    mode: ReasoningMode,
    status: InferenceStatus,
    problem_ref: ProblemID,
    conclusion: GSL_Expr,
    premises: List<ClaimRef>,
    assumptions: List<AssumptionRef>,
    evidence: List<EvidenceRef>,
    proof_ref: Option<ProofCertificateRef>,
    argument_graph: Option<ArgumentGraphRef>,
    confidence_or_uncertainty: Option<Represented<Uncertainty>>,
    epistemic_status: EpistemicStatus,
    validity_scope: ScopeRef,
    dependencies: List<DependencyRef>,
    retraction_conditions: List<Predicate>,
    falsification_conditions: List<Predicate>,
    design_epoch: DesignEpochRef,
    knowledge_snapshot: KnowledgeSnapshotRef,
    environment_commitment: EnvironmentCommitmentRef,
    resource_usage: ResourceUsage,
    provenance: Provenance
}

50.31 REASONING PIPELINE — UPA PROFILE

The current normalized process:
  FORMALIZE → RETRIEVE → FALSIFY → COMPOSE → CHECK → MANAGE → REASON
is retained as lineage but normalized as a Universal Process Algebra profile:

PROBLEM
  → FORMALIZE
  → CLASSIFY
  → RETRIEVE theories/evidence
  → SELECT reasoning modes
  → BUILD ReasoningPlan
  → EXECUTE modes
  → FALSIFY / CHALLENGE
  → COMPOSE compatible results
  → VERIFY formal obligations
  → PROPAGATE uncertainty
  → CHECK epistemic/scope/dependency status
  → TERMINATE / DEFER / ESCALATE
  → InferenceResult
  → Explain / Recommend / ResearchGap.

50.32 CONTINUOUS REVALIDATION / THEORY DEPENDENCY INTEGRATION

New evidence or a changed upstream theory triggers:
  Continuous Epistemic Revalidation
    → affected claims/theories discovered
    → TheoryDependencyGraph marks dependent theories/inferences REVALIDATION_REQUIRED
    → affected cached InferenceResults invalidated
    → applicable modes re-run
    → CONFIRMED / STRENGTHENED / WEAKENED / REFINED / CONTRADICTED / UNKNOWN.

Invalidation is impact-indexed, incremental and priority-bounded. A REFUTED upstream theory immediately
quarantines consequential affected outputs, but recomputation proceeds in batches/lazily from immutable
snapshots rather than synchronously traversing the entire graph or crashing active Runtime contexts.
Runtime may use only an unaffected last-valid binding or enter a declared conservative degradation.

Reasoning is therefore living, dependency-aware and DesignEpoch-bound.

50.33 EARTH CANONICAL BRANCH INTEGRATION

Local branch theory/proof/observation
  → BranchChangeManifest
  → Earth Frontier
  → Reasoning(50): comparison, deduction, induction, analogy, causal/counterfactual analysis,
                    adversarial challenge and discriminating-evidence identification
  → Research/Verity
  → MERGE | REMAIN_LOCAL | REJECT | UNRESOLVED.

Convergence/votes/popularity may nominate a candidate but cannot create truth.

50.34 INVARIANT VIEW — REASONING FAMILY

Canonical definitions: Part V.
Invariant references: RSN-001..010, RSN-011..014 RESERVED, RSN-015..021, INF-001..024.

50.35 DEPENDENCIES & MATHEMATICAL BINDING

Read:
  Language(6), Mathematics(7), Verity(8), Safety(9), Knowledge(10), Constitution(11),
  Time(20), Governance(30), Search(31), Evolution(33), Explanation(35), Dependency(46),
  Policy(48), Research(49), Economy(3), Agent(5).
Write:
  Events(45), Knowledge(10), and governed Research/Evolution requests.

Math families used include:
  M0 proof theory, formal logic, Hoare/type systems;
  M1 graph/order/algebra/category/refinement/concurrency;
  M3 probability/statistics/information/causal inference/decision/game theory;
  M4 optimization/complexity/solver selection.

Relevant proof obligations include P2, P3, P5, P6, P7a/P7b, P8, P12, P14, P18 and
other obligations induced by the actual reasoning plan and ObligationGraph.
