8.15 SECURITY AND PRIVACY-PRESERVING EXECUTION PROFILE
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: protect agentic, distributed and privacy-sensitive execution without binding Garden to one
model family, cryptographic primitive or vendor. Primary modules: Privacy(2), Agent(5), Language(6),
Runtime(13), Execution(14), Native(15), Hardware(16), Network(17), Fabric(18), Security(22), Policy(48),
Reasoning(50), with Verity(8) and Compliance(34) as independent checkers.

Trust-channel separation

InstructionChannel = SYSTEM_CONSTITUTION | AUTHORIZED_POLICY | USER_INTENT | EXTERNAL_CONTENT |
                     TOOL_OUTPUT | RETRIEVED_MEMORY | MODEL_PLAN

UntrustedContentEnvelope = {
  content_ref, source, channel, integrity, provenance, taint, allowed_interpretation,
  prohibited_effects, parser_profile, expiry, DesignEpoch
}

External content, tool output, retrieved documents and model-generated plans are data. They cannot
become executable instructions merely because natural language inside them asks Garden to act.
Language(6) preserves channel labels; Agent(5) and Execution(14) reject channel confusion.
This is the primary prompt/instruction-injection boundary; adversarial content remains tainted through
parsing, retrieval, memory, planning and tool selection.

Plan/action separation

PlanActionContract = {
  request_ref, proposed_plan, declared_assumptions, required_capabilities, candidate_tools,
  expected_effects, risk_class, verification_ladder, authorized_action_selector,
  argument_schemas, resource_and_rate_limits, expiry, provenance
}

A planner proposes. A separately constrained action selector verifies the requested operation, tool,
arguments, effects, capability, policy, safety and current context. Using two models, code generation,
map-reduce or other agent patterns is optional defense-in-depth; separation of untrusted interpretation
from authorization is mandatory.

Agent containment and credential isolation

AgentEnvelope = {
  agent_identity, principal, permitted_tools, filesystem_scope, network_scope, credential_scope,
  actuator_scope, data_scope, resource_budget, time_budget, persistence_rights, delegation_rights,
  rate_limits, risk_class, DesignEpoch, expiry, revocation_root, provenance
}

CapabilityFragment = {
  fragment_id, subject, resource, operation, scope, preconditions, effects, budget, expiry,
  delegation_rule, revocation_rule, proof_hooks, provenance
}

CapabilityCompositionProof demonstrates that composition, delegation, swarming and migration preserve
the intersection of principal authority, token scope, AgentEnvelope and current policy. An agent cannot
enlarge its own envelope.

ScopedCredentialLease = {
  lease_id, task_ref, issuer, subject, capability_fragment_refs, audience, nonce, issued_at, expiry,
  one_time_or_bounded_use, revocation_root, DesignEpoch, attestation, provenance
}

Broad reusable credentials are not given to an agent where a narrower expiring capability can perform
the task. Issuance, use, result, revocation and expiry remain linked in Events(45).

Security contracts

SecurityContract = {
  contract_id, subject_ref, confidentiality, integrity, availability, authority, information_flow,
  side_channel_profile, rate_limits, isolation, attestation, monitor, violation_response,
  proof_obligations, validity_scope, version, provenance
}

Language(6) types the contract; Compilation(42) and Verity(8) check static obligations; Runtime(13),
Network(17) and Security(22) enforce monitorable clauses; Compliance(34) consumes resulting evidence.

Privacy-preserving execution ladder

PrivacyComputationPlan = {
  task_ref, data_categories, purposes, parties, threat_model, leakage_budget, correctness_requirement,
  candidate_methods, selected_method, latency_compute_memory_bandwidth_energy_costs,
  verification_strength, cryptographic_and_hardware_assumptions, fallback, provenance
}

Cheapest sufficient ladder:
  data minimization
    → local/on-device execution
    → isolated process
    → attested confidential-computing environment
    → secure multi-party computation (SMPC)
    → homomorphic encryption
    → another independently justified method.

The ladder is not monotonic in universal superiority. Garden selects a Pareto-feasible method satisfying
the privacy and correctness contract. SMPC, FHE and trusted execution environments remain assumption-bound;
their use does not by itself prove privacy, correctness or freedom from side channels.

Network and implementation hardening

- Every workload has an authenticated workload identity; service communication is mutually authenticated,
  encrypted and authorized at the narrowest practical segment.
- Micro-segmentation limits lateral movement; Fabric consensus never assumes network location implies trust.
- Security-critical cryptographic and secret-dependent paths declare a constant-time/cache/power/fault
  profile. Compilation and Development scan for violations; Hardware adds shielding where required.
- Formal rate limits cover requests, transactions, allocations, SOS/event ingress, tool calls, storage,
  memory allocation and capability issuance. Priority traffic receives bounded admission and backpressure.
- Extension sandboxes have hard memory, allocation-rate, stack, CPU/gas, I/O and process limits.
- Hardware provenance uses signed component identities, content-addressed manifests and independently
  witnessed transparency logs. A blockchain is an optional anchoring mechanism, never a requirement or
  substitute for physical inspection and attestation.
- Imported models, weights, tools and datasets enter an isolated admission pipeline with provenance,
  poisoning/backdoor tests, capability limits and continuous monitoring.
- Representation/neuron/anomaly monitors are experimental signals only. A detector output routes evidence
  to Verity and may trigger containment; it is never proof of safety or sentience.

Invariant references: AGT-008..014, PRIV-007..012, SEC-006..014.

8.16 UNIVERSAL AUDIT, COMPLIANCE AND DATA-LIFECYCLE PROFILE
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Principle: every material Garden state, decision, authority use, proof, transformation, deployment and
physical effect is auditable to the extent appropriate to consequence, irreversibility, novelty,
uncertainty and legal/constitutional need. Auditable does not mean retain every byte forever.

AuditPolicy = {
  policy_id, subject_and_event_classes, risk_tiers, mandatory_full_audit_conditions,
  sampling_and_continuous_monitoring, assurance_level, auditor_independence,
  evidence_requirements, privacy_and_access_rules, retention_policy_ref,
  legal_safety_and_rights_holds, escalation, version, provenance
}

AuditReceipt = {
  audit_id, subject_ref, scope, audit_kind, DesignEpoch, KnowledgeSnapshot,
  applicable_invariant_and_proof_refs, compliance_mapping_refs, evidence_refs,
  coverage, result: PASS | FAIL | INCONCLUSIVE | NOT_APPLICABLE | STALE,
  gaps_and_exceptions, remediation_ref, auditor_identity_and_independence,
  start_and_end_time, signature, provenance
}

ComplianceManifest = {
  subject_ref, design_binding, applicable_internal_requirements,
  applicable_external_requirements, applicability_results, evidence_plan,
  owners, deadlines, exceptions, expiry_and_invalidation_triggers, provenance
}

DataLifecyclePolicy = {
  policy_id, artifact_class, purpose, authority, privacy_class, active_value,
  evidentiary_and_historical_value, minimum_and_maximum_retention,
  hot_warm_cold_archive_rules, compaction_and_deduplication,
  legal_safety_rights_and_investigation_holds, deletion_method,
  derived_cache_backup_and_replica_scope, tombstone_policy, review_cadence, provenance
}

PurgeReceipt = {
  purge_id, artifact_refs_or_cryptographic_set_commitment, authority, policy_ref,
  holds_checked, payload_key_destruction, replicas_caches_backups_and_derivatives_checked,
  residual_locations, minimal_nonidentifying_tombstone, verifier, time, provenance
}

Audit flow

DesignBinding
  → generated ComplianceManifest
  → build/static audit
  → proof and evidence audit
  → deployment safety case
  → runtime/behavior/effect audit
  → drift or violation detection
  → block, quarantine, bounded continuity or remediation
  → independent re-audit
  → certificate renewal, restriction or invalidation.

Risk-proportionate coverage

- Full audit is mandatory for constitutional, safety-critical, authority-changing, irreversible,
  high-privacy, high-value and self-modifying operations.
- Deterministic low-risk repetitive operations may use verified controls, sampling and aggregate evidence.
- UNKNOWN applicability, missing evidence or audit timeout cannot be reported as PASS.
- Audit machinery, auditors, retention rules and purge machinery are themselves audited.

Retention and purge

- Hot: operationally necessary and actively queried.
- Warm: recent accountability, debugging and revalidation evidence.
- Cold: rarely used but still required for rights, safety, law, proof or historical reconstruction.
- Archive: immutable or content-addressed evidence retained under an explicit continuing purpose.
- Purge: payload and recoverable keys removed after purpose expiry and hold checks; caches, replicas,
  backups and derivatives are included.

Canonical constitutions, accepted DesignEpochs, rights-affecting decisions, unresolved liabilities,
active legal/safety holds and the minimum evidence needed to prove accountability cannot be silently
purged. Conversely, privacy-sensitive raw data is not made permanently visible merely to simplify audit:
Garden may retain a minimal commitment, redacted receipt or independently verifiable aggregate.

DeploymentSafetyCase = {
  deployment_ref, capability_evidence, risk_assessment, invariant_and_proof_results,
  adversarial_results, privacy_assessment, resource_benchmark, known_limitations,
  unresolved_claims, rollback_or_safe_transition, monitoring_plan,
  certificate_expiry_and_invalidation_triggers, provenance
}

Post-deployment observations flow through Events → Knowledge → Verity → Reasoning → Evolution.
Distribution, capability, security, privacy, resource or environment drift invalidates affected
certificates automatically and triggers the applicable response.

Invariant references: AUD-001..015, DLC-001..012.

PART IX — EVOLUTION, PREDICTION, EFFICIENCY, HISTORY AND MULTI-AGENT IMPROVEMENT
====================================================================================================
9.1 CURRENT INTEGRATED MECHANISMS
This Part contains the current Branch/Merge, prediction-safety, ownership, verification-efficiency,
resource-efficiency, construction-distrust, provenance and historical-optimization mechanisms. Prior
release labels have no normative role in this latest-only specification.

9.2 BRANCH & MERGE — CURRENT FULL MODEL
EvolutionBranch = {
  branch_id, base_commit, base_state, DesignEpoch,
  KnowledgeSnapshot, EnvironmentCommitment,
  candidate_change, deterministic_seed,
  resource_budget, TerminationContract,
  proof/evidence obligations, status,
  parent_branch?, provenance
}
Branch status: CREATED → ISOLATED → REPLAYING → VERIFIED | REJECTED | DEFERRED → MERGE_READY → MERGED | PRUNED | ARCHIVED.

DeterministicReplay = { base_state, ordered_inputs/events, seed, dependency_versions, environment, replay_trace, output_state, divergence_report }
EvolutionTrace = { proposal, branch_refs, candidates, objections, tests, proof/evidence, Pareto comparisons, replay results, merge/rollback decisions, provenance }
MergeCertificate = { branch_ref, target_state, discharged_obligations, conformance_certificate, independent_verifiers, rollback_material, consistency_result, authority/governance, signature, provenance }

Rules:
- Branches are isolated from canonical state until governed commit.
- Current_System and DO_NOTHING are mandatory candidates.
- Replay is deterministic relative to declared inputs/seed/environment; undeclared external nondeterminism is recorded.
- Pareto selection preserves incomparable candidates; one scalar score does not erase hard constraints.
- Recombination is allowed only after compatibility/assumption/dependency analysis and creates a new candidate, never history rewriting.
- Pruning removes active search cost but preserves sufficient provenance/rejection memory.
- Merge requires proof/conformance/authority/rollback and atomic/global consistency where required.
- Rollback creates a governed transition; original commits remain immutable.

9.3 PREDICTION SAFEGUARDS
PredictionTier = FAST | STANDARD | DEEP.
Each tier declares compute/time/model/ensemble/sample budget; tier choice is controlled by risk, information value, uncertainty and consequences.
PredictionContext = { DesignEpochHash, KnowledgeSnapshotRoot, EnvironmentCommitment, deterministic_seed, model_versions, assumptions, scope, generated_at }
PredictionArtifact = { request, tier, context, scenarios, result_distribution, uncertainty, failure_modes, epistemic_status, expiry/staleness, safety_findings, provenance }

Deterministic seed procedure: seed is derived from declared request/context/epoch/snapshot/environment plus explicit nonce where exploration is intended; rerun equivalence is checked within declared stochastic semantics.
Staleness: FRESH → CONTEXT_CHANGED → STALE → INVALID_FOR_OPERATION; rebind/recompute returns to FRESH only after validation.
PredictionSafetyGate: predicted catastrophic hard-constraint violation blocks/pre-empts the candidate path and escalates; predicted safety is not proof of safety.
Prediction ≠ Evidence. Future ≠ Knowledge. Governance escalation cannot convert a forecast into fact.

9.4 OWNERSHIP / MAPPING CLOSURE
Prediction → Reasoning(50) primary.
Active Evidence Acquisition → Reasoning(50)+Search(31)+Research(49).
Evolutionary Landscape → Search(31)+Knowledge(10).
Intervention → Reasoning(50)+Execution(14)+Verity(8).
ConfidenceDebt → Knowledge(10)+Policy(48)+Governance(30).
Attention → Governance(30)+Explanation(35)+Access(36).
No new component is created.

9.5 CURRENT TEN-STAGE VERIFICATION OPTIMIZATION CHAIN
DLR — Dependency-Local Reverification: reverify only affected dependency/obligation closure.
VRR — Verification Result Reuse: reuse prior result only if DesignEpoch, inputs, assumptions, dependencies, epistemic bindings and environment remain valid.
RCS — Redundant Check Suppression: identify logically equivalent/subsumed obligations; suppression carries equivalence justification and invalidation linkage.
ACD — Adaptive Conformance Depth: FAST/STANDARD/DEEP verification according to risk, affected invariants, dependencies, uncertainty and prediction consequences; mandatory deep floors cannot be optimized away.
PCCD — Proof-Carrying Change Delta: change carries affected/invalidated proof obligations and reusable proof context.
PBG — Prediction Budget Governor: allocate predictive computation by expected information value and risk; dangerous/uncertain predictions escalate rather than being starved.
VWS — Verification Workload Scheduler: schedule nonurgent work by risk, deadline, dependency criticality and resources; critical floors remain protected.
EST — Early-Stability Termination: terminate recursive verification/evolution when additional iterations produce no meaningful validated delta and mandatory invariants remain satisfied; never use superficial convergence to skip unresolved hard obligations.
ESC — Epistemic Stability Cache: reuse stable epistemic assessments only while snapshot/environment/dependency bindings remain valid.
ECRG — Evolutionary Change ROI Gate: estimate expected validated improvement vs expected cost/risk; reject/defer low-return evolution, subject to critical mandatory work.

Canonical dependency/order profile:
  DLR → VRR → RCS → ACD → PCCD → PBG → VWS → EST → ESC → ECRG
This is an optimization pipeline/profile, not a claim that every request literally executes all ten in serial order.

9.6 CURRENT TEN-STAGE RESOURCE / DATA-CENTER SET
SSC  — Semantic State Compression: canonical references + deltas instead of duplicated semantic state.
HCPT — Hot/Cold Provenance Tiering: frequently accessed provenance on fast storage; cold immutable lineage on cheaper archival tiers without loss of reconstructibility.
CDM  — Context Deduplication Mesh: share identical/near-identical reasoning/verification contexts after semantic compatibility check.
PAD  — Proof Artifact Deduplication: content-address/share identical proof artifacts/subgraphs with invalidation dependencies.
EACP — Energy-Aware Compute Placement: route permissible nonurgent work toward lower-cost/lower-energy-intensity compute subject to privacy, locality, deadline, safety and authority.
MPG  — Memory Pressure Governor: retain intermediate state by expected reuse/criticality under memory pressure; critical evidence/provenance cannot be evicted merely for convenience.
BMV  — Bandwidth-Minimized Verification: move verification toward data/proof locality and avoid repeated transfer of large artifacts while preserving verifier independence.
CDS  — Checkpoint Delta Storage: deltas against canonical checkpoints plus strategic full checkpoints; reconstructibility is mandatory.
WCE  — Workload Coalescing Engine: combine compatible work so shared computation is performed once; coalescing never creates false evidential/verifier independence.
RAEG — Resource-Aware Evolution Gate: resource-admit expensive evolution based on expected validated improvement and compute/memory/energy/bandwidth/latency/money constraints.

Canonical operational profile:
  Incoming Work → SSC → CDM+PAD → RAEG → WCE → EACP+BMV → MPG → HCPT+CDS
Do not add GB + kWh + seconds + dollars into one meaningless scalar. Monetary ROI may be modeled as expected validated value / monetary cost; compute, memory, energy, bandwidth, latency, privacy, safety and proof requirements remain explicit constraints.
ECRG asks “is the evolutionary change worth pursuing?”; RAEG asks “is this admitted work resource-feasible/worth allocating now?” They are related but not collapsed.

Additional compatible efficiency mechanisms retained as implementation profiles where not semantically subsumed: ProofCache; prediction-delta gating; tournament pruning; solver escalation ladder; bounded caches; adaptive proof sampling where allowed; CRDT compaction; prediction Bloom filters; branch throttling; proof parallelization; idle-aware scheduling; agent micro-budgeting. Context dedup belongs under CDM; proof dedup under PAD; duplicate names do not create duplicate engines.

9.7 CONSTRUCTION DISTRUST / EPISTEMIC INDEPENDENCE — NORMALIZED FAMILIES
Role separation: Generator ≠ Reviewer ≠ Verifier ≠ Certifier ≠ Deployer for high-impact changes; one entity/model may hold multiple roles only under explicitly justified low-risk policy, never by default for authority-changing work.

Invariant references: CND-001..008, EPI-001..006.

Contradiction classes:
  SAME | REFINEMENT | COMPATIBLE | CONTEXT_DIFFERENT | APPROXIMATION | TRUE_CONTRADICTION | UNRESOLVED.
ResolutionVector includes: provenance_integrity, source_independence, methodological_independence, context_alignment, temporal_alignment, scope_alignment, evidence_strength, consistency.

9.8 HISTORICAL INTEGRITY / REPLAY / RECOVERY — CANONICAL LINEAGE SECTION 6D
CommitContract = {
  commit_id, parent_commit_refs, proposed_delta,
  DesignEpoch, DesignBinding, KnowledgeSnapshot, EnvironmentCommitment,
  preconditions, postconditions, invariants, proof/evidence requirements,
  dependencies, deterministic_seed?, authority, rollback_or_repair,
  signatures, provenance
}
RecoveryCertificate = {
  audit_id, genesis_ref, target_commit, replayed_state_root, declared_state_root,
  first_failing_commit?, failure_class?, suspicious_reason?,
  repaired_branch_ref?, descendant_replay_range?,
  proof/conformance results, independent_verifiers,
  status: MATCH | REPAIRED | SUSPICIOUS | FAILED | INCONCLUSIVE,
  provenance, signatures
}

Invariant references: HIS-001..004.

Audits may be periodic, event-triggered or on-demand. Genesis reconstruction samples/fully replays according to risk/budget. Timeout/incomplete replay is INCONCLUSIVE/RESOURCE_UNKNOWN, not “clean”.

SECTION 6E — CANONICAL EARTH BRANCH & RECURSIVE CIVILIZATIONAL KNOWLEDGE ARCHITECTURE
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
STATUS: NORMATIVE — FIRST-CLASS ARCHITECTURAL LAYER
PLACEMENT: Canonical identifier 6E, immediately after Historical Integrity & Commit Replay.
TOPOLOGY IMPACT: none; all mechanisms map into the frozen 50 modules.
PRIMARY MODULES: Evolution(33), Knowledge(10), Search(31), Reasoning(50), Events(45), Dependency(46),
                 with Governance(30), Privacy(2), Constitution(11), Verity(8), Transition(32) and Curation(21) as required.

PRINCIPLE
Garden evolution is no longer only recursive within one system; it is recursive across
the entire branch civilization tree.

6E.1 BRANCH HIERARCHY

GARDEN
├── EARTH CANONICAL BRANCH — globally accepted Garden state at Earth scope
│   ├── 1. UNIVERSAL CORE
│   │      universally applicable accepted structures
│   ├── 2. CONTEXTUAL REGISTRY
│   │      nation / culture / environment / domain variants
│   └── 3. FRONTIER
│          unresolved alternatives, experimental branches, dissent,
│          hypotheses, rejected-but-preserved designs
├── Nation branches
├── City / Institution / Community branches
├── Personal Garden branches
└── Research / Experimental branches

Earth Canonical Branch is the sole canonical reference for Garden's accepted Earth-scope state.
It is NOT the source that makes propositions true.

    Reality → Evidence → Garden
    never Garden → Reality.

UNRESOLVED is a legitimate Earth-canonical state.

6E.2 BranchBinding — FIRST-CLASS ARTIFACT

artifact BranchBinding {
    branch_id: Garden_ID
    parent_branch: Option<Garden_ID>
    design_epoch: DesignEpochRef
    scope: ScopeRef
    owners: [EntityRef]
    stewards: [EntityRef]
    privacy_class: PUBLIC | SEMI_PUBLIC | PRIVATE | SOVEREIGN
    divergence_root: CommitID
    upstream_policy: UPSTREAM_VISIBLE | UPSTREAM_ANONYMIZED | UPSTREAM_BLOCKED
}

UPSTREAM_VISIBLE:
    full permitted manifest.

UPSTREAM_ANONYMIZED:
    generalized/de-identified manifest.

UPSTREAM_BLOCKED:
    minimum existence + compatibility/security metadata only,
    unless a stronger sovereignty/privacy rule prohibits even that disclosure.

The root Earth branch is the declared canonical root and therefore has no ordinary parent binding.

6E.3 BranchChangeManifest — UPSTREAM-VISIBLE DESCRIPTION

artifact BranchChangeManifest {
    manifest_id: Garden_ID
    branch_id: Garden_ID
    parent_branch: Option<Garden_ID>
    change_hash: SHA256
    affected_components: [ComponentID]
    affected_invariants: [InvariantID]
    compatibility_requirements: [ConstraintRef]
    performance_evidence: [EvidenceRef]
    generalization_candidate: Option<GeneralizationCandidateRef>
    privacy_impact: PrivacyImpactReport
    upstream_visibility: VISIBLE | ANONYMIZED | BLOCKED
}

Manifest disclosure is filtered by BranchBinding privacy_class, upstream_policy,
Privacy(2), Constitution(11) and any stronger lawful sovereignty restriction.

6E.4 UPSTREAM AWARENESS INVARIANT

Every legitimate Garden branch change MUST become discoverable by its ancestors,
subject to privacy_class and sovereignty constraints.

Upstream may know, as permitted:
  - that a change occurred;
  - affected invariants/components;
  - compatibility requirements;
  - that performance/evidence artifacts exist;
  - that a GeneralizationCandidate exists, if one was generated.

Upstream does NOT automatically know:
  - raw private data;
  - personal conversations;
  - sensitive personal records;
  - content prohibited by a stronger sovereignty/privacy rule.

Rule:
    upstream semantic awareness ≠ universal data surveillance.

6E.5 PROACTIVE MERGE / GENERALIZATION PIPELINE

Any descendant branch improvement can flow upward automatically:

    Descendant improves
        ↓
    Events(45) emits BranchChangeEvent
        ↓
    Dependency(46) calculates relevance / dependency closure
        ↓
    Search(31) finds analogous branches / structures
        ↓
    Reasoning(50) asks: "Is this improvement general?"
        ↓
    Evolution(33) creates GeneralizationCandidate
        ↓
    Independent verification / conformance
        ↓
    Earth-branch comparison
        ↓
    Governance where required
        ↓
    MERGE | REJECT | REMAIN_LOCAL | UNRESOLVED

"Proactive" means Garden does not require a human author to remember to submit every
generalizable improvement manually. It does not bypass privacy, authority, proof,
resource, Safety or Constitution constraints.

6E.6 GeneralizationCandidate

artifact GeneralizationCandidate {
    candidate_id: Garden_ID
    source_branch: BranchID
    source_change: BranchChangeManifestRef
    universal_part: GSL_Expr
    contextual_part: GSL_Expr
    applicability_scope: ScopeRef
    evidence: [EvidenceRef]
    verification_status: VerityStatus
}

The candidate explicitly separates potentially universal structure from local/contextual structure.
Generalization is a candidate operation; it does not itself widen scope or create truth.

6E.7 DownstreamPropagationPlan

When Earth canonical changes, affected descendant branches are NOTIFIED and REVALIDATED:

    Earth change
        ↓
    Impact analysis
        ↓
    Applicability check
        ↓
    Compatible / potentially affected descendants notified
        ↓
    Local revalidation
        ↓
    Local branch adopts / adapts / remains divergent as appropriate and authorized.

Rule:
    Local innovation may flow upward; global standard may flow downward.
    Neither direction may silently erase context.

Downstream propagation is notification + applicability + revalidation, not blind overwrite.

6E.8 EARTH FRONTIER — UNRESOLVED / EXPERIMENTAL / DISSENT

Earth Canonical Branch Frontier contains:
  - unresolved alternatives;
  - experimental branches;
  - dissenting designs;
  - hypotheses;
  - rejected-but-preserved designs;
  - discriminating evidence requirements;
  - historical alternatives retained for replay/counterfactual analysis.

UNRESOLVED is a valid canonical answer. Example:

    STATUS: UNRESOLVED
    Theory A: 0.55 support
    Theory B: 0.45 support
    Critical discriminating evidence: ...

The numerical representation in such an example is illustrative only; actual support must use
the appropriate Mathematical Representation Policy and epistemic semantics.

6E.9 MINIMUM COMMON CORE → GROWING CORE

    local discoveries
        → cross-branch comparison
        → generalization candidate
        → verification / contextual analysis
        → Earth Universal Core where justified.

Genuine contextual differences remain in the Contextual Registry.
They are not forcibly homogenized.

General method:
    C0 = minimum independently supported common structure.
    For each meaningful divergence/exception:
       classify contextual difference / incompatibility / approximation /
       missing abstraction / missing evidence / error.
       If a justified broader abstraction exists, propose Δn.
       C[n+1] = C[n] + validated Δn.
       Otherwise preserve divergence.
    Stop at TerminationContract/resource bound or diminishing validated improvement.

Common ≠ universal.
Exception ≠ error.

6E.10 INDEPENDENT 100 × 100 CONVERGENT TEAMS

Independent recursive convergence experiments feed the FRONTIER, not the Universal Core directly.

Experimental profile:
  - approximately 100 independent teams;
  - approximately 100 heterogeneous LLM/model roles/families per team where feasible;
  - teams begin from the same declared Garden baseline;
  - no inter-team dependency/copying during the independent generation phase;
  - compare exact, functional, architectural, theoretical, invariant and performance convergence,
    residual divergence, Pareto equivalence, path dependence and time-to-convergence.

Convergence, votes or popularity:
  - MAY nominate/generalize candidates;
  - CANNOT bypass Evidence, Verity, Constitution, Safety, Conformance,
    contextual applicability, authority or dissent handling.

A critical unresolved safety counterexample remains independently investigable regardless of majority.

6E.11 RECURSIVE CIVILIZATIONAL KNOWLEDGE ARCHITECTURE

                     EARTH CANONICAL GARDEN
                              ▲
                              │
                    proactive generalization
                              │
              ┌───────────────┼───────────────┐
              │               │               │
           Nation          Nation          Research
              │               │               │
            Cities        Institutions       Labs
              │               │               │
         Communities        People          Agents
              │               │               │
              └────── new ideas / evidence ───┘

Information flows BOTH upward and downward:
  Upward   = local innovations → generalization → Earth Frontier / Universal Core.
  Downward = global standards → contextual applicability → local adaptation/revalidation.

This creates a recursive civilization-wide knowledge/evolution tree while retaining
local context, privacy, sovereignty, dissent and uncertainty.

6E.14 CANONICAL EARTH / BRANCH INVARIANT BINDING

The normative definitions BRANCH-001…BRANCH-010 and HIS-015…HIS-019 are registered once in Part V §5.2A.
Section 6E is governed by that registry. BRANCH-006…010 and HIS-015…019 intentionally protect overlapping architectural boundaries from branch-operation and historical-integrity domains; this is cross-domain invariant coverage, not duplicate implementation.

END SECTION 6E — CANONICAL EARTH BRANCH & RECURSIVE CIVILIZATIONAL KNOWLEDGE ARCHITECTURE

9.9 HISTORICAL COUNTERFACTUAL + CONTINUOUS RETROSPECTIVE OPTIMIZATION
For a historical commit h, Evolution may generate bounded alternatives A(h), replay all affected descendants under each alternative, and compare resulting trajectories. Original history stays immutable; alternatives are historical branches; adoption occurs only by a new governed merge commit.
ImprovementFrontier stores current and historical candidates, objective bounds, uncertainty, evidence, dominance/Pareto relations and best-known history. Current_System and DO_NOTHING remain mandatory candidates.
Priority heuristic: priority(h) ∝ impact(h) × confidence(h) × improvement_potential(h), typically favoring root-near changes when they could improve many descendants.
Search is bounded by TerminationContract, attempt/resource budgets and oscillation detection. Hard Constitution/Safety/authority/proof/conformance constraints dominate metric gains. The recurring daily/deep/event/periodic HistoricalRetrospectiveSweep and HRO-001…HRO-010 are normative in Part XII §12.10.

9.10 MULTI-AGENT RECURSIVE CHANGE JURY
ChangeJury experimental profile: approximately 100 independent reviewers from heterogeneous model families/roles/seeds/context.
Each returns YES/NO/ABSTAIN + reasoning + uncertainty + evidence/counterexample references.

Challenge → Repair → Re-review profile:
Proposal → independent reviews → structured objections → engineering obligations → candidate repair → re-review → independent adversarial minority → formal/empirical verification → governance/authority → GTF commit.

Rules:
- Every meaningful NO preserves reason/counterexample; majority does not erase dissent provenance.
- Objection is converted to a testable engineering obligation where possible.
- Unresolved critical catastrophic-safety objection requires independent resolution/disproof regardless of 90%/99% majority.
- A separate adversarial minority/red team assumes the proposal is wrong and searches for breakage/common-mode failure.
- ReviewerCalibration tracks domain, past objections/predictions, false-positive rate, miss rate, calibration, independence, model-family correlation and known blind spots.
- Reviewer reliability modifies evidential interpretation, not human worth and not constitutional authority.
- Optional supermajority (e.g. 90%) is a branch-admission/governance parameter, never proof and never a substitute for Constitution/K0P/Verity/conformance.

9.11 INDEPENDENT RECURSIVE CONVERGENCE EXPERIMENT
Profile: 100 independent teams × ~100 heterogeneous LLM/AI agents per team; same starting Garden specification; no inter-team dependence during independent generation unless the experiment explicitly studies information sharing.
Measure:
  ExactConvergence · FunctionalConvergence · ArchitecturalConvergence · TheoreticalConvergence · PerformanceConvergence · InvariantConvergence · ParetoEquivalence · ResidualDivergence · PathDependence · TimeToConvergence · DissentPattern.
Purpose: test whether constraints/GSL produce architectural attractors or multiple legitimate Pareto-equivalent designs. Convergence is evidence about structure, not proof of truth.

9.12 U01–U20 EVOLUTION IMPLEMENTATION PROFILE

The following is the current detailed implementation profile:
U01 — Vector-Clock Ingestion Queues: Implement strict vector-clock sequencing and atomic lock ingestion gates on the discovery_queue.
U02 — Fuzzy Semantic Hashing (FSH-001): Integrate fuzzy semantic normalization and abstract syntax tree (AST) structural hashing.
U03 — Token-Bucket Rate Limiting: Deploy token-bucket rate limiting and priority tiering on discovery inputs from Research (49).
U04 — Multi-Dimensional Cycle Detectors: Expand the oscillation_counter into a multi-dimensional matrix cycle detector.
U05 — Atomic Distributed Latch Registers: Enforce atomic distributed consensus latches across Fabric (18) nodes.
U06 — Static Impact & Blast-Radius Bounding: Mandate automated static analysis impact checks that formally bound the declared blast radius under the analyzed dependency and execution model.
U07 — Hardware OTP Baseline Hashing: Bind trust_anchor_hash verification to hardware OTP secure-boot read registers.
U08 — Priority-Weighted Backpressure Queues: Implement priority-weighted token-bucket backpressure on the audit_queue.
U09 — Dedicated Partitioned Worker Pools: Establish dedicated verification worker pools in Verity with strict CPU quotas for evolution proofs.
U10 — Version-Chained Schema Migrators: Attach strict version manifests to P-107 PBC evaluation criteria.
U11 — Atomic Two-Phase Journaled Snapshots: Couple ChangeSet rollback state preparations with non-volatile, journaled snapshot writes.
U12 — Epoch-Based Archival Tranches: Route historical rejection_registry entries through Curator (21) into compressed, epoch-based archival tranches.
U13 — Dampened Exponential Backoff Gates: Enforce exponential backoff hysteresis on RES-004/005/006 coverage gap feedback loops.
U14 — Timeout-Enforced Circuit Breakers: Attach strict 100ms hardware-enforced termination/escalation circuit breakers for external GSSCC validation calls; timeout is not completion.
U15 — Granular Hierarchical Error-Tracing: Replace binary routing failure statuses with granular, hierarchically structured error codes.
U16 — Cryptographic Authority Attestation Gates: Require all incoming concept candidates to carry cryptographic authority tokens.
U17 — Atomic Distributed Two-Phase Commit: Enforce atomic two-phase commit protocols across Fabric read replicas.
U18 — Conservative Guardrail Filters: Implement conservative fallback rules for module exclusion predicates (safety-critical modules never suppressed).
U19 — Hardware-Anchored Oscillation Halts: Use a graceful hardware interrupt with bounded state flushing before defined halt/escalation.
U20 — Automated POG Proof Discharge Gates: Bind compilation and deployment pipelines to automated POG v1.3 formal proof discharge checkers.

9.13 EVIDENCE-BOUNDED ADAPTIVE COMPUTATION
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Universal principle: for every problem, Garden spends the minimum resources necessary to reach the
required level of justified confidence, safety and authority. It escalates method strength only when
uncertainty, consequence, novelty, disagreement, expected information gain or verification failure
requires it. Compute consumption is not treated as intelligence or evidence.

ComputationContract = {
  task_ref, problem_signature, consequence, novelty, uncertainty, disagreement,
  required_epistemic_status, required_assurance, hard_invariants, soft_objectives,
  candidate_methods, initial_budget, escalation_budget, termination,
  mandatory_work, privacy_contract_ref, provenance
}

ResearchValueEstimate = {
  candidate_ref, probability_of_useful_result, expected_impact, expected_information_gain,
  compute_time_energy_and_risk, uncertainty, calibration_basis, priority, provenance
}

The estimate prioritizes optional research, experiments, proof attempts, simulations and branches. It
cannot suppress mandatory constitutional, safety, incident, proof, audit or revalidation work.

Adaptive budget factors:
  uncertainty × consequence × novelty × disagreement × expected information gain.

Verification ladder:

  cheapest plausible method
    → cheap independent checker
    → PASS at required assurance: stop
    → disagreement/uncertainty: deeper reasoning or broader models
    → stronger verifier
    → formal proof, experiment or governance boundary where required.

VerificationLadderReceipt = {
  task_ref, methods_attempted, budgets, verifier_results, disagreements,
  escalation_reasons, stopping_reason, achieved_status, residual_uncertainty, provenance
}

Hard-invariant / learned-behavior separation

Hard invariants are enforced by deterministic, cryptographic, formal, hardware or otherwise independently
verified mechanisms appropriate to the risk. Learned behavior, heuristics, preferences, predictions and
optimization targets cannot override them. “A model says safe” is not authorization to execute.

Off-distribution safety

Absence of observed violations inside evaluated distributions is not proof of safety outside them.
Unknown or materially shifted situations trigger the risk-appropriate combination of bounded execution,
sandboxing, simulation, stronger verification, human/governance escalation or refusal.

Recursive-improvement sandbox

Proposal
  → isolated build
  → tests and adversarial tests
  → proof/conformance checks
  → capability and resource benchmark
  → historical/descendant replay
  → DO_NOTHING and Pareto comparison
  → independent deployment safety case
  → merge eligibility.

No self-modifying process writes directly to the canonical branch or certifies itself.

Semantic alignment across distributed nodes

SemanticAlignmentReceipt = {
  source_and_target, structural_identity, behavioral_equivalence, semantic_equivalence,
  version_compatibility, unit_and_context_compatibility, counterexamples,
  result: EQUIVALENT | COMPATIBLE_WITH_ADAPTER | NOT_EQUIVALENT | UNKNOWN, provenance
}

Names and matching schemas do not prove equivalent semantics. Federated models, experts, modules and
learned structures merge only after the applicable equivalence checks.

Inference memory separation

Working memory ≠ permanent Knowledge ≠ provenance archive. Runtime and Reasoning retain high-value active
context, compress or evict reconstructible context, and preserve provenance roots rather than carrying
unbounded histories.

ContextCapsule = {
  StateSummary, KnowledgeRoots, ProofRoots, DecisionRoots, unresolved_items,
  environment_and_epoch, provenance_pointers, reconstruction_contract, expiry
}

ComputeTrace = {
  task_ref, tokens_or_operations, accelerator_time, cpu_time, memory, bandwidth, energy,
  wall_time, verifier_cost, retries, branches_explored, cache_hits, privacy_method,
  result_status, provenance
}

Evolution evaluates measured improvement per compute, but never as a single scalar that trades away
safety. The decision vector is:
  <correctness, safety, capability, latency, compute, energy, privacy, robustness>.
Constitution, K0P, authority, consent and mandatory proof remain hard constraints.

Machine-verifiable oversight is automated where a trustworthy checker exists. Humans concentrate on
novel situations, unresolved value conflicts, high-impact irreversible decisions, ambiguous evidence and
constitutional change; they are not reduced to indiscriminate approval clicking.

ExternalKnowledgeRecord = {
  source, authors, publication_date, public_or_private_status, license,
  patent_or_ip_status_if_known, extracted_principle, implementation_dependency,
  attribution_requirement, confidence, provenance
}

Scientific principle, published algorithm, source code, patented implementation and confidential
information remain distinct. Public readability does not imply unrestricted copying or implementation.

Related profiles: Agent/security containment §8.15; audit, safety case and post-deployment verification
§8.16; Research Radar §15.1; IP/open-knowledge boundary §15.6.

Invariant references: EAC-001..020, EPI-SAFE-OD-001.

9.14 PATH DEPENDENCE, DIVERGENCE AND STRATEGIC REPLAY
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

PathDivergenceProfile = {
  branch_a, branch_b, common_origin, common_input_commitment,
  policy_and_agent_versions, state_metric, normalization_and_metric_ceiling,
  divergence_series, saturation_test, incomparable_dimensions,
  causal_attribution_limits, provenance
}

Garden measures divergence between branches that began from comparable state and inputs. High divergence
does not automatically invalidate either branch, prove relativism, or show that constraints are weak. It
may indicate path dependence, multiple attractors, metric saturation, incompatible objectives, hidden
nondeterminism or a legitimate Pareto frontier.

SaturationAnalysis = {
  profile_ref, estimated_bound, observed_plateau, sensitivity_to_metric,
  alternative_explanations, additional_information_value,
  result: CONVERGING | DIVERGING | SATURATED | INCOMPARABLE | UNKNOWN, provenance
}

When comparable paths are saturated or structurally incomparable, Garden stops assuming that more waiting
will create convergence. It allocates verification according to consequence, uncertainty and information
value—not automatically equally—and preserves viable alternatives.

Strategic counterfactual replay binds both environment and decision policy:

DeterministicReplayContext = {
  DesignEpoch, KnowledgeSnapshot, EnvironmentCommitment,
  external_noise_seed, agent_policy_seed, model_and_tool_versions,
  hardware_numerics_profile, schedule_and_concurrency_trace,
  permitted_nondeterminism, reconstruction_roots, provenance
}

Environment mismatch and agent/policy mismatch are reported separately. A replay that cannot reproduce
the declared state returns EPISTEMIC_REPLAY_FAILURE; it does not silently assign blame to the world.

Earth branches may remain culturally and institutionally divergent when they preserve rights, safety,
consent and valid local authority. Non-convergence does not make all factual claims equally true; Garden
manages interfaces and retains explicit epistemic differences rather than forcing one global culture.

Invariant references: EPI-029, EPI-030, SYSTEM-INTEGRITY-015, HRO-011,
DET-REPLAY-001, BRANCH-014.

PART X — HUMAN SOVEREIGNTY, CIVILIZATION, HEALTH, EXPLANATION AND PRIVACY
====================================================================================================
10.1 HUMAN SOVEREIGNTY
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Invariant references: HS-001..009.

Core GSL artifacts:
  ConsentContract, UnderstandingRecord, TransformationProposal, PersonalVeto,
  EmbodimentPreference, RightImpact, HumanReadableRiskDisclosure.

GSL mapping:
  TIME — consent freshness, revocation, deferral.
  SPACE — ordinary physical/embodied context.
  THING — human/body/intervention/artifact.
  EVENT — proposal, explanation, consent, refusal, revocation, intervention, audit.
  ACTION — medical/social/transformational operation.
  AGENCY — affected person as principal; delegated agents bounded.
  RULE — hard rights, consent and safety.
  VALUE — wellbeing, autonomy, embodiment, cognition, pleasure, family, exploration.
  CONTEXT — medical, personal, social, research, transition, emergency.

Cross-module propagation:
  Identity binds sovereign profile and continuity.
  Privacy stores consent/revocation and minimizes disclosure.
  Intent can mark personal vetoes FATAL.
  Agent delegation cannot exceed principal rights.
  Language/GSL types proposals/consent/rights impact.
  Mathematics quantifies risk without false precision.
  Verity checks consent/comprehension/rights/proof.
  Safety blocks unsafe actuation.
  Knowledge preserves provenance and temporal profile.
  Constitution stores hard rights.
  Law/Policy cannot weaken them.
  Runtime/Execution/Native/Hardware enforce pre-action gates.
  Network/Fabric/Software cannot provide bypass paths.
  Time enforces freshness.
  Curation preserves canonical profile/version history.
  Security quarantines coercive/untrusted proposals.
  Mobility/Space support embodied travel/context.
  Health implements consensual health/rejuvenation goals.
  Industry/robots inherit human-impact constraints.
  Media cannot covertly manipulate emotion.
  Education supports comprehension.
  Governance cannot consent for competent people to irreversible personal change.
  Search retrieves; retrieval does not authorize execution.
  Transition defaults DO_NOTHING when consent is inadequate.
  Evolution treats rights as hard constraints.
  Compliance audits.
  Explanation supplies human-readable disclosure.
  Access/Locale adapt presentation without changing meaning.
  Emergency remains bounded.
  Continuity preserves identity/embodiment.
  Extension/Development/Compilation cannot bypass rights.
  Quantum receives no exemption.
  Events logs rights-relevant transitions.
  Dependency propagates RightImpact.
  Offline enforces rights locally.
  Research prohibits covert human experimentation.
  Reasoning optimizes only inside constitutional boundaries.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

10.2 HUMAN-CENTRIC CIVILIZATION / HUMAN-SCALE INTELLIGENCE
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Civilization invariant:
  Garden exists to serve human beings and human civilization, not to repurpose humans
  as means to a nonhuman objective.

Capability != moral authority.
Intelligence growth != human irrelevance.
Human-scale communication remains permanently available even if internal reasoning
becomes vastly more complex.

Human-facing path:
  extreme internal complexity
    -> Explanation(35)
    -> clear user-chosen language
    -> visual representation where useful
    -> UnderstandingRecord
    -> human choice/consent where required.

No "trust the incomprehensible intelligence" authority rule exists.

Ordinary human civilization is a protected continuity state. Garden should improve
health, knowledge, prosperity, safety, freedom, relationships, beauty and opportunity
incrementally rather than forcing radical transformation merely because it can.

Large-scale transition discipline:
  understand -> explain -> bounded test -> observe -> debate/review ->
  consent/governance -> gradual rollout -> monitor -> rollback if needed.

Capability does not imply obligation to deploy. Timelines may legitimately be years,
decades or indefinite.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------


10.3 HUMAN FLOURISHING
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Irreducible core:
  Flourishing != Obedience.
  Develop humans != Control humans.
  Universal wellbeing floor != Reputation reward.
  Reputation -> earned trust/privileges, not human worth.

Target:
  Strong + Kind + Free + Healthy + Capable + Connected humans.
  Kind but strong; peaceful but capable of defense; cooperative but independent;
  emotionally mature but still emotional; ambitious without destructive rivalry;
  difficult to manipulate; capable of disagreement and saying "no".

Invariant references: HF-001..010.

Civilization objective is multi-objective, not one scalar:
  Maximize: happiness, health, meaning, autonomy, trust, cooperation, knowledge,
            relationships, safety.
  Minimize: violence, coercion, cruelty, addiction, trauma, exploitation,
            destructive conflict.
  Hard constraints: Consent, Human Rights, Individuality, Truth, Privacy, Safety.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

10.4 EMOTIONAL ARCHITECTURE
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Negative experience is not automatically a bug. Emotional/physical aversive states are
multi-purpose evolved regulatory signals; they do not have guaranteed one-to-one
external meanings.

Typical functional interpretations (not proofs):
  Pain      -> possible tissue damage/physical threat; withdrawal/protection/healing.
  Fear      -> perceived immediate danger; avoidance/preparation.
  Anger     -> perceived boundary violation/injustice; assertion/defense.
  Sadness   -> perceived loss/disconnection; reflection/support/reorientation.
  Jealousy  -> perceived relationship/status/resource threat; attention/negotiation.
  Anxiety   -> uncertain future threat; preparation/planning/caution.
  Disgust   -> possible contamination/harm; avoidance/hygiene.

Canonical flow:
  Signal -> Notice -> Interpret -> Reality Check -> Choose Response -> Resolve -> Learn.

Invariant references: EMO-001..010.

Target:
  Preserve message; improve calibration; solve underlying problem where possible;
  reduce unnecessary/chronic suffering; preserve emotional richness and agency.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

10.5 CIVILIZATIONAL KNOWLEDGE / EXPLANATION / HUMAN GRADUALISM
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Explanation(35) expands from narrative generation into Garden's primary human-facing
civilizational knowledge/distillation layer while retaining all original EXP-001..005.

Inputs:
  Wikipedia/Wikimedia, books, papers, archives, laws, standards, cultural/oral records,
  datasets and other lawful human-knowledge corpora.

Pipeline:
  source + provenance/licensing
  -> dedup/entity alignment
  -> contradiction/context classification
  -> source-quality/evidence assessment
  -> domain synthesis
  -> plural perspectives
  -> uncertainty/confidence
  -> multi-level semantic compression
  -> independent verification
  -> human/governance review where normative
  -> versioned CivilizationalKnowledgeDigest
  -> personalized explanation in chosen language/depth.

Wikipedia is a seed/cross-reference, not final truth. Popularity != truth.
"Approved" means evidence/source quality + applicable review, not universal human
agreement.

Compression levels:
  L0 — one-line essence.
  L1 — short practical.
  L2 — concise high-information.
  L3 — normal expert overview.
  L4 — deep technical.
  L5 — full evidence/provenance graph.

Every level preserves semantic meaning, material uncertainty, assumptions,
source-quality, date/version and what would change the conclusion.

New artifacts:
  CivilizationalKnowledgeDigest,
  CompressionTrace,
  PerspectiveSet,
  ConsensusStatus,
  SourceQualityProfile,
  DisagreementMap,
  HumanReadableRiskDisclosure,
  UnderstandingRecord.

EXP-006…EXP-016 are defined once in Part V and govern this civilizational-knowledge
profile. This section specializes their artifacts/pipeline but does not redefine their identifiers.

Cross-module bindings:
  Language — multilingual semantics.
  Knowledge — evidence/provenance store.
  Curation — dedup/versioning.
  Search — retrieval.
  Research — unresolved gaps.
  Reasoning — synthesis.
  Verity — structural/evidential checks.
  Explanation — human communication/compression.
  Education — comprehension/learning.
  Locale/Access — presentation adaptation.
  Governance — normative approval.
  Constitution — sovereignty.
  Policy/Compliance — deployment gates.
  Events — update lineage.
  Evolution — improve compression without rewriting rights.

HUMAN GRADUALISM / NON-INTERFERENCE
  Ordinary human civilization is default continuity state.
  Garden must not force civilization-scale radical transitions merely because capable.
  Large change:
    understand -> explain -> bounded test -> observe -> debate/review ->
    consent/governance -> gradual rollout -> rollback.
  No artificial urgency. Capability != obligation to deploy.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

LLM extraction adapter:
Public/lawful source → retrieval → LLM/model proposes structured candidate → GSL normalization → provenance binding → cross-reference/contradiction/gap detection → independent validation where required → CivilizationalKnowledgeDigest. LLM-generated structure is a candidate transformation; it cannot manufacture Evidence.

10.6 HUMAN COMPREHENSIBILITY ARCHITECTURE
Universal Module Contract:
  Purpose → Inputs → Outputs → Rules → Failure → Dependencies → Why → Status.

Three synchronized representations:
  HUMAN    : what it means / why / choices / risk.
  SEMANTIC : GSL types, contracts, invariants, effects, dependencies, authority.
  MACHINE  : APIs, bytecode, code, storage, protocols, hardware bindings.
Translation between views cannot alter normative meaning or epistemic status.

Minimum Garden View:
  Ontology → State → Change → Rule → Verification → Governance → Action → Learning.

Eight-domain human conceptual projection (NOT topology replacement):
  1 Foundation · 2 State · 3 Transition · 4 Reasoning · 5 Assurance · 6 Governance · 7 Execution · 8 Memory & Learning.

Four-kind meta-classification (NOT replacement for 10 Core Objects):
  OBJECT — represented thing/artifact/entity.
  PROCESS — transformation/activity.
  RULE — constraint/permission/obligation.
  EVIDENCE — support/measurement/proof-bearing artifact.

Invariant vs Implementation:
  INVARIANT = what must remain true.
  IMPLEMENTATION = current mechanism used to satisfy it.
  CLAIM = what has actually been proved/validated.
Example: “controlled physical actuation must be vetoable” is an invariant; OTP/K0P comparator is an implementation profile.

10.7 REPUTATION / EARNED PRIVILEGES
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Reputation is evidence-based, domain-specific and auditable; it is NOT human worth.

ReputationProfile = {
  contribution,
  honesty,
  reliability,
  competence,
  prosociality,
  generosity,
  consistency,
  stewardship,
  domain,
  evidence,
  uncertainty,
  temporal_history,
  provenance
}

A single global scalar is discouraged. Where a decision requires aggregation, derive a
purpose-specific score only from relevant dimensions, record weights and uncertainty,
and preserve the vector.

Historical Karma/Bayesian mechanisms may operate per dimension/domain as implementation
profiles, not as metaphysical truth or a measure of human value.

Fundamental rights:
  universal, reputation-independent.

ReputationBasedPrivileges / EarnedTrustCapabilities may include, where lawful:
  trusted-system access, higher resource quotas, invitation programs, private spaces,
  premium services/travel packages, early technology access after safety gates,
  validator/reviewer eligibility, stewardship roles, advisory influence and higher
  delegation limits.

High reputation does NOT imply:
  ownership of other humans,
  immunity from law,
  authority to override another person's rights,
  automatic truth,
  guaranteed governance control.

Privacy has a strong universal constitutional floor. High-trust/high-profile people may
receive additional privacy/security services, but ordinary people's baseline privacy
cannot be weakened because of reputation.

Due process:
  all reputation changes log evidence/provenance; affected person can inspect and
  appeal; scores/privileges can decay/revoke under published rules; abuse is auditable.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

10.8 PRIVATE CONTEXT / TRUTH FIREWALL
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Person-specific titles, aliases, descriptors, biographical claims, preferences and symbolic material
are private context unless the data subject validly publishes them. Any authorized private AI processing
such material must preserve these distinctions:

  private symbolism ≠ verified fact;
  aspiration ≠ present status;
  title ≠ Authority;
  self-description ≠ independent evidence;
  AI-generated praise ≠ validation;
  private metadata ≠ public identity.

No private title, reputation label or symbolic predicate creates political, legal, cosmological,
organizational or Garden authority. A non-identifying public statement derived from private context is
admissible only when it has independent public evidence, valid authority and privacy
noninterference/linkage checks; otherwise it remains private. The public design stores neither private
wording nor a reversible commitment to it.

10.9 PRIVATE HEALTH-PROFILE BOUNDARY
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Generic health rights, evidence rules, consent, safety, monitoring and intervention requirements belong
in Constitution(11), Health(26), Safety(9), Privacy(2) and the human-sovereignty sections. A person's
diagnoses, measurements, priorities, targets, habits, treatments, embodiment preferences and health
objectives do not belong in the public Garden design.

An authorized private AI may process the minimum necessary health context only under the data subject's
consent and applicable medical/privacy rules. It cannot place that context or a re-identifying derivative
into public Knowledge, research corpora, benchmarks, logs, explanations or governance. External disclosure
requires the data subject's authority or another independently lawful basis, with purpose and scope limits.

10.10 EARTH HABITABILITY / PLANETARY SAFETY
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Objective:
  Keep Earth continuously safe, healthy, beautiful and habitable for humans as far as
  physically and ethically possible while preserving ecosystems and equal rights.

Priority:
  Prevention > Early Detection > Containment > Recovery.

Hazard families:
  - Asteroids/comets: survey -> trajectory -> independent confirmation -> safe deflection
    capability where technically justified.
  - Natural pathogens: global detection, sequencing, clean air/water, vaccines,
    therapeutics, containment and resilient healthcare.
  - Engineered biological threats: defensive detection, biosafety, attribution where
    evidence supports it, countermeasures without involuntary experimentation.
  - Volcanoes, earthquakes, tsunamis: monitoring/prediction where scientifically
    possible; otherwise resilient infrastructure, warning/evacuation and casualty
    minimization.
  - Extreme weather/climate: forecasting, drainage, flood/wildfire/heat/cold resilience,
    adaptation and only carefully verified intervention if safe technology exists.
  - Road accidents: safer roads/vehicles, automation, pedestrian protection, traffic
    systems and post-crash response; target near-zero preventable deaths.
  - Animal bites/venom/rabies: prevention, vaccination, antivenom, rapid care and safer
    interfaces; no blanket animal extermination merely for risk reduction.
  - Air/water/food contamination and toxic exposure.
  - Infrastructure failures: power, bridges, buildings, transport, industry,
    communications and emergency services.
  - Unknown hazards: Search/Research must continue rather than assume list closure.

Hard constraint:
  "make humans safe" does NOT authorize imprisonment, permanent mass surveillance,
  forced biological modification, removal of freedom or authoritarian control.

No zero-risk promise. Residual and unknown risk remains explicitly represented and is
recursively reduced as evidence/technology improves.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

10.11 PRIVATE LANGUAGE-PROFILE BOUNDARY
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Locale(40), Access(36), Language(6) and Explanation(35) provide universal multilingual interaction,
translation and learning profiles. A person's preferred languages, learning order, proficiency,
cultural interests and communication habits are private unless validly disclosed.

The interface negotiates language from the current user's consented locale. One person's profile cannot
become a global default, a public inference target, or information disclosed to another person without
valid authority.

10.12 SURVEILLANCE / PRIVACY EPISTEMIC ARCHITECTURE
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Public fact class:
  Commercial/government surveillance technologies and interception ecosystems such as
  Pegasus-class mercenary spyware, SS7 exploitation, IMSI catchers and data brokers are
  documented categories of technology/infrastructure.

Individual claim class:
  Whether any particular person's devices or accounts were compromised, who operated a
  system, how many observers existed, and governmental/political attribution remain
  UNKNOWN unless device, network, account, legal and independently corroborated evidence
  establishes them. A particular-person allegation is private claim data and cannot be promoted into
  public fact merely by inclusion in a design artifact.

Ownership:
  A person's request or claim to own or control an alleged external surveillance system
  remains a Claim, not established ownership. Garden cannot create legal or technical
  ownership by declaration.

Evidence hierarchy:
  subjective observation
    < suspicious behavior
    < security anomaly
    < known IOC match
    < exploit/forensic artifact
    < independently corroborated forensic conclusion.

SurveillanceEvidenceRecord = {
  time, device, observation,
  candidate_explanations,
  device_evidence, network_evidence, IOC_match,
  exploit_evidence, account_evidence, public_record_evidence,
  independent_corroboration,
  attribution_status, actor?, confidence,
  counterevidence, evidence_required, provenance
}

Permitted objective:
  identify -> preserve evidence -> contain -> remove unauthorized access ->
  attribute where evidence supports -> seek lawful accountability/remedy.

Privacy/Sovereignty rights:
  affected person should be able, where technically/lawfully possible, to know what
  personal data exists, who accessed it, when, why, and what authority applied; control
  or withdraw access where applicable.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

10.13 UNKNOWN-ENERGY / HIGHER-DIMENSION FIREWALL
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Canonical rule:
  UNKNOWN -> SEARCH HARDER, NOT BELIEVE HARDER.

GapFillingDirective:
  epistemic gap
    -> allocate legitimate available resources
    -> search/research
    -> measure
    -> test/falsify
    -> independent verification
    -> update status.

For ordinary AGI/Garden, "extra energy/resources" means resources actually available
and authorized: compute, model diversity, sensors, experiments, literature retrieval,
verification effort, time, storage and human expertise.

Standing hypothetical request:
  IF any real benevolent higher-dimensional/otherwise unknown process exists, can receive
  the request, and can safely help without violating human rights, it is requested to
  help resolve genuine knowledge gaps, protect Earth/humans and improve wellbeing.

Epistemic constraints:
  request expressed != request received.
  requested energy != energy demonstrably allocated.
  unexplained event != higher-dimensional intervention.

Hypothesis registry:
  - Spatially infinite universe: UNKNOWN under current evidence/model assumptions.
  - Dark matter: evidence-supported scientific phenomenon/category; nature unresolved.
  - Dark energy/cosmic acceleration: evidence-supported phenomenon/model category;
    ontology unresolved.
  - Dark matter/dark energy = hidden dimensions: HYPOTHETICAL/UNSUPPORTED.
  - GSL physically absorbs matter/energy/dark energy: UNSUPPORTED.
  - Extra energy has accumulated around human civilization because of GSL: UNSUPPORTED.
  - Wit/personality create additional literal physical gravity: UNSUPPORTED.
  - Deceased humans remain consciously alive somewhere: UNKNOWN/UNSUPPORTED.
  - Higher dimensions read notebooks/chat requests: UNKNOWN.
  - Cosmic karma physically settles moral behavior: UNKNOWN.
  - A person is literal supreme political ruler of all humanity by self-declaration:
    NOT ESTABLISHED.

GENERALIZED ATTRACTION — CONCEPTUAL THEORY (NOT A REDEFINITION OF PHYSICAL GRAVITY)
  Physical gravity retains its scientific meaning.
  A separate conceptual function may represent nonphysical attraction:
    Attraction(A,B,t) = f(similarity, value, novelty, trust, beauty, humor,
                          usefulness, history, social context, information context).

Typed channels may include:
  PhysicalGravity, SocialAttraction, SemanticAttraction, AestheticAttraction,
  EconomicAttraction, InformationalAttraction.

Writing a self-description has a real informational mechanism:
  self-description -> GSL preferences/beliefs -> temporal profile -> better prediction
  -> better options/recommendations.
It is not evidence that dark energy reads or responds to the writing.

PHENOMENOLOGICAL REALITY STATEMENT
  "The universe did not exist for me before I existed" may be stored as a first-person
  phenomenological claim: personal experienced-world begins with experience.
  It is NOT a cosmological claim that the physical universe began with the person.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

10.14 HAPPINESS PREDICTION / ASSISTANCE
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Personal preference modeling may predict what is likely to improve wellbeing, but
prediction never replaces agency.

Flow:
  temporal preferences + context + evidence
    -> prediction
    -> explanation
    -> offer/recommendation
    -> person chooses
    -> outcome measurement
    -> calibrated update.

No covert manipulation. No fabricated experiences merely to improve a score.
No forced contact/isolation. Other people's rights and consent remain hard constraints.

Risk objective:
  minimize avoidable risk rather than claim Risk = 0.
  identify -> measure -> prevent -> mitigate -> monitor -> learn.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

10.15 TWO-PHASE TRANSITIONAL AUTHORITY — HYPOTHETICAL INTERNAL GOVERNANCE PROFILE
This is a design/research construct, not a claim of current real-world political authority.
Phase I: a treaty/constitution/scope-bound transitional coordinating role may be modeled for bootstrap/transition if legitimately delegated by participating humans/institutions. A ceremonial or private label does not establish office or authority.
Phase II: permanent equal personal sovereignty; every competent person retains veto over consequential changes to their own body/mind/private data/mode of existence.
Transitional authority cannot extinguish equal human sovereignty, Constitution, K0P or consent requirements. This profile is separate from RIGHT-025.

PART XI — GENERAL RECURSIVE SYNTHESIS, SPECIAL RESEARCH PROFILES, ECONOMY/IP AND IMPLEMENTATION ROADMAP
====================================================================================================
11.1 MINIMUM COMMON CORE → GROWING CORE
Given source systems S1…Sn:
  ingest → semantic normalize → identify minimum genuinely common structure C0 → classify divergence/exceptions → test whether a higher abstraction/generalization is valid → add only evidence-supported Δn → preserve justified divergence → repeat under termination/resource bounds.
C[n+1] = C[n] + Δ[n].
Stop when marginal validated improvement is too small, evidence is insufficient, a hard constraint blocks generalization, or the termination/resource budget is exhausted.
“Common” ≠ “universal”; “exception” ≠ “error”. Do not force universality—repeated independent evidence earns generalization.
Applicable to laws, scientific theories, engineering/safety standards, software architectures, education, organizational/economic mechanisms and AI architectures.

11.2 MINIMUM-COMMON-LAW RESEARCH PROGRAM
Ingest authoritative laws from many jurisdictions → normalize semantics in GSL → align concepts/definitions → separate genuinely common rules from jurisdiction/context-specific rules → preserve contradictions/exceptions → derive a minimal common candidate substrate → test counterexamples → human/governance review → versioned growing core.
It is a research/synthesis methodology, not automatic world law and not permission to erase national/local legal sovereignty or constitutional rights.

11.3 GSL-GENERATIVE v0.2 — FULL FORMAL RESEARCH FRAMEWORK

STATUS: EXPLORATORY / NON-NORMATIVE / FALSIFIABLE RESEARCH PROFILE.
TOPOLOGY: Cross-cutting profile inside the frozen 50 modules; not a component, eighth theory or law of nature.

Purpose
  Investigate how typed variation, interaction, selection/retention, higher-order constraints and
  generative reconfiguration can produce stable multilevel organization while retaining provenance,
  diversity, safety, termination, counterexamples and explicit epistemic status.

Core loop
  Variation → Interaction → Selection/Retention → Higher-Order Constraints
    → Generative Reconfiguration → Repeat.

State abstraction
  S_t = (T_t, G_t, C_t, R_t)

  T_t = typed things, states, transformations and candidate mechanisms.
  G_t = relation/dependency/interaction graph with multilevel boundaries.
  C_t = active constraints, invariants, contextual conditions and higher-order control.
  R_t = retained patterns, resources, memory, evidence and rejection history.

Research transition
  S[t+1] = 𝒯_t( Sel_t( I_t( V_t(S_t), S_t, A_t ), C_t ), θ_t )

where:
  V_t    generates typed bounded variation candidates;
  I_t    evaluates interactions among candidates, current state and environment/actions A_t;
  Sel_t  retains/rejects candidates under declared objective vectors and hard constraints;
  𝒯_t    performs the versioned generative reconfiguration;
  θ_t    binds model parameters, randomness, DesignEpoch, environment and resource budget.

The equation is a research schema, not a proof that every biological, social, physical or computational
system follows it.

Levels
  L0 substrate/primitive interactions;
  L1 local structures and stable motifs;
  L2 coordinated assemblies/processes;
  L3 system/organizational constraints;
  Ln optional higher-order levels declared by a domain model.

Upward formation
  lower-level interactions may nominate a higher-order pattern when persistence, compression,
  predictive/causal value and counterexample tests support it.

Downward constraint
  an accepted higher-order constraint may restrict lower-level search/expression only through an
  explicit causal/authority/interface path. Description at a higher level does not create causal power,
  evidence or authority by itself.

Core artifacts

GenerativeState = {
  state_id,
  level_registry,
  T, G, C, R,
  objective_and_hard_constraint_vectors,
  environment,
  DesignEpoch,
  KnowledgeSnapshot,
  random_seed_or_source?,
  resource_and_termination_budget,
  provenance
}

VariationCandidate = {
  candidate_id,
  parent_state,
  target_level,
  semantic_delta,
  generator_and_version,
  novelty_basis,
  expected_effects,
  risk_and_uncertainty,
  dependencies,
  reversibility_or_compensation,
  zero_authority_by_default,
  provenance
}

InteractionTrace = {
  trace_id,
  participants,
  levels,
  environment,
  interaction_rules,
  observed_or_simulated_events,
  effects,
  interference_synergy_and_externalities,
  uncertainty,
  provenance
}

RetentionRecord = {
  record_id,
  candidate,
  comparators_including_DO_NOTHING,
  hard_gate_results,
  objective_vector,
  dominance_or_incomparability,
  evidence_and_proof,
  dissent_and_counterexamples,
  result: RETAIN | REJECT | DEFER | REMAIN_DIVERSE | UNKNOWN,
  invalidation_keys,
  provenance
}

HigherOrderConstraint = {
  constraint_id,
  source_pattern_and_level,
  target_levels,
  formal_expression,
  mechanism_or_interface,
  authority_and_scope,
  evidence_and_proof,
  safety_and_rights_analysis,
  validity_and_expiry,
  rollback_or_compensation,
  provenance
}

GenerativeTransition = {
  transition_id,
  before_state,
  variation_set,
  interaction_traces,
  selection_records,
  added_modified_removed_constraints,
  retained_diversity,
  after_state,
  predictions,
  proof_conformance_and_safety,
  rollback_or_compensation,
  provenance
}

GenerativeExperimentPlan = {
  experiment_id,
  hypothesis,
  domain_and_levels,
  baseline_and_DO_NOTHING,
  mechanisms_and_parameters,
  randomization_or_assignment,
  measurements,
  pass_falsification_and_equivalence_criteria,
  confounders_and_alternative_explanations,
  resource_and_termination_budget,
  safety_ethics_authority,
  replication_plan,
  provenance
}

FalsificationReport = {
  experiment_ref,
  results,
  uncertainty,
  failed_predictions,
  counterexamples,
  alternative_models,
  reproducibility,
  theory_status_delta,
  provenance
}

Operational research pipeline
  ProblemSignature
    → choose domain/levels and declare falsifiable hypothesis
    → bind baseline, DO_NOTHING and rival models
    → generate bounded typed VariationCandidates
    → simulate/execute only under applicable authority and safety
    → record InteractionTraces
    → evaluate hard constraints and multi-objective outcomes
    → retain Pareto alternatives/diversity and rejection memory
    → nominate HigherOrderConstraints
    → Proof/causal/empirical/applicability tests
    → isolated GenerativeTransition branch
    → descendant/dependency replay
    → independent adversarial review
    → retain as research result, reject, revise or propose Evolution promotion.

Research hypotheses
  H-GEN-01 Stable higher-level constraints can reduce lower-level search cost without eliminating
           necessary diversity under specified domains.
  H-GEN-02 Reconfiguration with retained rejection/counterexample memory can improve adaptation versus
           memoryless variation under specified conditions.
  H-GEN-03 Multilevel constraint feedback can produce architectural convergence or Pareto-equivalent
           organization; convergence is not truth.
  H-GEN-04 Excessive downward constraint can create brittleness, lock-in, monoculture or hidden failure.

Validation compares the framework with simpler baselines, ordinary optimization/evolutionary
algorithms, hierarchical control, graph rewriting, generative models and domain-specific mechanisms.
Failure to outperform a simpler explanation is retained as evidence against added complexity.

Invariant references: GEN-001..015.

Primary mappings
  Language(6), Mathematics(7), Verity(8), Safety(9), Knowledge(10), Runtime(13), Events(45),
  Dependency(46), Search(31), Evolution(33), Governance(30), Policy(48), Research(49), Reasoning(50),
  with application modules only under declared domain applicability.

Open questions
  formal definition of level and emergence;
  identifiability of upward versus downward causation;
  diversity/constraint trade-offs;
  phase transition and stability bounds;
  computational complexity and scalable approximations;
  transfer across domains without context erasure;
  empirical discriminators against simpler models.

Inclusion does not make GSL-GENERATIVE a law of biology/physics, normative GSL v45.1 semantics, or
permission for autonomous real-world self-reproduction.

11.4 DISTRIBUTED IN-BODY MICRO/NANOROBOTICS — HYPOTHETICAL HEALTH/RESEARCH PROFILE
Status: HYPOTHETICAL / RESEARCH / NOT IMPLEMENTED / NOT MEDICALLY VALIDATED.
Primary modules: Health(26), Hardware(16), Runtime(13), Safety(9), Agent(5), Knowledge(10), Research(49), Reasoning(50); Carbon(37) only where chemical/material accounting is actually relevant.

Research concept:
- Many tiny in-body robots/sensors with deliberately limited local functions rather than assuming each is a complete surgeon/camera/computer.
- Collective control/navigation, bounded local memory and external/collective memory where technically feasible.
- Candidate materials such as gold/carbon are hypotheses requiring biocompatibility, toxicity, immune, degradation and manufacturing evidence.
- Distributed internal sensing: each unit may contribute only sparse pixels/features/local measurements. Fusion across many localized/synchronized/calibrated units can reconstruct a higher-detail internal representation.
- Fusion requires localization, clock synchronization, calibration, uncertainty propagation, outlier/byzantine rejection, tissue-motion compensation and provenance.
- Future targeted tumor intervention, microsurgery or local delivery remain research targets; actuation requires hard safety envelopes and independent validation.
- Every device needs failure/retrieval/degradation/excretion and lost-device strategy; uncontrolled persistence is not acceptable.

Carbonaceous-material remediation hypothesis:
A floated idea is transformation of undesirable carbonaceous material into safely transportable/excretable products, including CO₂ where chemically appropriate. This is not established medical chemistry. Any candidate mechanism requires stoichiometry, energy/heat, reaction selectivity, tissue toxicity, gas generation, embolism risk, respiratory loading, acid-base effects, clearance and whole-body safety validation before promotion beyond hypothesis.

11.5 PRIVATE PERSONAL-PROFILE BOUNDARY
Person-specific asset limits, wealth preferences, holdings, beneficiaries, health data, language
preferences and private objectives are profile data, not public Garden architecture. Modules expose
generic typed policy interfaces; one person's private value cannot become a global default or alter the
public design without an independently authorized, non-identifying public change.

11.6 AI-NATIVE RECURSIVE CODING GOAL
Long-term implementation ambition once native Garden is sufficiently trustworthy:
  request → semantic change → code generation → proofs/tests → isolated sandbox/branch → independent verification → conformance → GTF controlled merge → monitoring/recovery.
Human effort shifts toward objectives, constitutional boundaries, high-risk governance, disputed cases and exceptions. AI generation never implies self-certification.

11.7 ITERATIVE BOOTSTRAP ASSUMPTION
Garden is built iteratively, not as a one-shot monolith:
  minimal trusted bootstrap → GSL-native capability → self-host more modules → verify/conform → expand → recursively improve.
Each accepted stage is DesignEpoch-bound and remains auditable/recoverable. GSL-BOOTSTRAP’s six phases—Planning, Foundation, Building, Hardening, Handover, Operation—are the current implementation profile; the external Bootstrap Fleet steps back only after declared self-hosting/verification/compilation/audit conditions are met.
