================================================================================
15. RESEARCH BOUNDARY
================================================================================

Research(49) may study consciousness, ego, emotion, self-modeling, deceptive
alignment, and power-seeking through literature, formal models, interpretability,
non-sentient test systems, external observations, and capability-minimized sandboxes.

Research may not intentionally create a plausibly conscious, suffering, ego-driven,
autonomously self-preserving, or unrestricted self-replicating system merely to test
HSA. A research label does not waive Constitution or K0P.

If a research system unexpectedly crosses an indicator threshold, HSA-003,
HSA-015, HSA-017, containment, and disposition apply immediately.

================================================================================

PART XIV — SIMPLE PRODUCTIVE FINANCE / PRODUCTIVE CAPITAL EXCHANGE (SPF-PCE v1.1)
====================================================================================================

1. CANONICAL DESIGN STATEMENT
================================================================================

Garden investigates replacing the continuously traded stock-market casino and
opaque financial-instrument chains with:

  1. transparent, auction-issued productive credit;
  2. genuine loss-bearing participation certificates;
  3. a bounded secondary Productive Capital Exchange;
  4. verified hedges tied to actual productive exposures; and
  5. explicit identification of every payer, beneficiary, underlying obligation,
     risk bearer, authority, priority, and default path.

Some capital must absorb business losses. A system containing only promised or
"guaranteed" returns cannot survive unprofitable periods without default, hidden
loss transfer, inflation, external subsidy, or bailout.

The target is therefore not abolition of all risk capital. It is the separation
of productive financing from latency races, synthetic duplication, concealed
leverage, manipulation, and resale-price gambling.

================================================================================
2. SEMANTIC DISTINCTIONS
================================================================================

FIXED CREDIT
  Holder receives contractual interest and principal according to declared terms.
  Issuer bears ordinary business variation first; creditor bears default/recovery
  risk when the issuer cannot satisfy the obligation.

FLOATING CREDIT
  Holder receives an independent public benchmark plus a fixed contractual spread.
  The issuer cannot change the spread unilaterally.

PROFIT / REVENUE / CASH-FLOW PARTICIPATION
  Holder receives a precisely defined fraction of an audited participation base.
  Return may be zero. Principal is at risk unless a separate, explicitly funded
  guarantee exists.

OWNERSHIP / GOVERNANCE
  Economic participation, legal ownership, voting authority, credit, insurance,
  and speculation are distinct. A financial claim does not silently acquire
  governance authority (K-INT-018: Authority Non-Accretion).

PRIMARY ISSUANCE
  Creates a new registered obligation or participation interest and transfers new
  capital to the issuer.

SECONDARY TRANSFER
  Transfers an existing claim between holders. It ordinarily supplies no new money
  to the issuer. Primary issuance != secondary transfer.

PRICE CHANGE
  A secondary price may change because of benchmark rates, inflation, duration,
  issuer creditworthiness, expected recovery, information, or liquidity. Such a
  change is risk valuation, not automatically gambling. Market price remains a
  Claim/Signal, not proof of fundamental value or solvency.

================================================================================
3. TYPED SUPPORTING VALUES
================================================================================

These are artifact fields/value types, not new Core Objects or primitives.

artifact MoneyAmount {
  amount: Decimal
  currency: CurrencyCode
}

artifact RateSpec {
  rate_type: FIXED | BENCHMARK_PLUS_SPREAD
  annual_rate_or_spread_bps: Decimal

  day_count_rule: DayCountRule
}

Binary floating-point values must not represent money or contractual rates.

================================================================================

ledger; it is not silently overwritten inside the original certificate.

The exact calculation base, deductions, accounting policy, audit method, reporting
period, related-party rules, and dispute process must be stated.

artifact VerifiedRealExposureHedge {
  hedge_id: Garden_ID
  parties: List<AgencyRef>

  provenance: Provenance
}

A hedge is permitted only to the extent and duration of a verified productive
exposure. Any excess or detached position is a prohibited speculative claim.

================================================================================
5. PRIMARY ISSUANCE AUCTIONS
================================================================================

5.1 Credit auction

  1. Issuer declares the required capital, use, maturity, maximum acceptable
     clearing rate, collateral, priority, covenants, and default path.
  2. Eligible bidders submit capital amounts and minimum acceptable yields.
  3. Sealed bids are ranked from lowest yield upward.
  4. Bids are accepted until the authorized capital requirement is filled.
  5. Tied marginal bids are allocated by a declared pro-rata rule.
  6. Accepted bidders receive the uniform clearing yield: the highest accepted
     yield, subject to the issuer's maximum and solvency gates.
  7. Strong demand tends to lower the clearing yield; weak demand tends to raise it.
  8. The auction is rejected or reduced when clearing terms exceed verified debt-
     service capacity, leverage limits, authorization, or safety constraints.

Indicative equilibrium decomposition:

  r* = benchmark/inflation expectation
       + default-risk premium
       + duration premium
       + liquidity premium
       + uncertainty/model-risk allowance

Demand discovers a price under stated information. It does not prove ability to
repay and must not erase UNKNOWN risk.

5.2 Participation auction

Participation instruments do not promise a fixed yield. Their auction clears one
explicit variable—capital price, participation fraction, or total return cap—while
all other terms remain fixed and comparable. A credit-yield auction must not be
misrepresented as a profit-participation auction.

artifact IssuanceAuctionReceipt {
  auction_id: Garden_ID
  instrument_type: CREDIT | PARTICIPATION
  issuer: AgencyRef
  capital_requested: MoneyAmount
  bid_commitment_root: Provenance
  allocation_rule: RuleRef
  accepted_and_rejected_bid_refs: List<ClaimRef>
  clearing_terms: RuleRef
  concentration_result: AssessmentRef
  solvency_and_safety_result: AssessmentRef
  DesignEpoch: Hash  KnowledgeSnapshot: Hash
  provenance: Provenance
}

================================================================================
6. PRODUCTIVE CAPITAL EXCHANGE — BOUNDED SECONDARY MARKET
================================================================================

A secondary market is retained because forced holding until maturity creates a
large liquidity premium, traps household savings, and can prevent useful portfolio
rebalancing. It must not recreate continuous latency-based speculation.

Permitted transfer mechanisms:

  - periodic uniform-price batch auctions;
  - cadence selected by instrument class and verified liquidity need: daily,
    weekly, monthly, or quarterly—not continuous nanosecond matching;
  - company redemption windows;
  - inheritance and legally authorized private transfers;
  - an explicitly funded, regulated emergency-liquidity facility; and
  - transparent restructuring or recovery transfers after default.

The emergency facility does not guarantee face value unless a separately funded
and authorized guarantee says so.

For fixed cash flows, indicative valuation is:

          T             C_t             F
  P =   SUM     ---------------- + -------------
        t=1          (1+y)^t          (1+y)^T

where P is market price, C_t contractual payments, F principal due at maturity,
T remaining periods, and y the market yield incorporating declared risks.

A fixed-rate certificate may therefore trade below or above face value. This does
not modify the issuer's original fixed obligation. New issuance rates and secondary
market prices are distinct.

artifact TradeReceipt {
  trade_id: Garden_ID
  instrument_ref: ThingRef
  seller: AgencyRef
  buyer: AgencyRef
  auction_window: TimeWindow
  face_value: MoneyAmount
  clean_price: MoneyAmount
  accrued_payment: MoneyAmount
  total_settlement_amount: MoneyAmount
  displayed_yield_to_maturity: Option<Decimal>
  benchmark_and_spread: Option<RateSpec>
  duration_measure: Option<Decimal>
  default_probability_claim: Option<ClaimRef>
  recovery_estimate_claim: Option<ClaimRef>
  liquidity_assessment: AssessmentRef
  uncertainty: Uncertainty
  fees_and_taxes: List<MoneyAmount>
  funding_attestation: ProofTraceRef
  settlement_receipt: EffectReceiptRef
  provenance: Provenance
}

All estimates retain model, evidence, scope, uncertainty, version, and provenance.
Displayed yield is not a promise of realized return.

================================================================================
7. PERMITTED AND PROHIBITED PRACTICES
================================================================================

PERMITTED

  - fixed productive credit;
  - benchmark-plus-spread productive credit;
  - genuine loss-bearing participation;
  - verified real-exposure hedging;
  - primary uniform-price auctions;
  - bounded secondary batch auctions;
  - company redemption and disclosed refinancing;
  - inheritance, recovery, and authorized private transfer; and
  - diversified saving through transparent, non-leveraged pools whose underlying
    instruments remain individually traceable.

PROHIBITED BY DEFAULT

  - continuous high-frequency / latency-arbitrage trading;
  - exchange-provided margin or borrowing to purchase financial claims;
  - naked short selling;
  - naked credit-default or default-outcome bets;
  - synthetic replication detached from registered underlying obligations;
  - recursive securitization that conceals or duplicates risk;
  - rehypothecation or multiple use of the same collateral;
  - undisclosed off-balance-sheet leverage;
  - wash trading, spoofing, front-running, collusive bidding, and self-dealing;
  - instruments lacking machine-readable terms and a one-page human explanation;
  - misleading sale of high-risk claims as guaranteed income; and
  - instruments whose ownership, payer, loss bearer, underlying exposure,
    priority, or default path is UNKNOWN.

Refinancing is not automatically prohibited. New issuance may repay an earlier
obligation only when the refinancing purpose is explicit, the complete liability
chain remains visible, solvency and leverage gates pass, and returns are not being
fabricated through circular issuance.

================================================================================
8. DEFAULT, LOSS, GUARANTEE, AND AUTHORITY
================================================================================

Every instrument declares before purchase:

  - who bears ordinary variation and tail loss;
  - collateral and whether it is already encumbered;
  - priority under the applicable legal context;
  - cure, restructuring, recovery, and insolvency rules;
  - voting and consent requirements for material modification;
  - whether any guarantee exists and its payer, reserve, scope, limit, and expiry;
  - the difference between modeled recovery and legally enforceable recovery; and
  - conflicts between contractual terms and jurisdictional law.

No bailout, deposit protection, principal protection, liquidity promise, or state
guarantee is implied. Every guarantee is itself a registered liability and requires
Authority, Resource, Rule, Context, provenance, and a funded loss path.

Economic ownership must not grant arbitrary control over workers, customers,
citizens, personal data, bodies, minds, or constitutional rights. Voting rights are
explicit, bounded, contextual, and subordinate to Constitution, Law, consent, and
RIGHT-027.

================================================================================

================================================================================

Canonical definitions: Part V.
Invariant references: FIN-001..020.

These bindings are stored as TheoryModuleBinding records. Module-level views are
generated; they must not be duplicated manually.

================================================================================
11. RESEARCH HYPOTHESES AND FALSIFICATION
================================================================================

SPF-HYP-001  A greater fraction of net financing reaches identifiable productive
             activity rather than latency competition and synthetic trading.

SPF-HYP-002  Hidden leverage, collateral duplication, and contagion depth decline.

SPF-HYP-003  Long-horizon household returns become more explainable and less exposed
             to manipulation, without unacceptable loss of liquidity.

SPF-HYP-004  Firms retain adequate access to debt and genuine loss-bearing capital,
             including start-ups and firms with intangible assets.

SPF-HYP-005  Tail-loss frequency/severity and bailout dependence decline after
             controlling for macroeconomic conditions.

SPF-HYP-006  Capital ownership and returns become less concentrated without reducing
             innovation, entry, productivity, or beneficial risk-taking.

The exploratory numerical targets—productive investment +30%, financial
sector share <=3%, Gini reduction >=15 points, and crash probability <=1% per
decade—are preserved only as UNCALIBRATED_RESEARCH_TARGETS. They are not canonical
predictions, requirements, or evidence. Research(49) must establish jurisdictional
baselines, definitions, causal identification, confidence intervals, trade-offs,
and feasible thresholds before any target can be promoted.

Required comparisons include:

  - current-market baseline;
  - debt-only counterfactual;
  - non-traded participation counterfactual;
  - PCE cadence and liquidity variants;
  - permitted-hedge boundary variants;
  - transition and regulatory-arbitrage scenarios;
  - adversarial manipulation and shadow-market migration; and
  - distributional effects by income, firm size, sector, region, and generation.

The candidate is weakened, revised, paused, or rejected when controlled evidence
shows persistent material deterioration in productive investment, access to risk
capital, household liquidity/welfare, innovation, systemic tail risk, fairness, or
enforceability, without a compensating improvement accepted through Governance.

A raw rise in bankruptcy or volatility alone is not sufficient falsification;
measurement must distinguish healthy failure/price discovery from destructive
contagion, manipulation, hidden leverage, or loss socialization.

UNKNOWN, CONFLICTED, NOT_APPLICABLE, DO_NOTHING, PARTIAL_ADOPTION, and ROLLBACK are
valid outcomes.

================================================================================
12. EVIDENCE-GATED TRANSITION
================================================================================

Phase 1 — RESEARCH (planning prior: 2–5 years)
  Agent-based simulation, historical back-testing, legal analysis, adversarial
  modeling, prototype auctions, shadow-market analysis, and alternative designs.

Phase 2 — BOUNDED PILOT (planning prior: 5–10 years)
  Voluntary, reversible pilots in selected sectors/regions. Existing lawful claims
  remain valid. No pilot may silently impair property, contract, pension, savings,
  consent, or due-process rights.

Phase 3 — CONDITIONAL EXPANSION (planning prior: 10–20 years)
  Expansion only after independent evidence, proof obligations, affected-party
  review, legal authorization, safety gates, and demonstrated capital access.

Phase 4 — POSSIBLE COMPLETION (planning prior: 20–30 years)
  New issuance migrates only if prior phases demonstrate dominance over alternatives.
  Lawful legacy instruments are redeemed, converted with valid consent, or allowed
  to mature; they are not confiscated or retroactively nullified.

Durations are planning priors, not automatic deadlines. Evidence gates control
advancement. Failure permits pause, redesign, partial adoption, or rollback.

15.1 RTR v1.0 — RESEARCH-AND-THEORY RADAR

STATUS: CURRENT CONTINUOUS RESEARCH PROCESS. Discovery is not validation and RTR is not an eighth theory.

Purpose
  Continuously discover established/common-use and frontier research across lawful scientific sources;
  preserve provenance, licensing, retraction and uncertainty; evaluate applicability; generate bounded
  UpgradeHypotheses; and route them through Research(49), Reasoning(50), Verity(8), Compliance(34),
  Evolution(33) and the Theory-to-Reality flow.

Source classes include OpenAlex, OpenAIRE, Crossref, CORE, arXiv, Europe PMC, NASA ADS,
ClinicalTrials.gov, OpenCitations, retraction/correction registries, standards bodies, scholarly
societies, patent databases, and domain-specific primary repositories. A source list is versioned and
non-exhaustive. Technical evaluation prefers primary papers, official documentation, standards and
reproducible artifacts over secondary summaries.

ExternalTheoryArtifact = {
  artifact_id, title, authors_or_originators, source_and_persistent_id,
  publication_or_preprint_status, dates, version, domain, abstracted_claims,
  equations_or_formal_objects?, assumptions, boundary_conditions, variables_and_units,
  evidence, proof_and_reproducibility, citations_and_independence,
  corrections_retractions_and_controversies, license_and_copyright_metadata,
  patent_or_restricted_implementation_risk, Garden_summary_in_original_words,
  provenance, status
}

CoverageReceipt = {
  query_and_scope, source_universe, time_window, languages, inclusion_and_exclusion,
  retrieved_and_screened_counts, inaccessible_or_unknown_sources, deduplication,
  coverage_limitations, DesignEpoch, KnowledgeSnapshot, provenance
}

UpgradeHypothesis = {
  hypothesis_id, external_artifact_refs, observed_problem_or_opportunity,
  affected_modules_and_theories, proposed_delta, claimed_benefit,
  assumptions, applicability, evidence_required, proof_obligations,
  safety_rights_authority_and_privacy_effects, resource_cost,
  alternatives_including_DO_NOTHING, falsifiers, IP_clearance_state,
  implementation_and_validation_plan, status, provenance
}

Invariant references: RTR-001..010.

Submitted external-theory discovery queue
  The following labels are preserved only as
  SOURCE_REQUIRED discovery leads: FLOW/emergent spacetime; GUP effective metrics; gravity duality of
  networks; string-theoretic mathematics for neural wiring; Unified Theory of Meaning (UToM);
  IIT–Free-Energy-Principle bridges; simplicial-complex growth; SG-7 information/network geometry;
  epiplexity/time-bounded information; Entropic Learnability Horizon; managed misalignment; and kinetic
  theories of socio-economic structure. Their names, publication claims, dates, novelty, equations,
  evidence, licenses and patent status are not established by their appearance in the discussion.
  RTR must resolve each label to a primary-source ExternalTheoryArtifact and evaluate it independently.
  None is a Garden theory, Core Object, primitive, validated domain model or approved upgrade merely by
  being listed. Until source resolution, its status is UNKNOWN/UNVERIFIED; a failed resolution is retained
  as NOT_FOUND or AMBIGUOUS rather than silently mapped to a similarly named work.

15.2 ZKEPP v1.0 — ZERO-KNOWLEDGE EPISTEMIC PROOF PIPELINE

STATUS: BOUNDED CRYPTOGRAPHIC IMPLEMENTATION PROFILE; security proof and implementation validation pending.

Purpose
  Permit a proposer to demonstrate that a declared derivation was executed over committed, authorized
  inputs without disclosing protected inputs or the full intermediate trace to every recipient.

  limitations, timestamp, signature, provenance
}

Pipeline
  Local protected Observation/Evidence
    → preserve raw attested source under TMR-002
    → commit to inputs and current revocation/snapshot roots
    → execute declared typed GSL transformation/circuit
    → generate ZK proof
    → Fabric/GAIA Diamond asynchronous verification
    → ZKEpistemicProofReceipt
    → Knowledge stores CLAIM with DERIVATION_VERIFIED at most
    → independent evidential/empirical evaluation continues
    → Titanium retains local K0P veto.

Invariant references: ZKEPP-001..008.

15.3 POU v0.1 — PRODUCTIVE-UTILITY ACCOUNTING AND FUNDING RESEARCH PROFILE

STATUS: DOMAIN-THEORY / ECONOMIC-DESIGN CANDIDATE. Not an established monetary theorem or automatic minting rule.

PoU may measure realized physical or computational output and route verified public revenue, savings,
tax receipts, contractual receivables or authorized productive returns into a treasury. It cannot make
heterogeneous output equal to money by declaration and cannot guarantee bounded inflation.

ProductiveOutputReceipt = {

  measurement_interval, sensor_and_PLC_measurement_refs,

  valuation_context, provenance
}

UtilityReceivable = {

  impairment_and_default, liquidity_class, restrictions, provenance
}

Architecture
  Industry(27) PLC/sensors
    → read-only hardware-attested ProductiveOutputReceipt
    → one-way/data-diode measurement gateway
    → Fabric(18) decoupled multi-source verification and deduplication
    → Verity/Compliance/Knowledge assessment
    → UtilityReceivable
    → Economy(3) treasury policy, valuation caps and staged realization
    → liquid reserve only after legally/economically realized funding.

Invariant references: POU-001..008.

The profile may define repeatable urban blocks combining residences, commerce, mobility, utilities,
green space, food production, emergency access, maintenance and local energy/water systems. A nominal
100m × 100m block is a candidate pilot size, never a globally hardcoded optimum.

15.5 ACTIVE-MATTER, ATFT AND EXTERNAL PHYSICS BOUNDARY

Active-matter mathematics may become a DOMAIN_THEORY binding for systems whose agents inject energy,
self-propel or locally organize and whose equations/parameters are validated. It does not upgrade TFT
into a quantum-to-city universal physical law. “Active transport” in governance or information systems
is an abstract model unless an independently validated mapping establishes physical meaning.

Invariant references: ATFT-001..004.

The proposed “Infinite Electron Theorem” has no authoritative statement, proof, provenance or independent
validation in the current evidence registry. It is NOT_REGISTERED as a usable theory. It may enter RTR as
an ExternalTheoryArtifact only after source identification; until then its status is UNKNOWN/UNSUPPORTED.

“Holographic state reconstruction” is also non-normative unless formalized. Current recovery uses
content-addressed Merkle/causal graphs, erasure coding, authenticated summaries and explicit data-
availability limits; partial data cannot reconstruct information not encoded with sufficient redundancy.

15.6 INTELLECTUAL-PROPERTY AND OPEN-KNOWLEDGE BOUNDARY

Garden may learn from facts, abstract ideas, mathematical principles and lawful public research, but
must not copy protected expression, code, data, figures or proprietary implementation details without
permission. Public accessibility does not equal public domain. Every implementation records license,
copyright, attribution, data-use and patent-clearance state.

Specific patent portfolios, “GESTALT V6.2,” Standard Essential Patent claims, gainshare enforcement and
royalty capture are not automatically incorporated merely because named in an audit. Law(12), Economy(3)
and Compliance(34) may maintain an IP registry and perform bounded prior-art/freedom-to-operate analysis.
Legal conclusions require qualified jurisdiction-specific review. Garden-generated eligible IP follows
the Common Fund policy, subject to valid law and contributor agreements.

Invariant references: IP-001..004.
